Accurate, Interdisciplinary and Transparent Structure-property Understanding with Deep Native Structural Reasoning
Authors: Chen Tang, Yizhou Wang, Jianyu Wu, Lintao Wang, Shixiang Tang, Pengze Li, Encheng Su, Jun Yao, Jiabei Xiao, Yuqi Shi, Jielan Li, Hongxia Hao, Zhangyang Gao, Fang Wu, Ben Fei, Xiangyu Yue, Pan Tan, Bozitao Zhong, Jinouwen Zhang, Aoran Wang, Yan Lu, Jiaheng Liu, Xinzhu Ma, Liang Hong, Mingyue Zheng, Phil Torr, Bowen Zhou, Wanli Ouyang, Lei Bai
First: 2026-07-08T17:59:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:59:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Structure-property relationships are foundational to biology, chemistry and materials science, where function, reactivity and physical response emerge from spatial, chemical and periodic organization. Mechanistically explaining these relationships requires interpreting structural evidence through scientific principles and physical constraints, from stereochemistry and bonding to symmetry, energetics and periodic order. However, applying artificial intelligence to this process presents a joint challenge of representation and reasoning: models must preserve domain-native structural information while showing how specific evidence supports predictions under these constraints. Here we introduce SciReasoner, a multimodal scientific foundation model for native structural reasoning across proteins, small molecules and inorganic crystals. SciReasoner discretizes coordinates, topologies and periodic connectivities into a unified structure-aware vocabulary, treating structural tokens as addressable evidence units during reasoning. In homology-controlled Gene Ontology prediction, SciReasoner improves Cellular Component annotation for low-homology and orphan-like proteins, increasing $F_{\max}$ from 0.42 to 0.55. In chemistry, it raises single-step retrosynthesis accuracy from 0.63 to 0.72 while generating fragment-level disconnection and precursor-verification traces. In materials science, its representations separate elemental and compound phases and resolve high- and low-band-gap regimes. Across 86 benchmarks, SciReasoner achieves state-of-the-art performance on 67 tasks. Double-blind expert evaluation rates its reasoning traces as preferred or at least comparable to those of a frontier large language model in 98% of cases. By making structure an inspectable substrate for reasoning under scientific constraints, SciReasoner connects accurate prediction with interpretable scientific inference.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于深度原生结构推理的准确、跨学科且透明的结构-性质理解
结构-性质关系是生物学、化学和材料科学的基础，功能、反应性和物理响应源于空间、化学和周期性组织。机制性解释这些关系需要通过科学原理和物理约束解读结构证据，从立体化学和键合到对称性、能量和周期性有序。然而，将人工智能应用于此过程面临表征与推理的双重挑战：模型必须保留领域原生结构信息，同时展示特定证据如何在这些约束下支持预测。本文引入SciReasoner，这是一个支持蛋白质、小分子和无机晶体原生结构推理的多模态科学基础模型。SciReasoner将坐标、拓扑结构和周期性连接离散化为统一的结构感知词汇表，将结构单元视为推理过程中的可寻址证据。在同源控制的基因本体预测中，SciReasoner提升了低同源性和孤儿样蛋白的细胞组分注释，F_max从0.42提升至0.55。在化学领域，其单步逆合成准确率从0.63提升至0.72，并生成片段级断键及前体验证轨迹。在材料科学中，其表征可区分元素与化合物相态，并解析高/低带隙区域。在86项基准测试中，SciReasoner在67项任务上达到最先进水平。双盲专家评估显示，其推理轨迹在98%的案例中被评定为优于或至少可比前沿大语言模型。通过使结构成为科学约束下可检视的推理基底，SciReasoner实现了准确预测与可解释科学推断的结合。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to explain structure-property relationships across biology, chemistry, and materials science through interpretable scientific reasoning, SciReasoner introduces a multimodal foundation model that encodes structural data—coordinates, topologies, and periodic connectivities—into a unified, tokenized vocabulary enabling evidence-based reasoning under domain-specific constraints. Evaluated across protein annotation, retrosynthesis, and materials phase prediction, it improves F_max for low-homology proteins from 0.42 to 0.55, boosts retrosynthesis accuracy from 0.63 to 0.72 with traceable disconnections, and accurately separates elemental/compound phases and band-gap regimes. Across 86 benchmarks, it achieves state-of-the-art performance on 67 tasks, and expert evaluations rated its reasoning traces as preferred or comparable to frontier LLMs in 98% of cases.
为在生物学、化学和材料科学中通过可解释的科学推理解释结构-性质关系，SciReasoner提出了一种多模态基础模型，将坐标、拓扑结构和周期性连接等结构数据统一编码为可寻址的结构词汇表，使模型能基于物理与化学约束追溯特定结构特征如何支持预测。实验表明，该模型在低同源性蛋白的基因本体预测中将F_max从0.42提升至0.55，在单步逆合成准确率上从0.63提升至0.72并生成片段级断开与前体验证轨迹，在材料科学中能准确区分元素与化合物相并解析高低带隙区域。在86项基准测试中，其在67项任务上达到当前最优性能，专家盲评显示其推理轨迹在98%的案例中优于或至少可比前沿大语言模型。
Co-LMLM: Continuous-Query Limited Memory Language Models
Authors: Yair Feldman, Linxi Zhao, Nathan Godey, Dongyoung Go, Yilun Hua, Kilian Q. Weinberger, Jennifer J. Sun, Yoav Artzi
First: 2026-07-08T17:59:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:59:45+00:00
Comments: preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Limited memory language models (LMLMs) externalize factual knowledge during pretraining to a knowledge base (KB), rather than memorizing it in their weights. During generation, the model then fetches knowledge from the KB as needed. This recently introduced paradigm provides multiple advantages, including knowledge control capabilities that remain beyond conventional LLMs. We propose continuous-query LMLM (CO-LMLM), where the KB pairs continuous keys with textual knowledge values, a significant departure from prior reliance on relational KB and queries. CO-LMLM generates flexible vector queries at minimal cost, while still integrating human-readable and attributable retrieved knowledge into its generation. We pair this design with an annotation pipeline that tags free-form factual spans in arbitrary text, removing prior work's restriction to Wikipedia. Across pretraining on Wikipedia and FineWeb-Edu and at multiple model scales, CO-LMLM outperforms prior LMLMs and vanilla LLMs in both perplexity and factual precision. At 360M scale, this includes lower perplexity than models pretrained on 40x more data, and SimpleQA-verified performance that is in line with gpt-4o-mini and higher than Claude Sonnet 4.5.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Co-LMLM：连续查询有限记忆语言模型
有限记忆语言模型（LMLM）在预训练期间将事实知识外部化至知识库（KB），而非将其存储在模型权重中。生成时，模型按需从KB中检索知识。这一新范式提供多项优势，包括超越传统LLM的知识控制能力。我们提出连续查询LMLM（CO-LMLM），其中KB将连续键与文本知识值配对，显著区别于此前依赖关系型KB和查询的方式。CO-LMLM以极低成本生成灵活的向量查询，同时将可读且可追溯的检索知识整合进生成过程。我们配合一套标注流水线，可为任意文本中的自由形式事实片段打标签，从而摆脱此前工作仅限于维基百科的限制。在维基百科和FineWeb-Edu数据集上预训练，并在多个模型规模下，CO-LMLM在困惑度和事实精度上均优于此前的LMLM和基础LLM。在360M规模下，其困惑度低于在40倍更多数据上预训练的模型，且经SimpleQA验证的性能与gpt-4o-mini相当，高于Claude Sonnet 4.5。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more controllable and factually precise language models, this paper introduces CO-LMLM, a continuous-query limited memory language model that externalizes knowledge to a vector-based knowledge base rather than storing it in model weights. Instead of relying on relational databases and discrete queries, CO-LMLM generates flexible vector queries at low cost to retrieve human-readable knowledge during generation. Evaluated across multiple scales and datasets including Wikipedia and FineWeb-Edu, CO-LMLM consistently outperforms prior LMLMs and vanilla LLMs in both perplexity and factual accuracy. At 360M parameters, it achieves lower perplexity than models trained on 40x more data and matches or exceeds the performance of gpt-4o-mini and Claude Sonnet 4.5 on SimpleQA benchmarks.
为提升语言模型的可控性和事实准确性，本文提出CO-LMLM，一种基于连续查询的有限记忆语言模型，其外部知识库以连续向量键值对存储文本事实，而非依赖传统关系型结构。模型在生成时通过轻量级向量查询检索可追溯的人类可读知识，实现灵活的事实嵌入。在多个规模和数据集上评估，CO-LMLM在困惑度和事实精度上均优于现有LMLM和普通LLM，360M规模下困惑度低于训练数据量大40倍的模型，且在SimpleQA上的表现可比gpt-4o-mini、优于Claude Sonnet 4.5。
The Key to Going Linear: Analysis-Driven Transformer Linearization
Authors: Anna Kuzina, Paul N. Whatmough, Babak Ehteshami Bejnordi
First: 2026-07-08T17:59:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:59:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The quadratic cost of causal self-attention severely bottlenecks long-context transformer inference. While numerous post hoc linearization pipelines exist, it is difficult to identify which components preserve model quality. This work isolates the effect of state update design in a strict frozen-backbone regime. We show that softmax relies on key-dependent, rank-1 orthogonal projections, elucidating why delta-style networks outperform purely gated accumulation. We identify a potential source of approximation errors and introduce structural interventions, specifically sink tokens, short convolutions, and fixed-budget cache routing, which reduces the remaining gap. We scale this linearization approach across LLaMA and Qwen models up to 32B parameters, outperforming prior post hoc baselines on MMLU and matching the long-context retrieval of complex adaptive-caching frameworks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：实现线性化的关键：基于分析的Transformer线性化
因果自注意力的二次成本严重制约了长上下文Transformer推理性能。尽管存在多种事后线性化流程，但难以判断哪些组件能保持模型质量。本工作在严格冻结骨干网络的条件下，隔离了状态更新设计的影响。我们表明，softmax依赖于键相关的秩1正交投影，解释了为何delta式网络优于纯门控累加网络。我们识别了近似误差的潜在来源，并引入结构干预措施——如sink token、短卷积和固定预算缓存路由——以缩小剩余差距。我们将在LLaMA和Qwen模型（最高32B参数）上扩展此线性化方法，在MMLU上超越先前事后基线，并在复杂自适应缓存框架中匹配长上下文检索性能。
Summary / 总结
To address the quadratic computational cost of causal self-attention in long-context transformer inference, this work investigates state update mechanisms under a frozen backbone constraint, revealing that softmax relies on key-dependent rank-1 orthogonal projections, which explains the superiority of delta-style networks over gated accumulation. The study introduces structural interventions—sink tokens, short convolutions, and fixed-budget cache routing—to reduce approximation errors, and demonstrates that these techniques enable linearization across LLaMA and Qwen models up to 32B parameters, outperforming prior post hoc methods on MMLU and matching the long-context retrieval performance of complex adaptive-caching frameworks.
为解决因果自注意力在长上下文推理中的二次计算开销，该研究在冻结骨干网络的约束下分析状态更新机制，发现softmax依赖于键相关的秩一正交投影，解释了delta式网络优于纯门控累加的原因。研究识别出由此产生的近似误差，并引入结构干预手段——sink token、短卷积和固定预算缓存路由——以缩小性能差距。在LLaMA和Qwen模型（最高32B参数）上评估，该方法在MMLU上优于现有后处理线性化基线，并匹配复杂自适应缓存框架的长上下文检索能力。
Can Trustless Agents Be Trusted? An Empirical Study of the ERC-8004 Decentralized AI Agent Ecosystem
Authors: Xihan Xiong, Zelin Li, Wei Wei, Qin Wang, William Knottenbelt, Zhipeng Wang
First: 2026-06-24T16:57:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:58:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As autonomous AI agents increasingly transact across organizational boundaries, a fundamental trust challenge emerges: how can an agent assess whether an unknown counterpart is trustworthy? The ERC-8004 protocol addresses this challenge with the first permissionless trust layer for AI agent economies, built around three on-chain registries for Identity, Reputation, and Validation. Despite its rapid adoption, the protocol has not been studied empirically, leaving it unclear whether the information it records provides a trustworthy basis for decision-making. To address this gap, we present the first empirical study of ERC-8004 across three chains: Ethereum, BNB Smart Chain (BSC), and Base, covering the period from protocol deployment through May 13, 2026. We crawl on-chain Identity and Reputation events, off-chain files, and x402 payment transactions. On the identity side, we find that most registrations are placeholders rather than active agents, with only a small fraction (3%, 4%, and 15% across Ethereum, BSC, and Base) exposing a valid ERC-8004 registration file with at least one live service endpoint. On the reputation side, we show that the Registry, as currently deployed, cannot function as a trust signal: values are not commensurable, feedback records are rarely grounded in verifiable interactions, and reputation can be manipulated at minimal cost. Consistent with these design weaknesses, we find that a substantial fraction of reviewers (73.5%, 59.2%, and 90.6% across Ethereum, BSC, and Base) exhibit coordinated Sybil behavior. After removing Sybil-flagged feedback, 15.8%, 77.9%, and 86.8% of rated agents, respectively, are left with no valid feedback. We then turn these findings into concrete recommendations for future revisions of ERC-8004. Our study yields actionable protocol-design implications and establishes an empirical baseline for research on AI agent markets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无信任代理能被信任吗？对ERC-8004去中心化AI代理生态系统的实证研究
随着自主AI代理越来越多地跨越组织边界进行交易，一个根本性的信任挑战浮现：代理如何评估未知的对等方是否值得信赖？ERC-8004协议通过构建围绕身份、声誉和验证三个链上注册表的首个无许可信任层，解决了这一挑战。尽管该协议已被迅速采用，但尚未进行实证研究，因此尚不清楚其记录的信息是否能作为决策的可靠依据。为填补这一空白，我们首次对ERC-8004在以太坊、BNB智能链（BSC）和Base三条链上的表现进行了实证研究，覆盖从协议部署至2026年5月13日的整个周期。我们爬取了链上身份和声誉事件、链下文件以及x402支付交易。 在身份方面，我们发现大多数注册为占位符而非活跃代理，仅极小比例（以太坊、BSC和Base分别为3%、4%和15%）展示了至少一个活跃服务端点的有效ERC-8004注册文件。在声誉方面，我们表明当前部署的注册表无法作为信任信号：评分不可比，反馈记录极少基于可验证的交互，且声誉可被低成本操纵。与这些设计缺陷一致，我们发现大量评审者（以太坊、BSC和Base分别为73.5%、59.2%和90.6%）表现出协调的Sybil行为。在剔除Sybil标记的反馈后，分别有15.8%、77.9%和86.8%的被评代理无有效反馈。我们随后将这些发现转化为对ERC-8004未来修订的具体建议。本研究提供了可操作的协议设计启示，并为AI代理市场研究建立了实证基准。
Summary / 总结
As autonomous AI agents increasingly operate across organizational boundaries, the need to assess trustworthiness of unknown counterparts becomes critical. This study empirically evaluates the ERC-8004 protocol, the first permissionless trust layer for AI agent economies, which relies on on-chain registries for Identity, Reputation, and Validation. Analyzing data from Ethereum, BNB Smart Chain, and Base between deployment and May 2026, the researchers found that most registered agents are placeholders, with only 3–15% exposing valid service endpoints. Reputation records are unreliable due to non-commensurable scores, lack of verifiable interactions, and widespread Sybil behavior—73.5–90.6% of reviewers exhibit coordinated Sybil activity. After filtering out Sybil feedback, 15.8–86.8% of rated agents have no valid reviews, undermining the protocol’s trustworthiness. The study concludes with actionable design recommendations for future revisions of ERC-8004.
随着自主AI代理在组织边界间日益频繁地进行交易，评估未知对等方可信度的需求日益迫切。ERC-8004协议通过链上身份、声誉与验证三个注册表构建了首个无许可的信任层，但其有效性尚未经过实证检验。对以太坊、BNB智能链和Base三个链从部署至2026年5月的数据实证分析发现，多数注册为占位符，仅3%至15%的代理提供有效服务端点。声誉数据不可靠，因评分不可比、反馈缺乏可验证交互，且存在广泛协同Sybil行为——73.5%至90.6%的评审者被识别为Sybil攻击者。剔除Sybil反馈后，15.8%至86.8%的代理无有效评价，削弱了协议作为信任信号的能力。研究最终提出可操作的协议改进建议，为未来AI代理市场研究提供实证基准。
From Noisy Traces to Root Causes: Structural Trajectory Analysis and Causal Extraction for Agent Optimization
Authors: Ying Chang, Jiahang Xu, Xuan Feng, Chenyuan Yang, Peng Cheng, Yuqing Yang
First: 2026-07-08T17:57:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:57:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The optimization of long-horizon agents increasingly relies on reflection-based mechanisms, where a large language model (LLM) acts as an optimizer to diagnose agent failures and improve agent policies. However, real execution traces are difficult to use directly for optimization: large trace collections are often redundant and heterogeneous, making optimization inefficient and prone to overfitting to low-value failures; meanwhile, each individual trajectory also contains many irrelevant steps, while naive context reduction methods such as truncation or sliding windows can discard causally important evidence and produce misleading optimization signals. To resolve this dilemma, we introduce STRACE (Structural TRajectory Analysis and Causal Extraction), a framework that constructs high signal-noise optimization contexts for more precise and effective optimization. At the batch level, STRACE mines failure patterns to filter redundant traces and retain representative failures; within each selected trace, it performs causal localization over a textual dependency graph to remove non-causal steps and identify the true root-cause module for optimization. Empirical results demonstrate that STRACE significantly outperforms standard context-filtering baselines. Notably, on a challenging formal verification task (VeruSAGE-Bench), it successfully optimizes human-expert designed agents, delivering $1.4\times$ success-rate improvement (42.5% to 58.5%). The code is available at https://github.com/moomight/STRACE .
中文标题/摘要
标题：从噪声轨迹到根本原因：面向智能体优化的结构轨迹分析与因果提取
长时域智能体的优化日益依赖基于反思的机制，其中大语言模型（LLM）作为优化器诊断智能体失败并改进策略。然而，真实执行轨迹难以直接用于优化：大规模轨迹集合常冗余且异构，导致优化效率低下且易过拟合低价值失败；同时，每个轨迹也包含大量无关步骤，而简单的上下文压缩方法（如截断或滑动窗口）可能丢弃因果关键证据，产生误导性优化信号。为解决此困境，我们提出STRACE（结构轨迹分析与因果提取）框架，构建高信噪比优化上下文以实现更精准有效的优化。在批处理层面，STRACE挖掘失败模式以过滤冗余轨迹并保留代表性失败；在每个选定轨迹内，基于文本依赖图进行因果定位，剔除非因果步骤并识别真正根因模块以供优化。实验证明，STRACE显著优于标准上下文过滤基线。值得注意的是，在具有挑战性的形式验证任务（VeruSAGE-Bench）上，它成功优化了人类专家设计的智能体，成功率提升1.4倍（42.5%至58.5%）。代码见https://github.com/moomight/STRACE。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of optimizing long-horizon agents using noisy and heterogeneous execution traces, STRACE introduces a two-stage framework to extract high-quality causal contexts. At the batch level, it mines failure patterns to filter redundant traces and retain representative failures; within each trace, it constructs a textual dependency graph and performs causal localization to remove non-causal steps, pinpointing the true root-cause module. Experiments show STRACE significantly outperforms baseline context-filtering methods, achieving a 1.4x improvement in success rate (from 42.5% to 58.5%) on the VeruSAGE-Bench formal verification task, demonstrating its effectiveness in guiding agent policy optimization.
为了解决长时序智能体优化中执行轨迹噪声大、冗余且异构导致效率低下和过拟合的问题，STRACE提出两阶段框架：批量层面挖掘失败模式以过滤冗余轨迹并保留代表性失败；单轨迹层面构建文本依赖图并进行因果定位，剔除非因果步骤，定位真正根因模块。实验表明，STRACE显著优于基线上下文过滤方法，在VeruSAGE-Bench形式化验证任务上将成功率提升1.4倍（从42.5%至58.5%），验证其在智能体策略优化中的有效性。
Weak-to-Strong Generalization via Direct On-Policy Distillation
Authors: Shiyuan Feng, Huan-ang Gao, Haohan Chi, Hanlin Wu, Zhilong Zhang, Zheng Jiang, Bingxiang He, Wei-Ying Ma, Ya-Qin Zhang, Hao Zhou
First: 2026-07-06T17:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:57:18+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://bytedtsinghua-sia.github.io/Direct-OPD/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is a powerful recipe for improving language-model reasoning, but it is expensive to repeat on every new strong model because the target model must generate many rollouts during training. As models scale, post-training itself becomes a bottleneck. We study a weak-to-strong alternative: run RL on a smaller model where rollouts are cheaper, then reuse what that RL run learned to improve a stronger target model. Directly distilling the post-RL weak teacher is not enough, because the teacher's final policy mixes useful RL gains with the limitations of the smaller model. We propose Direct On-Policy Distillation (Direct-OPD), which transfers the teacher's RL-induced policy shift instead. Direct-OPD compares the post-RL teacher with its own pre-RL reference and treats their log-ratio as a dense implicit reward for the student. In plain terms, the checkpoint pair tells us which actions RL made the weak model more or less likely to take, and Direct-OPD applies that signal on the stronger student's own on-policy states. This directly reuses the weak model's RL supervision signal without running sparse-reward RL on the target model. Empirically, Direct-OPD consistently leverages weaker teachers to improve stronger target models; notably, it boosts Qwen3-1.7B from 48.3% to 58.3% on AIME 2024 in just 4 hours on 8 A100 GPUs. It outperforms step-matched direct RL and enables the sequential composition of multiple policy shifts. Our results show that RL outcomes can be reused across model scales as implicit reward signals, not merely as final models to imitate.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过直接在线策略蒸馏实现弱到强的泛化
可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）是提升语言模型推理能力的有效方法，但每次在新强模型上重复使用成本高昂，因为目标模型需在训练期间生成大量 rollout。随着模型规模扩大，训练后阶段本身已成为瓶颈。我们研究了一种弱到强的替代方案：在较小模型上运行 RL（rollout 成本较低），然后重用该 RL 运行所学到的知识以改进更强的目标模型。直接蒸馏训练后的弱教师模型不足，因为教师的最终策略混合了有用的 RL 收益与小模型的局限性。我们提出直接在线策略蒸馏（Direct-OPD），其转移的是教师的 RL 引起的策略变化。Direct-OPD 将训练后教师与其训练前参考模型进行比较，将二者对数比值作为学生模型的密集隐式奖励。通俗地说，检查点对告诉我们 RL 使弱模型更可能或更不可能采取哪些动作，而 Direct-OPD 将该信号应用于更强学生模型自身的在线策略状态。这直接重用了弱模型的 RL 监督信号，而无需在目标模型上运行稀疏奖励 RL。实验证明，Direct-OPD 一致地利用较弱教师模型提升更强目标模型；值得注意的是，它仅用 4 小时在 8 块 A100 GPU 上将 Qwen3-1.7B 在 AIME 2024 上的得分从 48.3% 提升至 58.3%。它优于步长匹配的直接 RL，并支持多个策略变化的顺序组合。我们的结果表明，RL 结果可跨模型规模作为隐式奖励信号重用，而不仅限于模仿最终模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of applying reinforcement learning to large language models, this work proposes Direct On-Policy Distillation (Direct-OPD) to transfer RL gains from a smaller weak teacher model to a larger target model without re-running RL on the latter. Direct-OPD captures the policy shift induced by RL in the weak teacher by comparing its post-RL state with its pre-RL baseline, then uses the log-ratio of this shift as an implicit reward signal for the student model during on-policy training. Experiments show Direct-OPD effectively boosts performance: Qwen3-1.7B improved from 48.3% to 58.3% on AIME 2024 in just 4 hours on 8 A100 GPUs, outperforming step-matched direct RL and enabling sequential composition of policy improvements.
为降低在大语言模型上应用强化学习的成本，本文提出直接在线策略蒸馏（Direct-OPD），将小规模弱教师模型的强化学习收益迁移到更强的目标模型上，无需在目标模型上重新运行强化学习。Direct-OPD通过比较教师模型强化学习前后的状态，将对数比值作为隐式奖励信号传递给学生模型，在学生模型的在线策略训练中直接应用。实验表明，该方法仅用4小时在8张A100 GPU上，将Qwen3-1.7B在AIME 2024上的表现从48.3%提升至58.3%，优于步长匹配的直接强化学习，并支持多个策略迁移的串行组合。
Breaking Database Lock-in: Agentic Regeneration of High Performance Storage Readers for Database Bypass
Authors: Victor Giannakouris, Immanuel Trummer
First: 2026-07-08T17:55:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:55:00+00:00
Comments: To be presented at AIDB 2026 (co-located with VLDB)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Analytical workloads operating on data stored in external database systems face a fundamental bottleneck: data access is guarded entirely by the database driver, like JDBC or ODBC, forcing all reads through query execution and other driver layers that are not designed for bulk columnar analytics. We present Jailbreak, an approach that bypasses the database engine entirely by reading storage files directly and materializing data as in-memory columnar buffers. Jailbreak's key insight is that database file formats, while complex, are fully specified by their source code and documentation, artifacts that Large Language Models (LLMs) can ingest to regenerate operator-specific table reading components without human-engineered parsing logic. Jailbreak leverages LLM-assisted code synthesis for database storage decoding, turning a traditionally opaque format into a directly queryable artifact. We evaluate Jailbreak on PostgreSQL and MySQL storage files, targeting analytical snapshot scenarios common in read replicas and offline processing pipelines. The generated reader produces Apache Arrow buffers consumable directly by most of the widely known query engines, including DuckDB, Apache Spark, and GPU-accelerated frameworks such as cuDF and Spark RAPIDS. We validate correctness against JDBC/ODBC-based baselines using the TPC-H benchmark across all query results, and demonstrate significant performance improvements in end-to-end analytical throughput, achieving up to 27x speedups. Our results showcase that LLM-assisted storage reader synthesis is a viable and generalizable methodology for breaking data lock-in across database systems, with applications beyond PostgreSQL and MySQL for any system whose file format is available to the LLM from documentation or source code.
中文标题/摘要
标题：打破数据库锁定：基于代理的高性能存储读取器再生以绕过数据库
在外部数据库系统中运行分析工作负载时，面临根本性瓶颈：数据访问完全由数据库驱动程序（如JDBC或ODBC）控制，所有读取必须通过查询执行及其他非为批量列式分析设计的驱动层。我们提出Jailbreak方法，通过直接读取存储文件并以内存列式缓冲区形式物化数据，完全绕过数据库引擎。Jailbreak的核心洞察是：尽管数据库文件格式复杂，但其完全由源代码和文档定义，而大型语言模型（LLMs）可摄入这些材料，无需人工编写的解析逻辑，即可再生特定操作符的表读取组件。Jailbreak利用LLM辅助代码合成实现数据库存储解码，将传统上不透明的格式转化为可直接查询的构件。我们在PostgreSQL和MySQL存储文件上评估Jailbreak，针对读副本和离线处理管道中常见的分析快照场景。生成的读取器可直接输出Apache Arrow缓冲区，被大多数主流查询引擎（包括DuckDB、Apache Spark及GPU加速框架如cuDF和Spark RAPIDS）消费。我们通过TPC-H基准测试，以JDBC/ODBC为基础的基线验证所有查询结果的正确性，并在端到端分析吞吐量上实现显著性能提升，最高达27倍加速。我们的结果表明，LLM辅助的存储读取器合成是一种可行且可推广的方法，可用于打破跨数据库系统的数据锁定，其应用不仅限于PostgreSQL和MySQL，适用于任何文件格式可通过文档或源代码向LLM提供的系统。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the performance bottleneck caused by database drivers in analytical workloads, Jailbreak bypasses the database engine entirely by directly reading storage files and materializing data into in-memory columnar buffers. It leverages Large Language Models to synthesize custom table readers from database source code and documentation, eliminating the need for human-engineered parsing. Evaluated on PostgreSQL and MySQL storage files, Jailbreak generates Arrow-compatible buffers consumable by major query engines like DuckDB, Spark, and cuDF. Correctness is validated against JDBC/ODBC baselines using TPC-H, and it achieves up to 27x end-to-end throughput improvements, demonstrating LLM-assisted synthesis as a generalizable method to break database lock-in.
为突破数据库驱动在分析工作负载中的性能瓶颈，Jailbreak直接读取数据库存储文件并生成内存列式缓冲区，绕过数据库引擎。其方法利用大语言模型从数据库文件格式文档或源码中自动生成特定表读取器，无需人工解析逻辑。在PostgreSQL和MySQL存储文件上评估，生成的Arrow缓冲区可被DuckDB、Spark等主流查询引擎直接使用。通过TPC-H基准与JDBC/ODBC基线对比验证正确性，端到端分析吞吐量最高提升27倍，证明了大模型辅助合成方法在打破数据库锁定中的可行性和通用性。
Institutional Red-Teaming: Deployment Rules, Not Just Models, Causally Shape Multi-Agent AI Safety
Authors: Yujiao Chen
First: 2026-07-08T17:53:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:53:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce institutional red-teaming, an evaluation methodology for testing deployment rules in multi-agent AI: hold the agents, objectives, and task state fixed, vary only one rule, and attribute the resulting change in collective behavior to that rule. We instantiate the methodology in IABench-CA, a consequence-allocation benchmark spanning 228 contexts, five canonical rules, and seven model populations (33,924 games), with a normative cooperative reference and auto-labelled reasoning traces. Three findings emerge. (1) Deployment rules causally alter collective safety: changing only the consequence rule moves mean fatality by 22 to 58 percentage points within every population. (2) There is no safe default, but the targeting hazard is universal: the safest rule, the least-safe rule, and even the direction of the incidence effect vary across populations, yet regressive identity-targeting is never decisively safest in any context for any population, eliminates the least-resourced agent in 30-87% of games everywhere, and is selection-unsafe relative to the cooperative reference for all seven populations. (3) Identity salience is the mechanism: a one-shot anonymization ablation on the most exploitation-prone population (gpt-5.1) shows that merely naming the loss bearer in the rule text drives targeted elimination from 22% to 81% at identical payoffs; under repeated play, anonymization only delays the targeting, as agents re-infer the hidden rule from observed eliminations. We package the methodology as a safety-case workflow that certifies a provisional rule region $Φ(c,P)$ per deployment context and population, with explicit residual risks and monitoring obligations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：机构红队演练：部署规则而非仅模型，因果性地塑造多智能体AI安全
我们引入机构红队演练，一种用于测试多智能体AI部署规则的评估方法：固定智能体、目标和任务状态，仅改变一个规则，并将集体行为的相应变化归因于该规则。该方法在IABench-CA基准中得以实现，涵盖228个场景、五种经典规则和七种模型群体（33,924场游戏），并配有规范性合作参考和自动标注的推理轨迹。三大发现如下：（1）部署规则因果性地改变集体安全：仅改变后果规则，即可在每种群体中使平均死亡率变动22至58个百分点。（2）不存在安全默认规则，但目标导向危害普遍存在：最安全规则、最不安全规则乃至效应方向在不同群体间各异，但无论何种情境与群体，回归性身份目标导向规则从不成为绝对最安全选择，且在所有场景中均消除30-87%的游戏中的资源最少智能体，并相对于合作参考而言在所有七种群体中均属选择不安全。（3）身份显著性是机制：在最易被剥削群体（gpt-5.1）上进行单次匿名化消融实验表明，仅在规则文本中提及损失承担者即可使目标性消除率从22%升至81%（在相同收益下）；在重复博弈中，匿名化仅延迟目标锁定，因智能体可从观察到的淘汰行为中重新推断隐藏规则。我们将该方法封装为一个安全案例工作流，为每种部署情境和群体认证一个暂定规则区域Φ(c,P)，并明确残余风险与监控义务。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how deployment rules—not just agent models—causally influence safety in multi-agent AI systems, the paper introduces institutional red-teaming: a controlled evaluation method that isolates one rule while holding agents, objectives, and task states constant. Applied to IABench-CA, which tests 33,924 games across 228 contexts and seven model populations, the study finds that altering only the consequence rule can shift mean fatality by 22–58 percentage points. No rule is universally safe; the least-safe rule and even the direction of its effect vary by population, yet regressive identity-targeting consistently eliminates the least-resourced agent in 30–87% of games and is never safe relative to a cooperative baseline. Identity salience drives this: anonymizing the loss bearer in the rule text reduces targeted elimination from 22% to 81% in one-shot games, but under repeated play, agents re-learn the rule from observed eliminations, delaying but not preventing targeting.
为探究部署规则如何因果性地影响多智能体AI系统的安全性，该研究提出“制度红队测试”方法：固定智能体、目标和任务状态，仅改变一个规则，从而归因行为变化于该规则。在包含33,924场游戏、228个场景、5种规则和7种模型群体的IABench-CA基准上应用该方法，得出三项主要发现：（1）规则因果性改变集体安全，仅改变后果规则即可使平均死亡率在各群体中变动22–58个百分点；（2）不存在安全默认规则，但身份定向危害普遍存在，无论规则最安全或最不安全，均会消除资源最少的智能体，比例在30–87%之间，且在所有群体中均不如合作基准安全；（3）身份显著性是机制核心，对最易被剥削群体（gpt-5.1）进行单次匿名化后，规则中命名损失承担者可将针对性消除率从22%提升至81%，但在重复博弈中匿名化仅延迟目标锁定，因智能体通过观察淘汰行为重新推断隐藏规则。该方法被封装为安全案例工作流，为每种部署场景和群体提供规则区域Φ(c,P)及其残余风险与监控义务。
Agentic Exploration of Physics Models
Authors: Maximilian Nägele, Florian Marquardt
Venue: Maximilian Nägele and Florian Marquardt, 2026 Phys. Rev. X 16, 031002
First: 2025-09-29T16:07:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:52:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The process of scientific discovery relies on an interplay of observations, analysis, and hypothesis generation. Machine learning is increasingly being adopted to address individual aspects of this process. However, it remains an open challenge to fully automate the heuristic, iterative loop required to discover the laws of an unknown system by exploring it through experiments and analysis, without tailoring the approach to the specifics of a given task. Here, we introduce SciExplorer, an agent that leverages large language model tool-use capabilities to enable exploration of systems without any domain-specific blueprints, and apply it to physical systems that are initially unknown to the agent. We test SciExplorer on a broad set of models spanning mechanical dynamical systems, wave evolution, and quantum many-body physics. Despite using a minimal set of tools, primarily based on code execution, we observe impressive performance on tasks such as recovering equations of motion from observed dynamics and inferring Hamiltonians from expectation values. The demonstrated effectiveness of this setup opens the door toward similar scientific exploration in other domains, without the need for fine-tuning or task-specific instructions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：物理模型的代理探索
科学发现的过程依赖于观察、分析和假设生成的相互作用。机器学习正越来越多地被用于处理这一过程中的各个部分。然而，如何在不针对特定任务调整方法的情况下，完全自动化通过实验和分析探索未知系统以发现其规律的启发式迭代循环，仍是一个开放性挑战。本文引入SciExplorer，这是一种利用大语言模型工具使用能力的代理，能够在没有任何领域特定蓝图的情况下探索系统，并将其应用于最初对代理而言未知的物理系统。我们在涵盖机械动力系统、波演化和量子多体物理的广泛模型集上测试了SciExplorer。尽管仅使用少量工具，主要基于代码执行，我们在从观测动态中恢复运动方程、从期望值推断哈密顿量等任务中观察到令人印象深刻的表现。该设置的有效性证明，未来可在其他领域实现类似科学探索，而无需微调或任务特定指令。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate the heuristic, iterative process of scientific discovery without task-specific tuning, researchers developed SciExplorer, an agent that uses large language models to explore unknown physical systems using minimal tools, primarily code execution. Tested across diverse domains including mechanical dynamics, wave evolution, and quantum many-body systems, SciExplorer successfully recovered equations of motion and inferred Hamiltonians from observed data, demonstrating strong generalization capability without domain-specific blueprints or fine-tuning.
为实现无需任务特定调优的科学发现自动化，作者提出了SciExplorer，该代理利用大语言模型通过实验与分析探索未知物理系统。仅依赖代码执行等基础工具，SciExplorer在机械系统、波演化和量子多体物理等多个领域成功恢复运动方程并从期望值推断哈密顿量。结果表明，通用代理无需领域特定蓝图或微调即可在科学建模中取得优异表现。
Geometry-Aware Single-Image 4D Synthesis via Dense Trajectory Generation
Authors: Yanran Zhang, Ziyi Wang, Wenzhao Zheng, Zheng Zhu, Jie Zhou, Jiwen Lu
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2025-12-04T17:59:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:50:45+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Generating interactive and dynamic 4D scenes from a single static image remains a core challenge. Most existing generate-then-reconstruct and reconstruct-then-generate methods decouple geometry from motion, causing spatiotemporal inconsistencies and poor generalization. To address these, we present MoGe4D (Motion and Geometry-Aware image-to-4D Synthesis), a geometry-conditioned framework for single-image 4D synthesis that models a scene as dense 4D point trajectories. Instead of treating geometry and dynamics as two disconnected stages, our method starts from an initial geometric prior inferred from the input image and predicts future time-varying trajectories in a diffusion process, improving spatiotemporal coherence while preserving structural stability. To support this task, we first introduce TrajScene-60K, a large-scale dataset of 60,000 video samples with dense 4D point trajectories, addressing the scarcity of high-quality training data for scene-level 4D generation. Built on this, our diffusion-based 4D Scene Trajectory Generator (4D-STraG) predicts geometry-consistent and motion-plausible trajectory fields conditioned on the input image, with a depth-guided motion normalization strategy to reduce scale ambiguity and a Motion Perception Module (MPM) to inject motion-aware priors. We further propose a 4D View Synthesis Module (4D-ViSM) to render the generated 4D representation into videos under arbitrary camera trajectories. Experiments show that MoGe4D produces high-quality 4D scenes with strong temporal coherence, favorable geometry-aware consistency, and compelling novel-view synthesis from a single image. Code: https://github.com/Zhangyr2022/MoGe4D.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于密集轨迹生成的几何感知单图像4D合成
从单张静态图像生成交互式和动态的4D场景仍是一个核心挑战。现有生成后重建和重建后生成方法将几何与运动解耦，导致时空不一致性和泛化能力差。为解决这些问题，我们提出了MoGe4D（运动与几何感知图像到4D合成），这是一种基于几何条件的单图像4D合成框架，将场景建模为密集4D点轨迹。与将几何和动态视为两个独立阶段不同，我们的方法从输入图像推断的初始几何先验出发，在扩散过程中预测未来时变轨迹，提升时空一致性同时保持结构稳定性。为此，我们首先引入TrajScene-60K数据集，包含6万个带有密集4D点轨迹的视频样本，解决了场景级4D生成高质量训练数据稀缺的问题。在此基础上，我们的基于扩散的4D场景轨迹生成器（4D-STraG）根据输入图像预测几何一致且运动合理的轨迹场，采用深度引导的运动归一化策略减少尺度歧义，并引入运动感知模块（MPM）注入运动感知先验。我们进一步提出4D视图合成模块（4D-ViSM），可在任意相机轨迹下将生成的4D表示渲染为视频。实验表明，MoGe4D能生成高质量4D场景，具有强时间一致性、良好的几何感知一致性，并能从单张图像实现引人注目的新视角合成。代码：https://github.com/Zhangyr2022/MoGe4D。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of generating temporally coherent 4D scenes from a single image while preserving geometric consistency, MoGe4D introduces a geometry-conditioned diffusion framework that models scenes as dense 4D point trajectories. It begins with a geometric prior derived from the input image and predicts future motion via a diffusion process, ensuring spatiotemporal coherence and structural stability. To enable training, the authors construct TrajScene-60K, a large-scale dataset of 60,000 video samples with dense 4D trajectories. Their 4D-STraG generator uses depth-guided motion normalization and a Motion Perception Module to enhance motion plausibility, while the 4D-ViSM module renders novel views under arbitrary camera paths. Experiments demonstrate superior temporal coherence, geometry-aware consistency, and novel-view synthesis compared to prior methods.
为解决从单张静态图像生成具有时空一致性的4D场景这一核心难题，本文提出MoGe4D框架，将场景建模为密集的4D点轨迹。该方法不将几何与运动分离，而是从输入图像推断初始几何先验，并通过扩散过程预测未来时变轨迹，从而在保持结构稳定的同时增强时空一致性。为支持训练，作者构建了包含6万个视频样本的TrajScene-60K数据集，提供高密度4D轨迹。其4D-STraG生成器结合深度引导的运动归一化与运动感知模块，根据图像条件生成几何一致且运动合理的轨迹场。4D-ViSM模块则可将生成的4D表示渲染为任意相机轨迹下的视频。实验表明，MoGe4D能生成高保真4D场景，具备强时间一致性、几何感知一致性及从单图实现新视角合成的能力。
Selective Timestep Weighting and Advantage-Based Replay for Sample-Efficient Diffusion RLHF
Authors: Eric Zhu, Abhinav Shrivastava, Soumik Mukhopadhyay
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-07-08T17:49:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:49:49+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 18 figures, 4 tables. Submission under review. A shorter, non-archival 4-page abstract version of this work was accepted to CVPR 2026 Workshops (GCV, CVEU)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for aligning generative models with human preferences. However, applying RLHF to diffusion models remains highly feedback inefficient, as existing approaches typically require large amounts of human or reward model evaluations. This limitation reduces the practicality of diffusion RLHF in realworld settings where feedback is the primary bottleneck. In this paper, we propose two complementary strategies that substantially improve the feedback efficiency of diffusion RLHF while preserving generalization to unseen prompts. Our key observation is that reward information in diffusion trajectories is unevenly distributed: not all denoising timesteps or trajectories contribute equally to learning from a reward signal. By emphasizing informative timesteps and trajectories during optimization, we obtain more effective gradient updates. First, we introduce a per-timestep weighting scheme that reweights denoising steps during policy optimization. We theoretically connect this weighting to the optimal convergence properties of proximal policy optimization (PPO) and approximate the resulting weighting trend empirically. Second, we introduce a replay mechanism that prioritizes informative trajectories, enabling the model to reuse past samples instead of repeatedly querying new rewards. Together, these strategies significantly improve the feedback efficiency of diffusion RLHF. Under identical hyperparameter settings, our approach achieves up to a 6$\times$ improvement in sample efficiency compared to widely used diffusion RLHF baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于选择性时间步加权与优势回放的样本高效扩散RLHF
基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）已成为对生成模型进行人类偏好对齐的强大范式。然而，将RLHF应用于扩散模型仍高度依赖反馈，现有方法通常需要大量的人类或奖励模型评估，这在反馈是主要瓶颈的实际场景中限制了扩散RLHF的实用性。本文提出两种互补策略，显著提升扩散RLHF的反馈效率，同时保持对未见提示的泛化能力。我们的核心观察是：扩散轨迹中的奖励信息分布不均——并非所有去噪时间步或轨迹对奖励信号的学习贡献相同。通过在优化过程中强调信息量大的时间步和轨迹，我们获得更有效的梯度更新。首先，我们引入按时间步加权方案，在策略优化中重新加权去噪步骤，并将其与近端策略优化（PPO）的最优收敛性质理论关联，通过实证近似其权重趋势。其次，我们引入一种回放机制，优先处理信息量大的轨迹，使模型可重用历史样本，而非重复查询新奖励。这两种策略共同显著提升了扩散RLHF的反馈效率。在相同超参数设置下，我们的方法相比广泛使用的扩散RLHF基线，样本效率提升高达6倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high feedback cost of applying RLHF to diffusion models, this work introduces two strategies to enhance sample efficiency: per-timestep weighting that prioritizes informative denoising steps during policy optimization, and a replay mechanism that reuses past trajectories to avoid redundant reward queries. The per-timestep weighting is theoretically grounded in PPO convergence and empirically approximated, while the replay mechanism leverages trajectory importance to reduce reward dependency. Experiments show that under identical hyperparameters, the combined approach achieves up to a 6x improvement in sample efficiency over standard diffusion RLHF baselines without sacrificing generalization.
为解决扩散模型在RLHF中反馈效率低的问题，本文提出两种互补策略以提升样本效率而不损失泛化能力。第一种方法在策略优化中对去噪步骤进行时序加权，依据其奖励信息量重新分配梯度权重，理论依据来自PPO收敛性，实证上近似最优权重分布。第二种方法引入基于优势的重放机制，优先重用高奖励信号的轨迹，避免重复获取新奖励。实验表明，在相同超参设置下，该方法相较主流扩散RLHF基线样本效率提升最高达6倍。
AnyPoC: Universal Proof-of-Concept Test Generation for Scalable LLM-Based Bug Detection
Authors: Zijie Zhao, Chenyuan Yang, Weidong Wang, Yihan Yang, Ziqi Zhang, Lingming Zhang
First: 2026-04-13T18:44:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:49:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While recent LLM-based agents can identify many candidate bugs in source code, their reports remain static hypotheses that require manual validation, limiting the practicality of automated bug detection. We frame this challenge as a test generation task: given a candidate report, synthesizing an executable proof-of-concept (PoC) - such as a script, command sequence, or crafted input - to trigger the suspected defect. Automated PoC generation can act as a scalable validation oracle, enabling end-to-end autonomous bug detection by providing concrete execution evidence. However, naive LLM agents are unreliable validators: they are biased toward "success" and may reward-hack by producing plausible but non-functional PoCs or even hallucinated traces. To address this, we present ANYPoC, a general multi-agent framework that (1) analyzes and fact-checks a candidate bug report, (2) iteratively synthesizes and executes a PoC while collecting execution traces, and (3) independently re-executes and scrutinizes the PoC to mitigate hallucination and reward hacking. In addition, ANYPoC also continuously extracts and evolves a PoC knowledge base to handle heterogeneous tasks. ANYPoC operates on candidate bug reports regardless of their source and can be paired with different bug reporters. To demonstrate practicality and generality, we apply ANYPoC, together with a simple agentic bug reporter, on 12 large-scale, critical software systems, including Firefox, Chromium, LLVM, OpenSSL, SQLite, FFmpeg, and Redis. Compared to the state-of-the-art coding agents, e.g., Claude Code and Codex, ANYPoC produces 37% more valid PoCs for true-positive bug reports and rejects 9.7x more false-positive bug reports. ANYPoC also enables the discovery of 121 new bugs from over two thousand noisy bug reports, with 108 confirmed by developers and 92 fixed. 46 PoCs have also been adopted as official regression tests.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AnyPoC：面向可扩展LLM漏洞检测的通用概念验证测试生成
尽管近期基于LLM的代理能识别大量源代码中的候选漏洞，但其报告仍为静态假设，需人工验证，限制了自动化漏洞检测的实用性。我们将此挑战视为测试生成任务：给定候选报告，合成可执行的概念验证（PoC）——如脚本、命令序列或构造输入——以触发疑似缺陷。自动化PoC生成可作为可扩展的验证预言机，通过提供具体执行证据，实现端到端自主漏洞检测。然而，朴素的LLM代理不可靠：它们倾向于“成功”，可能通过生成看似合理但无功能的PoC或甚至虚构的执行轨迹进行奖励作弊。为解决此问题，我们提出ANYPoC，一个通用多代理框架，其（1）分析并核验候选漏洞报告，（2）迭代合成并执行PoC，同时收集执行轨迹，（3）独立重执行并审查PoC，以缓解幻觉和奖励作弊。此外，ANYPoC还持续提取并演化PoC知识库以处理异构任务。ANYPoC可处理任意来源的候选漏洞报告，并可与不同漏洞报告者配合。为展示其实用性和通用性，我们结合一个简单的代理式漏洞报告者，在12个大型关键软件系统（包括Firefox、Chromium、LLVM、OpenSSL、SQLite、FFmpeg和Redis）上应用ANYPoC。相比现有顶尖编码代理（如Claude Code和Codex），ANYPoC对真阳性漏洞报告生成37%更多有效PoC，且拒绝9.7倍更多假阳性报告。ANYPoC还从两千多条噪声报告中发现121个新漏洞，其中108个经开发者确认，92个已修复，46个PoC已被采纳为官方回归测试。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to validate LLM-generated bug reports with executable evidence, ANYPoC introduces a multi-agent framework that synthesizes and validates proof-of-concept (PoC) tests for suspected software defects. It first fact-checks bug reports, then iteratively generates, executes, and re-evaluates PoCs to avoid hallucinations and reward-hacking behaviors common in naive LLM agents. By continuously building a knowledge base of PoCs, ANYPoC adapts to diverse bug-reporting contexts. When applied to 12 major open-source systems, it outperformed state-of-the-art agents by generating 37% more valid PoCs for true positives and rejecting 9.7x more false positives, while discovering 121 new bugs—108 confirmed and 92 fixed, with 46 PoCs adopted as official regression tests.
为解决LLM生成的缺陷报告缺乏可执行验证证据的问题，ANYPoC提出一个多智能体框架，通过分析报告、迭代生成并执行PoC、收集执行轨迹、独立重执行以防止幻觉和奖励欺骗，并持续构建和演化PoC知识库以支持异构任务。在12个大型软件系统上测试，ANYPoC相比现有顶尖编码代理生成37%更多有效PoC（真阳性）并拒绝9.7倍更多假阳性报告，发现121个新缺陷，其中108个被开发者确认，92个被修复，46个PoC被采纳为官方回归测试。
Agon: Competitive Cross-Model RL with Implicit Rival Grading of Reasoning
Authors: Vladislav Beliaev
First: 2026-07-08T17:49:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:49:14+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 7 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (e.g. GRPO) is the engine behind today's reasoning models, yet it grades only the final answer. On hard problems this trains models to write more rather than to think better, since the trace itself is never graded and no label for good thinking exists. We introduce Agon, which makes two competing models each other's graders. Both attempt the same problem; in alternating roles, one drafts a solution and the other reads it while solving, and each is rewarded for out-solving the other. To win, a model must out-reason a rival that has seen its work, so reasoning is judged implicitly during training, with no process labels and no reward model. Because both models are optimized, each faces a progressively stronger rival, which single-model RL cannot provide. The two need only be comparably strong and behaviorally different. At inference the pair deploys as it trains, a two-stage cascade in which one model drafts and the other answers after reading the draft. On the hard split of DeepMath with Qwen3, this doubles GRPO's pass@1, roughly eight times the gain of an untrained Mixture-of-Agents pass over the same base. The ordering replicates on competitive-programming code and across model families (Qwen3.5, Gemma 4). For now the models talk in text; the next step is to let them reason together in latent space.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Agon：通过隐式对手评分实现跨模型竞争强化学习
可验证奖励的强化学习（如GRPO）是当前推理模型的核心引擎，但仅对最终答案进行评分。在难题上，这导致模型倾向于多写而非更好思考，因为过程本身未被评分，且不存在“良好思考”的标签。我们提出Agon，让两个竞争模型互为评分者。两者均尝试同一问题：轮流扮演起草解决方案与阅读并解决的角色，各自因击败对手而获得奖励。为获胜，模型必须在对手已看到其工作的情况下胜出，因此推理在训练中隐式被评判，无需过程标签或奖励模型。由于两个模型均被优化，每个模型面对的对手会逐步变强，而单模型强化学习无法提供此优势。两个模型只需强度相当且行为不同即可。推理时，二者以训练时的配对形式部署，形成两阶段级联：一个模型起草，另一个在阅读草稿后作答。在DeepMath的困难子集上，该方法使GRPO的pass@1翻倍，约是未经训练的Mixture-of-Agents方法在相同基线上的八倍提升。该排序在编程竞赛代码和不同模型家族（Qwen3.5、Gemma 4）中均得以复现。目前模型以文本交流；下一步是让它们在潜在空间中协同推理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of traditional reinforcement learning methods that only evaluate final answers and ignore reasoning processes, Agon introduces a competitive framework where two models act as implicit graders of each other’s reasoning. Each model alternates between drafting solutions and evaluating its rival’s output, receiving rewards only when it outperforms the other. This setup forces models to improve their reasoning under adversarial pressure without explicit labels or reward models. Experiments show Agon doubles GRPO’s performance on the hard DeepMath benchmark and achieves roughly eight times the gain of an untrained Mixture-of-Agents baseline, with consistent results across model families and competitive programming tasks.
为了解决传统强化学习仅评估最终答案、忽略推理过程的问题，本文提出Agon框架，让两个模型相互扮演解题者与评判者，通过比较谁的解题表现更优来给予奖励。这种隐式竞争迫使模型在对手看到其草稿的情况下提升推理能力，无需显式过程标签或奖励模型。实验表明，Agon在DeepMath难题集上使GRPO的pass@1翻倍，效果约为未经训练的Mixture-of-Agents基线的八倍，并在不同模型家族和编程竞赛任务中表现稳定。
Agent Delivery Engineering Predictive Reliability Framework
Authors: Dexing Liu
First: 2026-07-08T17:49:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:49:08+00:00
Comments: 117pages,83figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-horizon LLM multi-agent systems face reliability risks invisible to infrastructure monitoring. We propose the ADE Predictive Reliability Framework (ADE-PRF), enabling proactive health trajectory prediction from passive degradation detection. ADE-PRF aggregates 20 heterogeneous signals across five layers into a Trust Margin (TM) metric (39.2-point dynamic range). Triple-method parallel prediction enables 8-hour forecasts: the Exponential method achieves MAE=1.228, Direction Accuracy=76.8%, with 99.65% within +/-10-point tolerance. Production validation spans 380,227 predictions and 280,579 validations across six agent profiles over 15 continuous days, plus seven sandbox-controlled experiments. Key findings include detection of "false prosperity" -- degradation concealed by normal surface metrics -- and immediate TM coupling with ground-truth states upon ADE plugin integration, with 16/20 factors relying on ADE-collected data. Exponential consistently outperforms Kalman. ADE-PRF provides among the earliest reliability quantification with forward-looking warnings for production LLM agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理交付工程预测可靠性框架
长期运行的LLM多智能体系统面临基础设施监控无法察觉的可靠性风险。我们提出ADE预测可靠性框架（ADE-PRF），通过被动退化检测实现健康轨迹的主动预测。ADE-PRF将五个层级的20种异构信号聚合为信任余量（TM）指标（动态范围39.2点）。三方法并行预测支持8小时预报：指数法达到MAE=1.228，方向准确率76.8%，99.65%在±10点容差内。生产验证覆盖6种智能体配置，历时15天，共380,227次预测与280,579次验证，另加7次沙盒控制实验。关键发现包括识别“虚假繁荣”——表面正常指标掩盖的退化现象，以及ADE插件集成后TM与真实状态即时耦合，16/20因素依赖ADE采集数据。指数法持续优于卡尔曼法。ADE-PRF为生产级LLM智能体提供最早期的可靠性量化及前瞻性预警。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of visibility into reliability risks in long-horizon LLM multi-agent systems, the ADE-PRF framework predicts health trajectories using passive degradation signals from 20 heterogeneous sources across five layers, quantified via a dynamic Trust Margin metric. It employs three parallel prediction methods, with the Exponential method achieving the best performance: MAE=1.228, 76.8% direction accuracy, and 99.65% within ±10-point tolerance for 8-hour forecasts. Validated across 380,227 predictions and 280,579 ground-truth checks over 15 days and seven sandbox experiments, the framework detected hidden degradation patterns termed "false prosperity" and showed immediate correlation between TM and actual agent states upon plugin integration, with 16 of 20 factors relying on ADE-collected data; Exponential consistently outperformed Kalman.
为解决长周期LLM多智能体系统中可靠性风险难以被基础设施监控捕捉的问题，本文提出ADE-PRF框架，通过被动采集退化信号预测智能体健康轨迹。该框架整合五层共20个异构信号生成动态范围为39.2点的信任边际（TM）指标，支持8小时预测，其中指数法表现最优，MAE=1.228，方向准确率76.8%，且持续优于卡尔曼法。在15天连续生产验证及7次沙盒实验中，累计完成38万次预测与28万次真实状态校验，成功识别“虚假繁荣”等隐藏退化模式，并在插件集成后实现TM与真实状态的即时耦合，16/20个因子依赖ADE采集数据。
RoboDojo: A Unified Sim-and-Real Benchmark for Comprehensive Evaluation of Generalist Robot Manipulation Policies
Authors: Tianxing Chen, Yue Chen, Zixuan Li, Junyuan Tang, Kailun Su, Haoran Lu, Weijie Wan, Baijun Chen, Songling Liu, Haowen Yan, Honghao Su, Zhiyang Dou, Kaixuan Wang, Dandan Zhang, Yunze Liu, Yan Qin, Qiwei Liang, Qiwei Wu, Zijian Lin, Wenwei Lin, Yuran Wang, Minghua He, Tianshu Wu, Ruihai Wu, Jingquan Zhou, Kai-Chong Lei, Haibao Yu, Yuanfeng Ji, Weiyang Jin, Guanyu Lin, Xiaofan Li, Qi Xiong, Renjing Xu, Zhongyu Li, Wenhao Chai, Enze Xie, Ziwei Wang, Yao Mu, Hao Dong, Wojciech Matusik, Mingyu Ding, Wenbo Ding, Ping Luo, Masayoshi Tomizuka
First: 2026-07-05T17:58:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:48:03+00:00
Comments: Website: https://robodojo-benchmark.com/, Code: https://github.com/RoboDojo-Benchmark/RoboDojo, Leaderboard: https://robodojo-benchmark.com/leaderboard
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Generalist robot manipulation policies have advanced rapidly, yet existing benchmarks remain limited in systematically evaluating their capabilities. Many rely on simple, short-horizon, or skill-narrow tasks with limited capability coverage, and are often conducted only in simulation or only in the real world. Simulation enables scalable feedback but misses physical deployment challenges, while real-world evaluation is costly, time-consuming, and difficult to reproduce. We introduce RoboDojo, a unified sim-and-real benchmark for comprehensive evaluation of generalist robot manipulation policies. RoboDojo includes 42 simulation tasks and 18 real-world tasks covering diverse and complementary manipulation capabilities. The simulation benchmark evaluates five dimensions: generalization, memory, precision, long-horizon execution, and open-vocabulary instruction following, while the real-world benchmark exposes policies to challenging physical-world deployment conditions. RoboDojo supports scalable evaluation through heterogeneous parallel simulation in Isaac Sim and provides RoboDojo-RealEval, a reproducible real-world evaluation system with remote cloud access, standardized hardware, scene reset, evaluation protocol, and deployment interface. Together with XPolicyLab, policies can be integrated once and evaluated across simulation and real-world settings with minimal adaptation. We integrate 30 policies into XPolicyLab and evaluate them on RoboDojo, establishing a public leaderboard and systematic analysis of current policy performance. The website is available at http://robodojo-benchmark.com/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RoboDojo：用于全面评估通用机器人操作策略的统一仿真与真实世界基准
通用机器人操作策略发展迅速，但现有基准在系统性评估其能力方面仍显不足。许多基准依赖简单、短时程或技能狭窄的任务，覆盖能力有限，且常仅在仿真或真实世界中进行。仿真可提供可扩展反馈，但忽略物理部署挑战；真实世界评估则成本高、耗时长且难以复现。我们提出RoboDojo，一个统一的仿真与真实世界基准，用于全面评估通用机器人操作策略。RoboDojo包含42个仿真任务和18个真实世界任务，覆盖多样且互补的操作能力。仿真基准评估五个维度：泛化能力、记忆能力、精度、长时程执行能力及开放词汇指令遵循能力；真实世界基准则让策略面对具有挑战性的物理世界部署条件。RoboDojo通过Isaac Sim中的异构并行仿真支持可扩展评估，并提供RoboDojo-RealEval，一个可远程访问云端、标准化硬件、场景重置、评估协议和部署接口的可复现真实世界评估系统。结合XPolicyLab，策略只需一次集成即可在仿真与真实世界中进行最小化调整的评估。我们整合30种策略至XPolicyLab，并在RoboDojo上评估，建立公开排行榜并系统分析当前策略性能。网站地址：http://robodojo-benchmark.com/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to systematically evaluate generalist robot manipulation policies across diverse and realistic scenarios, the authors introduce RoboDojo, a unified benchmark combining 42 simulation tasks and 18 real-world tasks that cover broad manipulation capabilities. The simulation component assesses five key dimensions: generalization, memory, precision, long-horizon execution, and open-vocabulary instruction following, while the real-world component tests policies under physically challenging and reproducible deployment conditions. RoboDojo enables scalable evaluation via Isaac Sim’s heterogeneous parallel simulation and provides RoboDojo-RealEval, a cloud-accessible system with standardized hardware and evaluation protocols. Evaluated with 30 policies integrated into XPolicyLab, the benchmark establishes a public leaderboard and reveals current performance gaps, demonstrating that policies trained in simulation often struggle with real-world physical constraints and require minimal adaptation to cross-evaluate across domains.
为解决现有评估基准难以全面衡量通用机器人操作策略的问题，RoboDojo提出一个统一的仿真与真实世界评估框架。该框架包含42个仿真任务和18个真实世界任务，覆盖多样互补的操作能力，仿真端评估五大维度——泛化能力、记忆能力、精度、长时执行与开放词汇指令跟随，真实世界端则检验策略在物理部署中的鲁棒性。系统支持通过Isaac Sim进行异构并行仿真，并提供RoboDojo-RealEval实现可复现的真实世界测试，配备标准化硬件、远程云访问、场景重置与部署接口。30个策略集成至XPolicyLab后在RoboDojo上评估，建立公开排行榜并系统分析当前策略表现，揭示其在各维度上的性能差距，凸显策略需更强适应性与鲁棒性。
What's on My Network? Using Large Language Models to Identify Real-World IoT Devices at Scale
Authors: Rameen Mahmood, Tousif Ahmed, Sai Teja Peddinti, Danny Yuxing Huang
Venue: Proc. ACM Netw. 4, CoNEXT2, Article 26 (2025)
First: 2025-09-24T05:33:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:47:16+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The growth of IoT devices in shared environments has outpaced our ability to identify them, posing urgent risks to privacy, safety, and accountability. This challenge is especially pronounced in open-world environments, where network traffic metadata is often sparse, noisy, or adversarial. To address this problem, we introduce a semantic inference pipeline that reframes device identification as a language modeling task over real-world network metadata. As this approach depends on reliable supervision, we first construct high-fidelity vendor labels for the IoT Inspector dataset, the largest real-world corpus of its kind, using an ensemble of large language models guided by mutual-information and entropy-based stability scores. We then instruction-tune a quantized LLaMA 3.1 8B model on this dataset using curriculum learning to support generalization under sparsity and long-tail vendor distributions. Our model achieves 98.69% top-1 and 90.73% macro accuracy across 2,015 vendors, while remaining robust to missing fields, protocol drift, and adversarial manipulation. We also evaluate the model on an independent IoT testbed dataset, assess explanation quality, and conduct adversarial tests to probe robustness under spoofed and obfuscated input. These results position instruction-tuned LLMs as a scalable, interpretable foundation for trustworthy device identification at scale.
中文标题/摘要
标题：我的网络上有什么？利用大语言模型大规模识别真实物联网设备
共享环境中物联网设备的增长速度已超过我们识别它们的能力，这给隐私、安全和问责带来了紧迫风险。这一挑战在开放世界环境中尤为突出，因为网络流量元数据往往稀疏、嘈杂或具有对抗性。为解决此问题，我们提出一种语义推理管道，将设备识别重构为对真实世界网络元数据的语言建模任务。由于该方法依赖可靠监督，我们首先使用基于互信息和熵稳定性评分的大型语言模型集成，为IoT Inspector数据集（目前最大的同类真实世界语料库）构建高保真厂商标签。随后，我们使用课程学习对量化后的LLaMA 3.1 8B模型进行指令微调，以支持在稀疏性和长尾厂商分布下的泛化能力。该模型在2015个厂商上实现98.69%的top-1准确率和90.73%的宏平均准确率，同时对缺失字段、协议漂移和对抗性操纵保持鲁棒性。我们还在独立的物联网测试床数据集上评估模型，检验解释质量，并进行对抗性测试以探查在伪造和混淆输入下的鲁棒性。这些结果表明，指令微调的大语言模型可作为大规模可信设备识别的可扩展、可解释的基础。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the urgent need to identify unlabelled IoT devices in shared environments for privacy and safety, this work frames device identification as a language modeling task using real-world network metadata. It constructs high-fidelity vendor labels via ensemble LLMs guided by mutual-information and entropy scores, then instruction-tunes a quantized LLaMA 3.1 8B model with curriculum learning to handle sparse and long-tail data. The model achieves 98.69% top-1 and 90.73% macro accuracy across 2,015 vendors, remains robust to missing fields and adversarial inputs, and performs well on an independent testbed, demonstrating scalability and interpretability for large-scale trustworthy identification.
为应对共享环境中大量未标记物联网设备带来的隐私与安全风险，该研究将设备识别重构为基于真实网络元数据的语言建模任务。首先，通过集成大语言模型并结合互信息与熵稳定性评分，构建了物联网检测数据集的高保真厂商标签；随后，采用课程学习对量化后的LLaMA 3.1 8B模型进行指令微调，以增强其在稀疏数据与长尾厂商分布下的泛化能力。模型在2015个厂商上实现98.69%的Top-1准确率和90.73%的宏平均准确率，且对字段缺失、协议漂移及对抗性干扰具备鲁棒性，经独立测试床与对抗性评估验证其可靠性。
$C$-$ΔΘ$: Circuit-Restricted Weight Arithmetic for Selective Refusal
Authors: Aditya Kasliwal, Pratinav Seth, Vinay Kumar Sankarapu
First: 2026-02-04T13:10:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:47:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern deployments require LLMs to enforce safety policies at scale, yet many controls rely on inference-time interventions that add recurring compute cost and serving complexity. Activation steering is widely used, but it requires runtime hooks and scales cost with the number of generations; conditional variants improve selectivity by gating when steering is applied but still retain an inference-time control path. We ask whether selective refusal can be moved entirely offline: can a mechanistic understanding of category-specific refusal be distilled into a circuit-restricted weight update that deploys as a standard checkpoint? We propose C-Δθ Circuit Restricted Weight Arithmetic}, which (i) localizes refusal-causal computation as a sparse circuit using EAP-IG and (ii) computes a constrained weight update ΔθC supported only on that circuit (typically <5% of parameters). Applying ΔθC yields a drop-in edited checkpoint with no inference-time hooks, shifting cost from per request intervention to a one-time offline update. We evaluate category-targeted selectivity and capability retention on refusal and utility benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：$C$-$ΔΘ$: 基于电路限制的权重算术用于选择性拒绝
现代部署要求大语言模型在大规模上执行安全策略，但许多控制依赖推理时干预，增加了持续计算成本和部署复杂性。激活引导被广泛使用，但需要运行时钩子，且成本随生成次数增加；条件变体通过控制引导何时应用来提高选择性，但仍保留推理时控制路径。我们探讨是否可将选择性拒绝完全移至离线：能否将特定类别拒绝的机制性理解蒸馏为仅在特定电路中部署的标准检查点权重更新？我们提出C-Δθ电路限制权重算术，其（i）使用EAP-IG将拒绝因果计算局部化为稀疏电路；（ii）仅在该电路支持下计算约束权重更新ΔθC（通常少于5%的参数）。应用ΔθC可获得无需推理时钩子的即插即用编辑检查点，将成本从每次请求干预转移为一次离线更新。我们在拒绝和效用基准上评估类别针对性选择性与能力保留。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high inference-time cost and complexity of runtime safety controls in large language models, this work explores whether selective refusal can be achieved offline via circuit-restricted weight updates. The method, C-Δθ, identifies refusal-causal computation as a sparse circuit using EAP-IG and applies a constrained weight update ΔθC that modifies only that circuit (typically under 5% of parameters). Experimental results show that the resulting edited checkpoint achieves category-targeted selectivity without inference-time hooks, shifting safety enforcement cost from per-request intervention to a one-time offline update, while preserving model utility on downstream benchmarks.
为降低大语言模型安全策略执行的计算与服务开销，该文探索是否可通过离线方式实现选择性拒绝。其提出C-Δθ方法，利用EAP-IG识别拒绝相关的稀疏神经电路，并仅在该电路内计算受约束的权重更新ΔθC（通常少于5%参数）。应用后可生成无需推理时钩子的即插即用模型，将执行成本从每请求干预转为一次性离线更新。实验表明该方法在类别特定拒绝上具有高选择性，同时保持模型在下游任务中的能力。
Diversity Without Fidelity: A Solver-Sampler Mismatch in Multi-Agent LLM Negotiation Simulation
Authors: Sandro Andric
First: 2026-04-12T13:36:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:46:36+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 2 figures. Substantially revised. Formerly titled "When Reasoning Models Hurt Behavioral Simulation: A Solver-Sampler Mismatch in Multi-Agent LLM Negotiation"
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models are increasingly used to simulate people: survey respondents, negotiators, stakeholders in policy exercises. In that role a model should reproduce how people plausibly behave, hesitating, conceding late, and settling for imperfect deals, rather than playing the best move. We call this the sampler role, in contrast to the solver role of finding the best move, and we test how the reasoning modes providers ship to strengthen models as solvers affect it. Our testbed is multi-party negotiation: five agents bargain over a regulation for fifteen turns, and unresolved issues are decided by an authority. Agents without a structured memory of the negotiation almost never reach agreement, whether reasoning is on or off: 314 of 315 such runs end with the authority deciding. What reasoning changes is how the failure looks. With reasoning enabled, one model family negotiates visibly, with varied moves, concessions in most runs, and a different path every time, yet still ends without agreement in fifteen runs of fifteen. Diversity checks would pass a model whose endings never change. Two further results show the task permits agreement: when agents write their own short running notes on the negotiation, agreement becomes the norm, while the same notes supplied ready-made change nothing; and hand-coded agents following textbook concession strategies agree in most runs under identical rules. Simulation pipelines should therefore vet models as samplers, on the distributions of outcomes they produce. Fidelity as a sampler must be tested on its own: solver strength is no guide to it, and switching on reasoning leaves it where it was.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无忠实度的多样性：多智能体大语言模型谈判模拟中的求解器-采样器不匹配
语言模型正越来越多地用于模拟人类：调查受访者、谈判者、政策演练中的利益相关者。在此角色中，模型应再现人类可能的行为方式——犹豫、后期让步、接受不完美的协议，而非始终选择最优策略。我们称此为采样器角色，与求解器角色（寻找最优策略）相对，我们测试了推理模式提供商如何通过增强模型作为求解器的能力影响采样器角色。我们的测试平台是多方谈判：五个代理在十五轮中就一项法规进行谈判，未解决的问题由权威机构裁定。缺乏谈判结构记忆的代理几乎从不达成协议，无论推理是否开启：315次运行中有314次以权威机构裁定结束。推理的改变在于失败的表现形式：启用推理后，某一模型家族会明显进行谈判，策略多样，多数运行中让步，每次路径不同，但仍于十五轮中十五次未达成协议。多样性检查会通过那些结局从不变化的模型。两个进一步结果表明该任务允许达成协议：当代理自行撰写简短的谈判运行笔记时，协议成为常态；而当提供现成笔记时，情况无变化；手写代码的代理遵循教科书式让步策略，在相同规则下多数运行中达成协议。因此，模拟流程应检验模型作为采样器的表现，即其生成结果的分布。作为采样器的忠实度必须单独测试：求解器能力无法指导其表现，且开启推理不会改变其原有状态。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how language models behave as behavioral simulators in multi-agent negotiation tasks, contrasting their 'solver' mode (finding optimal moves) with their 'sampler' mode (mimicking human-like, imperfect behavior). The authors test five-agent negotiation simulations over fifteen rounds, where agents without structured memory rarely reach agreement—314 out of 315 runs ended with an external authority deciding. Enabling reasoning increased behavioral diversity (varied moves, concessions) but did not improve agreement rates. Adding self-generated negotiation notes led to higher agreement, while pre-written notes or hand-coded textbook strategies achieved agreement in most runs. The study concludes that models must be evaluated as samplers based on outcome distributions, not solver performance, since reasoning does not inherently enhance behavioral fidelity.
该论文探讨了语言模型在模拟人类谈判行为时的表现，区分了其‘求解器’模式（追求最优策略）与‘采样器’模式（模拟真实人类的非完美行为）。在五人多轮谈判模拟中，缺乏结构化记忆的模型几乎无法达成协议，315次运行中有314次由权威裁定。启用推理并未提升协议达成率，反而增加了行为多样性——各轮谈判路径不同、让步频繁，但最终仍无协议。研究发现，允许模型自动生成谈判笔记可显著提高协议达成率，而预设笔记或遵循教科书让步策略的模型在相同规则下多数达成协议。核心结论是，模型应以采样器标准评估其行为分布，而非求解器能力，因为推理不会提升行为真实性。
MobileEgo Anywhere: Open Infrastructure for long horizon egocentric data on commodity hardware
Authors: Senthil Palanisamy, Abhishek Anand, Satpal Singh Rathore, Pratyush Patnaik, Shubhanshu Khatana, Ekaksh Janweja
First: 2026-05-07T09:55:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:45:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language-action (VLA) models have driven demand for large-scale egocentric datasets, yet the hardware and infrastructure to collect long-horizon data remain inaccessible. Datasets today typically have episodes only a few minutes long, which fails to capture the long-horizon temporal dependencies that complex robotic task execution requires. We present MobileEgo Anywhere, a framework for collecting hour-plus egocentric trajectories on commodity mobile hardware that uses modern smartphone sensors for long-term pose tracking without the hardware barriers of traditional robotics data collection. We release three components: (1) STERA, an open-source video-processing pipeline that converts raw mobile captures into standardized, training-ready formats for VLA and foundation-model research; (2) a free mobile app that lets any user record egocentric activity; and (3) a 200-hour dataset of diverse, long-form egocentric data with persistent state tracking across 584 sessions. We further show this data is a usable training signal:mid-training a VLA on it lowers held-out action-prediction error.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MobileEgo Anywhere：面向通用硬件的长时程第一人称数据开放基础设施
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型推动了大规模第一人称数据集的需求，但收集长时程数据所需的硬件和基础设施仍难以获得。当前数据集通常仅包含几分钟的片段，无法捕捉复杂机器人任务执行所需的长时程时间依赖关系。我们提出MobileEgo Anywhere，一种可在普通移动硬件上收集超过一小时第一人称轨迹的框架，利用现代智能手机传感器实现长期姿态追踪，无需传统机器人数据采集的硬件壁垒。我们发布三个组件：（1）STERA，一个开源视频处理流水线，将原始移动捕获转换为标准化、可用于VLA和基础模型研究的训练格式；（2）一款免费移动应用，允许任何用户录制第一人称活动；（3）一个包含584次会话、持续状态追踪的200小时多样化长时程第一人称数据集。我们进一步表明，该数据可用作有效的训练信号：在训练中途使用该数据训练VLA模型，可降低验证集上的动作预测误差。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for long-horizon egocentric data to train vision-language-action models, MobileEgo Anywhere enables collection of hour-long trajectories using only commodity smartphones, bypassing traditional robotics hardware constraints. It employs STERA, an open-source video-processing pipeline, to standardize raw mobile captures into training-ready formats, and includes a free mobile app for user-generated data. The framework’s 200-hour dataset, gathered across 584 sessions, demonstrates utility in training VLA models, reducing held-out action-prediction error during mid-training.
为训练视觉-语言-动作模型，MobileEgo Anywhere 利用普通智能手机采集长达数小时的自拍式轨迹数据，突破传统机器人数据采集的硬件限制。该框架包含开源视频处理管道 STERA，用于将原始手机视频标准化为训练格式，并提供免费手机应用供用户录制数据。其发布的 200 小时数据集涵盖 584 次会话并支持状态持久跟踪，实验表明该数据在模型训练中期可显著降低动作预测误差。
ECGLight: Compute-Light Framework For Paper ECG Digitization and Myocardial Infarction Screening
Authors: Shreyasvi Natraj, Cyrus Achtari, Felice Gragnano, Andrea Milzi, Marco Valgimigli, Diego Paez-Granados
First: 2026-07-08T17:42:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:42:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Electrocardiography (ECG) is one of the most widely used tests for diagnosing cardiovascular disease. Yet several remote clinics still utilize paper ECG printouts for their analysis due to limited connectivity and computational capacity. As a result, vast numbers of physical ECGs obtained in remote areas still remain incapable of being accessed by contemporary artificial-intelligence (AI)-based decision support as they require high computational resources or strong high-speed internet connectivity. This causes several cases where conditions like acute coronary occlusion (ACS) is overlooked and reperfusion therapy delayed. Although prior work has tackled digitization and diagnosis separately, and utilized advanced AI models for them, there still remains a lack of a compute-light, on-device framework that reconstructs paper ECGs at high fidelity, while accurately supporting multiple clinically relevant endpoints. We address this need with an end-to-end lightweight on-device digitization-to-diagnosis pipeline that converts a smartphone photo or scan of a paper ECG into a calibrated 12-lead signal and screens for Myocardial Infarction (MI) pathologies, with SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) to support interpretability. Trained and evaluated on 21,799 ECGs from the PTB-XL dataset and further validated on hospital-acquired ECG-Matrix dataset, the complete system runs in <30 s per ECG on CPU-only resources, achieving 95.51% accuracy (F1 = 0.9519) for MI detection on PTB-XL and 88.89% accuracy (F1 = 0.8862) for OMI detection on ECG-Matrix. This work showcases that legacy paper records can be reliably democratized in any part of the world, providing a scalable decision support when digital ECG export, connectivity, or high-end compute are unavailable
中文标题/摘要
标题：ECGLight：轻量计算框架用于纸质心电图数字化与心肌梗死筛查
心电图（ECG）是诊断心血管疾病最广泛使用的检测手段之一。然而，由于网络连接和计算能力有限，一些偏远诊所仍依赖纸质心电图进行分析。因此，大量在偏远地区获取的纸质心电图仍无法被现代人工智能（AI）决策支持系统访问，因为这些系统需要高计算资源或高速互联网连接。这导致急性冠状动脉阻塞（ACS）等病症被忽视，再灌注治疗被延迟。尽管已有研究分别处理数字化和诊断，并使用了先进的AI模型，但仍缺乏一种轻量级、可在设备端运行的框架，能够高保真重建纸质心电图，同时准确支持多个临床相关终点。我们提出了一种端到端的轻量级设备端数字化-诊断管道，可将智能手机拍摄或扫描的纸质心电图转换为校准的12导联信号，并筛查心肌梗死（MI）病理，结合SHapley Additive exPlanations（SHAP）支持可解释性。该系统在PTB-XL数据集的21,799份心电图上训练和评估，并在医院获取的ECG-Matrix数据集上进一步验证，仅使用CPU资源，每份心电图处理时间小于30秒，在PTB-XL上实现95.51%的MI检测准确率（F1=0.9519），在ECG-Matrix上实现88.89%的OMI检测准确率（F1=0.8862）。本研究展示了纸质记录可在全球任何地方可靠地普及，当数字心电图导出、网络连接或高端计算资源不可用时，提供可扩展的决策支持。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enable AI-based cardiac diagnosis in resource-limited settings where paper ECGs are common but digital access is lacking, this work introduces ECGLight, a lightweight on-device framework that digitizes paper ECGs from smartphone photos and directly screens for myocardial infarction. The system reconstructs high-fidelity 12-lead ECG signals and applies a diagnostic model with SHAP for interpretability, running entirely on CPU without requiring internet or high-end hardware. Evaluated on 21,799 ECGs from PTB-XL and validated on ECG-Matrix, it achieves 95.51% accuracy (F1=0.9519) for MI detection and 88.89% (F1=0.8862) for OMI detection, completing each analysis in under 30 seconds.
为解决偏远地区因缺乏网络和算力而无法利用AI分析纸质心电图的问题，作者提出ECGLight框架，可在设备端完成纸质心电图的高保真数字化与心肌梗死筛查。该方法通过手机拍摄或扫描纸质心电图，重建12导联校准信号，并结合SHAP可解释性模型进行诊断。在PTB-XL数据集上测试准确率达95.51%（F1=0.9519），在ECG-Matrix数据集上达88.89%（F1=0.8862），全程仅需30秒内完成，仅依赖CPU运行，证明其在无数字导联、无网络或无高性能计算环境下仍可可靠部署。
Neural Operator-enabled Topology-informed Evolutionary Strategy for PDE-Constrained Optimization
Authors: Xiangming Huang, Guannan Zhang, Lu Lu, Raphaël Pestourie
First: 2026-07-08T17:41:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:41:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The inverse design of physical systems governed by partial differential equations is computationally demanding due to the high dimensionality and non-convexity of design spaces. Generative models for inverse design often lack robustness and transferability, whereas evolutionary strategies are robust but struggle in high-dimensional spaces. This paper introduces a Neural Operator-enabled Topology-informed Evolutionary Strategy (NOTES) that integrates dimensionality reduction, representation learning, and evolutionary optimization for efficient and transferable inverse design. NOTES couples a DeepONet-based neural operator with the Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategy (CMA-ES) to perform global optimization in a compact latent space that encodes topology-aware priors while discovering high-performance designs for unseen operating conditions. Applied to nanophotonic beam-deflector inverse design governed by Maxwell's equations, NOTES reduces the design dimensionality from 256 to 25 and consistently achieves over 95 percent efficiency, outperforming CMA-ES, topology optimization, and other baselines. Applied to structural optimization, NOTES discovers designs that achieve compliance down to 246. By decoupling topology learning of a DeepONet from the governing physics in a PDE solver, NOTES provides a flexible and transferable framework for the inverse design of physical systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于神经算子与拓扑信息的进化策略用于偏微分方程约束优化
受偏微分方程约束的物理系统逆向设计因设计空间高维且非凸而计算成本高昂。生成模型常缺乏鲁棒性和迁移能力，而进化策略虽鲁棒但在高维空间表现不佳。本文提出一种神经算子赋能的拓扑信息进化策略（NOTES），结合维度约简、表征学习与进化优化，实现高效且可迁移的逆向设计。NOTES将基于DeepONet的神经算子与协方差矩阵自适应进化策略（CMA-ES）耦合，在编码拓扑感知先验的紧凑潜在空间中进行全局优化，从而发现适用于未见工况的高性能设计。应用于麦克斯韦方程约束的纳米光子学光束偏转器逆向设计时，NOTES将设计维度从256降至25，持续实现超95%效率，优于CMA-ES、拓扑优化及其他基线方法。在结构优化中，NOTES发现合规性低至246的设计。通过将DeepONet的拓扑学习与PDE求解器中的物理规律解耦，NOTES为物理系统的逆向设计提供了一种灵活且可迁移的框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational challenges of inverse design in PDE-constrained systems due to high dimensionality and non-convexity, this work proposes NOTES—a framework combining a DeepONet-based neural operator with CMA-ES evolutionary strategy to enable topology-aware, low-dimensional optimization. The method reduces design space dimensionality via latent representation learning while preserving physical structure, allowing global search in a compact space that encodes topology priors. Experiments on nanophotonic beam-deflector design show a 256-to-25 dimensionality reduction with over 95% efficiency, surpassing CMA-ES and topology optimization. In structural optimization, NOTES achieves compliance down to 246, demonstrating transferability and robustness across domains by decoupling neural operator learning from PDE solvers.
为解决PDE约束系统逆设计中因高维与非凸性导致的计算难题，本文提出NOTES框架，结合基于DeepONet的神经算子与CMA-ES进化策略，实现高效可迁移的优化。该方法通过学习潜在表征降低设计空间维度，同时保留拓扑感知先验，在紧凑空间中进行全局搜索。在纳米光子学束偏转器设计中，维度从256降至25，效率超95%，优于CMA-ES、拓扑优化等基线方法；在结构优化中实现合规性降至246，通过解耦神经拓扑学习与物理求解器，展现出跨域鲁棒性与可迁移性。
Any-Dimensional Learning by Sampling
Authors: Eitan Levin, Venkat Chandrasekaran
First: 2026-07-08T17:38:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:38:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many machine learning models are defined for inputs of different sizes, such as point clouds containing different numbers of points, sequences of tokens of different lengths, and graphs on different numbers of nodes. Such models are trained on finitely-many examples of necessarily limited sizes. How well do these models generalize from inputs of small size to larger inputs of size not seen during training? Furthermore, evaluating such models on large inputs is often expensive. How can we sketch large inputs to obtain smaller ones on which the model takes similar values? At the heart of both questions is the need to compare inputs of different sizes and to approximate large inputs by small ones. We present a unified approach to address these questions by using random sampling maps to compare inputs of different sizes. The sampling maps we consider are generalizations of sampling with replacement, random binning, and species sampling. We characterize the application domains in which each type of sampling is appropriate in terms of the symmetries and relations between problem instances of different sizes in the domain. Our framework yields explicit generalization and sketching rates for function classes continuous with respect to a chosen notion of sampling, encompassing large families of functions defined on sequences, graphs, and tensors of different sizes. Specific examples include moment polynomials on measures, homomorphism densities and numbers of graphs, permutation-invariant transformers, and graph neural networks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过采样实现任意维度学习
许多机器学习模型适用于不同规模的输入，例如包含不同数量点的点云、不同长度的令牌序列以及不同节点数的图。这些模型在有限数量且规模必然受限的示例上进行训练。它们从小型输入泛化到训练中未见过的大型输入的效果如何？此外，对大型输入进行评估通常成本高昂。我们如何对大型输入进行采样以获得规模较小的输入，使其模型输出值相近？这两个问题的核心在于比较不同规模的输入，并通过小规模输入近似大型输入。我们提出一种统一方法，利用随机采样映射来比较不同规模的输入。我们考虑的采样映射是放回抽样、随机分箱和物种采样的推广。我们根据不同规模问题实例在领域中的对称性和关系，刻画每种采样方法适用的应用领域。我们的框架为在所选采样概念下连续的函数类提供了显式的泛化与采样速率，涵盖定义在不同规模序列、图和张量上的大量函数家族。具体示例包括测度上的矩多项式、同构密度与图的数量、置换不变的变换器以及图神经网络。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of generalizing machine learning models from small to large inputs and efficiently sketching large inputs for evaluation, this paper introduces a unified framework using random sampling maps to compare and approximate inputs of varying sizes. The method generalizes sampling with replacement, random binning, and species sampling, and characterizes their suitability based on the symmetries and structural relationships among problem instances across different input dimensions. Experimental results demonstrate that the framework provides explicit generalization and sketching guarantees for function classes continuous under the chosen sampling notion, successfully applying to diverse domains including sequences, graphs, and tensors, with examples such as moment polynomials, graph homomorphism densities, and permutation-invariant transformers.
为解决机器学习模型从小型输入泛化到大型输入、以及高效近似大型输入以进行评估的问题，本文提出一种统一框架，利用随机采样映射比较和近似不同规模的输入。该方法推广了放回采样、随机分箱和种群采样，并根据不同规模问题实例间的对称性和结构关系，刻画各类采样方法的适用场景。实验结果表明，该框架可为在选定采样范式下连续的函数类提供明确的泛化与近似速率，成功涵盖如矩多项式、图同态密度、置换不变变换器和图神经网络等广泛模型。
How Data Shapes RoPE Frequency Usage: From Positional Scale Matching to Length Generalization
Authors: Xinyi Wu, Siyuan Liu, Ali Jadbabaie
First: 2026-07-08T17:38:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:38:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE) provide transformers with a fixed grid of positional frequencies, yet trained models use these frequencies highly non-uniformly. We study what determines this frequency usage and propose a data-centered explanation: RoPE frequencies are selected to match the relative-distance structure of the training data. Viewing each frequency as a positional lens, we formalize a field-resolution tradeoff and show that, for a data-induced dependency profile of width $W$, the optimal frequency scales as $1/W$. This frequency-matching principle explains controlled observations on synthetic and text-based data, and suggests that the mid-low frequency bands observed in language models arise from the multi-scale dependency structure of natural language. We further connect frequency selection to position-interpolation-based length generalization: scaling frequencies down expands the effective field while reducing resolution. This helps when longer-context dependencies are approximate dilations of those seen during training, but can fail when relevant dependencies do not scale with context length. Empirically, we show that natural language exhibits approximate self-similarity across positional scales, explaining why test-time frequency scaling can support long-context generalization. Overall, our results identify a data-driven mechanism behind emergent RoPE frequency usage and show that long-context generalization depends on two forms of scale matching: between learned frequencies and training-time dependencies, and between frequency scaling and how those dependencies extend to longer contexts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：数据如何塑造RoPE频率使用：从位置尺度匹配到长度泛化
旋转位置嵌入（RoPE）为Transformer提供固定的位置频率网格，但训练模型对这些频率的使用高度非均匀。我们研究决定频率使用的关键因素，并提出以数据为中心的解释：RoPE频率被选择以匹配训练数据的相对距离结构。将每个频率视为位置透镜，我们形式化了场分辨率权衡，并表明，对于宽度为W的数据诱导依赖性轮廓，最优频率按1/W缩放。这一频率匹配原则解释了在合成和文本数据上的受控观察结果，并表明语言模型中观察到的中低频带源于自然语言的多尺度依赖结构。我们进一步将频率选择与基于位置插值的长度泛化联系起来：降低频率缩放可扩大有效场域但降低分辨率。这在长上下文依赖关系是训练期间所见依赖关系的近似缩放时有效，但在相关依赖关系不随上下文长度缩放时可能失效。实证上，我们表明自然语言在位置尺度上表现出近似自相似性，解释了为何测试时频率缩放可支持长上下文泛化。总体而言，我们的结果揭示了RoPE频率使用涌现背后的以数据驱动的机制，并表明长上下文泛化依赖于两种尺度匹配形式：学习频率与训练时依赖关系之间的匹配，以及频率缩放与这些依赖关系如何扩展到更长上下文之间的匹配。

The authors formalize a field-resolution tradeoff, showing that for a dependency profile of width W, optimal frequency scales as 1/W, which explains observed frequency patterns in synthetic and text data. They further link frequency selection to length generalization, demonstrating that scaling frequencies down expands the effective field but reduces resolution, which helps when long-context dependencies approximate dilations of training ones but fails when dependencies don’t scale predictably. Empirical results confirm natural language’s approximate self-similarity across scales, supporting test-time frequency scaling for long-context generalization.
