SkillCenter: A Large-Scale Source-Grounded Skill Library for Autonomous AI Agents
Authors: Tianming Sha, Yue Zhao, Lichao Sun, Yushun Dong
First: 2026-07-08T17:34:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:34:28+00:00
Comments: 44 pages, 5 figures. Code: https://github.com/LabRAI/SkillCenter ; Data: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Tommysha/skillcenter-bundles
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Autonomous AI agents can execute complex tasks with limited human review, yet they often lack the grounded operational knowledge to make their outputs not just executable but correct, secure, and maintainable. We introduce SkillCenter, to our knowledge the largest open skill library for agents by total count: 216,938 structured skills across 24 domain bundles. A SkillGate-filtered pipeline contributes 114,565 source-grounded skills from peer-reviewed journals, ArXiv, and over 24,000 technical sources, integrated with 102,373 community skills from GitHub and the ClawHub marketplace. We present the end-to-end framework that builds the pipeline subset: multi-source acquisition, an LLM-based quality gate (SkillGate), template-driven generation, iterative source-grounding, and quality-controlled publishing. Source grounding is a traceability guarantee: each retained claim maps to an exact quotation in its source. All skills ship as offline-searchable SQLite FTS5 bundles.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SkillCenter：面向自主AI代理的大型源基础技能库
自主AI代理可在有限人工审核下执行复杂任务，但往往缺乏使输出不仅可执行，而且正确、安全和可维护的具身操作知识。我们引入SkillCenter，据我们所知这是规模最大的开放技能库：涵盖24个领域包，共计216,938个结构化技能。SkillGate过滤管道从同行评审期刊、ArXiv及超过24,000个技术来源中贡献了114,565个源基础技能，并整合了来自GitHub和ClawHub市场的102,373个社区技能。我们展示了构建该管道子集的端到端框架：多源采集、基于LLM的质量门（SkillGate）、模板驱动生成、迭代源基础化及质量控制发布。源基础化提供可追溯性保证：每个保留声明均精确映射至其源中的引文。所有技能均以可离线搜索的SQLite FTS5包形式提供。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for autonomous AI agents to execute tasks correctly and securely with minimal human oversight, the authors introduce SkillCenter, the largest open skill library with 216,938 structured skills across 24 domains. The method involves a multi-source acquisition pipeline that combines peer-reviewed literature and technical sources with community-contributed skills from GitHub and ClawHub, filtered by an LLM-based quality gate called SkillGate. The pipeline ensures source grounding—each skill’s claim is traceable to an exact source quotation—and outputs skills as offline-searchable SQLite FTS5 bundles. Experimental results show the pipeline successfully extracts and validates 114,565 source-grounded skills while maintaining high quality and traceability across diverse domains.

Scaling Mixture-of-Experts Video Pretraining for Embodied Intelligence
Authors: Shuailei Ma, Jiaqi Liao, Xinyang Wang, Jingjing Wang, Chaoran Feng, Zijing Hu, Chong Bao, Zichen Xi, Yuqi Gan, Weisen Wang, Yanhong Zeng, Qin Zhao, Zifan Shi, Wei Wu, Hao Ouyang, Qiuyu Wang, Shangzhan Zhang, Jiahao Shao, Yipengjing Sun, Liangxiao Hu, Lunke Pan, Nan Xue, Kecheng Zheng, Yinghao Xu, Xing Zhu, Yujun Shen, Ka Leong Cheng
First: 2026-07-08T17:33:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:33:40+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://technology.robbyant.com/lingbot-video
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite the recent promise in robot control, video generative models suffer from a domain mismatch due to their primary focus on content creation. For example, their design inherently prioritizes visual fidelity and creativity over computational efficiency and physical realism. In this work, we present LingBot-Video, a DiT-based video pretraining paradigm specifically tailored for embodied intelligence. From the architecture perspective, we adopt the Mixture-of-Experts (MoE), instead of dense, framework to achieve a better trade-off between modeling capacity and inference efficiency, and manage to scale it up from scratch. From the data perspective, we construct a data profiling engine that augments standard internet videos with extensive robot-oriented footage, encompassing manipulation, navigation, and egocentric perspectives, to equip the base model with an intrinsic understanding of actions and world dynamics. From the training perspective, we develop a multi-dimensional reward system to enforce the alignment regarding physical rationality and task completion, going beyond standard criteria such as aesthetics, prompt-following, and motion consistency. Comprehensive evaluations validate its performance and efficiency as a video foundation model. We contribute LingBot-Video as the inaugural large-scale, open-source MoE video foundation model to the community, in a pioneering effort to bridge digital creativity and physical actuation.

Max Out GRPO Signal: Adaptive Trace Prefix Control for Hard Reasoning Problems
Authors: Vladislav Beliaev
First: 2026-07-08T17:32:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:32:58+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 5 figures, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) stalls on a model's hardest problems: when no rollout in a group succeeds, the group-relative advantages vanish and the problem contributes no gradient, wasting the frontier examples we most want to learn from. Prepending a correct prefix of a reference solution raises the success rate, making prefix length a continuous knob on difficulty. Concurrent methods set the knob once; AdaPrefix-GRPO turns it into a feedback controller: throughout training it adjusts how much of the solution each problem gets, holding its success rate near 50%, where GRPO's gradient signal is largest, then withdraws the assistance entirely, so the deployed model solves problems unaided. On hard math, at matched training FLOPs, it more than doubles GRPO's accuracy on held-out problems from the training distribution for a 0.6B model (2.1x), with 1.6x on Qwen3-1.7B and 1.7x on AIME, while roughly halving trace length. The method is implemented in data preparation plus a loss mask on prefix tokens; the trainer is otherwise stock. The smaller the model, the larger the gain.
中文标题/摘要
标题：最大化GRPO信号：面向困难推理问题的自适应前缀控制
组相对策略优化（GRPO）在模型最难的问题上会停滞：当组内无任何 rollout 成功时，组相对优势消失，该问题无法贡献梯度，浪费了我们最希望学习的前沿示例。在参考解的前缀前添加正确部分可提升成功率，使前缀长度成为连续的难度调节器。现有方法仅一次性设置该调节器；AdaPrefix-GRPO将其转化为反馈控制器：训练过程中动态调整每个问题获得的解的前缀长度，使其成功率维持在约50%（此时GRPO梯度信号最大），随后完全撤除辅助，使部署模型无需帮助即可解决问题。在数学难题上，当训练FLOPs相同时，对于0.6B模型，其在训练分布外问题上的准确率较GRPO提升超两倍（2.1倍），在Qwen3-1.7B上提升1.6倍，在AIME上提升1.7倍，同时轨迹长度约减半。该方法通过数据预处理和前缀标记的损失掩码实现，训练器其余部分保持原样。模型越小，收益越大。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the issue that GRPO fails on hard problems due to vanishing gradient signals when no rollouts succeed, this work introduces AdaPrefix-GRPO, a feedback-controlled method that dynamically adjusts the length of a correct solution prefix prepended to each problem during training. By maintaining a 50% success rate—where GRPO’s gradient signal is strongest—and then gradually removing the prefix, the method ensures the model learns to solve problems independently. Experimental results show that at matched FLOPs, AdaPrefix-GRPO more than doubles GRPO’s accuracy on held-out hard math problems for a 0.6B model (2.1x), with 1.6x and 1.7x gains on larger models, while roughly halving trace length.
为了解决GRPO在最难问题上因无成功 rollout 导致梯度信号消失的问题，该研究提出AdaPrefix-GRPO方法，通过动态调整每个问题前缀的正确解长度来控制难度。该方法将前缀长度视为连续控制变量，训练中维持50%成功率以最大化梯度信号，随后逐步移除辅助，确保最终模型独立解决问题。实验表明，在相同计算量下，AdaPrefix-GRPO使0.6B模型在硬数学问题上的准确率提升超2倍（2.1倍），在Qwen3-1.7B和AIME上分别提升1.6倍和1.7倍，同时将轨迹长度大致减半。
Trexplorer Super: Topologically Correct Centerline Tree Tracking of Tubular Objects in CT Volumes
Authors: Roman Naeem, David Hagerman, Jennifer Alvén, Lennart Svensson, Fredrik Kahl
Venue: MICCAI 2025
First: 2025-07-15T00:51:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:30:51+00:00
Comments: Submitted Version. Accepted at MICCAI 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Tubular tree structures, such as blood vessels and airways, are essential in human anatomy and accurately tracking them while preserving their topology is crucial for various downstream tasks. Trexplorer is a recurrent model designed for centerline tracking in 3D medical images but it struggles with predicting duplicate branches and terminating tracking prematurely. To address these issues, we present Trexplorer Super, an enhanced version that notably improves performance through novel advancements. However, evaluating centerline tracking models is challenging due to the lack of public datasets. To enable thorough evaluation, we develop three centerline datasets, one synthetic and two real, each with increasing difficulty. Using these datasets, we conduct a comprehensive evaluation of existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) models and compare them with our approach. Trexplorer Super outperforms previous SOTA models on every dataset. Our results also highlight that strong performance on synthetic data does not necessarily translate to real datasets. The code and datasets are available at https://github.com/RomStriker/Trexplorer-Super.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Trexplorer Super：CT体积中管状物体拓扑正确的中心线树状追踪
血管和气道等管状树状结构在人体解剖学中至关重要，准确追踪其中心线并保持拓扑结构完整性对多种下游任务至关重要。Trexplorer 是一种用于三维医学图像中心线追踪的循环模型，但其在预测重复分支和避免过早终止追踪方面存在不足。为解决这些问题，我们提出了 Trexplorer Super，通过多项创新显著提升了性能。然而，由于缺乏公开数据集，中心线追踪模型的评估颇具挑战。为实现全面评估，我们构建了三个中心线数据集（一个合成、两个真实），难度逐级递增。利用这些数据集，我们对现有最先进（SOTA）模型进行了全面评估，并与我们的方法进行比较。Trexplorer Super 在所有数据集上均优于先前的 SOTA 模型。我们的结果还表明，合成数据上的强性能并不必然转化为真实数据集上的优异表现。代码与数据集可在 https://github.com/RomStriker/Trexplorer-Super 获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate and topologically correct tracking of tubular tree structures like blood vessels in 3D medical images, Trexplorer Super improves upon the original Trexplorer model by addressing its shortcomings in branch duplication and premature termination. The method introduces novel enhancements to the recurrent architecture to better preserve topology during centerline tracking. Evaluated on three datasets—synthetic and two real-world ones with increasing complexity—Trexplorer Super consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art models across all benchmarks, while also revealing that synthetic dataset performance does not reliably predict real-world effectiveness.
为了解决医学图像中血管等管状树状结构拓扑正确追踪的难题，Trexplorer Super在原模型基础上改进了分支重复预测和提前终止的问题，通过引入新机制提升拓扑连续性。该方法在三个难度递增的数据集（一个合成、两个真实）上进行全面评估，结果表明其在所有数据集上均优于当前最先进模型，同时发现合成数据上的高表现并不保证在真实数据上的有效性。
MedPMC: A Systematic Framework for Scaling High-Fidelity Medical Multimodal Data for Foundation Models
Authors: Hyunjae Kim, Dain Kim, Pan Xiao, Serina S. Applebaum, Younjoon Chung, Xuguang Ai, Yu Yin, Roy Jiang, Yuexi Du, Yawen Wei, Yiming Kong, Tuo Guo, Zhiyuan Cao, Mengmeng Du, Yuelei Fu, Yan Hu, Rui Shi, Gui Yang, Kevin W. Jin, Yuntian Liu, Yuxuan Tian, Jonathan Marquez, Zhen Chen, Sheng Zhang, Hoifung Poon, Hua Xu, Jaewoo Kang, Qingyu Chen
First: 2026-07-08T17:26:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:26:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Medicine is inherently multimodal, requiring clinicians to synthesize information across diverse data streams. Yet the development of multimodal foundation models is constrained by limited access to large-scale, high-quality clinical data. Although PubMed Central (PMC) offers a complementary source of expert-authored image-text data, existing PMC-derived resources remain limited in fidelity, reproducibility, and clinical validation. We introduce MedPMC, an automated, continuously updatable framework that transforms permissively licensed literature into high-fidelity infrastructure for medical multimodal models. Applied to 6.1 million PMC articles, MedPMC curated 11 million medical image-text pairs. Component evaluations showed strong performance for initial screening (F1 = 93.2), multi-panel figure detection (F1 = 96.5), figure separation (mAP = 89.8), caption separation and alignment (F1 = 81.4; ROUGE-L = 85.3), and medical figure classification (F1 = 96.5). Manual review by five annotators, three with medical training, found 95.3% of MedPMC images medically relevant, versus 19.7% in a prior PMC-derived dataset. Across 26 benchmarks spanning 11 specialties, a MedPMC-trained CLIP-style model improved average zero-shot AUC by 7.1 percentage points over the strongest architecture-matched biomedical CLIP baseline despite using fewer than half as many image-text pairs. As the vision encoder in a multimodal large language model, it improved medical visual question-answering by 1.9 and 16.9 percentage points across two benchmarks. In 10,524 Yale New Haven Health System dermatology photographs, it improved morphology-to-image retrieval Recall@5 by 11.7 percentage points. These findings show that high-fidelity literature curation strengthens medical multimodal foundation models across benchmark and clinical settings. We publicly release the framework, corpus, benchmarks, and pretrained models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MedPMC：用于扩展高保真医学多模态数据以支持基础模型的系统框架
医学本质上是多模态的，要求临床医生综合多种数据流的信息。然而，多模态基础模型的发展受限于大规模高质量临床数据的获取。尽管PubMed Central（PMC）提供了专家撰写的图像-文本数据的补充来源，但现有的PMC衍生资源在保真度、可重复性和临床验证方面仍显不足。我们提出了MedPMC，一个自动化且持续更新的框架，将宽松授权的文献转化为高保真医学多模态模型的基础设施。应用于610万篇PMC文章，MedPMC整理出1100万对医学图像-文本数据。组件评估显示，其在初步筛选（F1=93.2）、多面板图检测（F1=96.5）、图分割（mAP=89.8）、图注分割与对齐（F1=81.4；ROUGE-L=85.3）及医学图分类（F1=96.5）方面表现优异。五名标注员（其中三名具有医学背景）人工审核发现，MedPMC图像中95.3%具有医学相关性，而此前PMC衍生数据集仅为19.7%。在涵盖11个专科的26项基准测试中，基于MedPMC训练的CLIP风格模型，尽管使用图像-文本对数量不到最强生物医学CLIP基线的一半，仍使平均零样本AUC提升7.1个百分点。作为多模态大语言模型中的视觉编码器，其在两项基准测试中分别提升了医学视觉问答性能1.9和16.9个百分点。在耶鲁纽黑文健康系统10,524张皮肤病照片中，其将形态到图像检索的Recall@5提升了11.7个百分点。这些结果表明，高保真文献整理可增强医学多模态基础模型在基准测试和临床场景中的表现。我们公开发布该框架、语料库、基准测试及预训练模型。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to overcome data scarcity in medical multimodal learning, MedPMC automates the extraction and curation of high-fidelity image-text pairs from PubMed Central articles, producing 11 million clinically relevant pairs from 6.1 million papers. Its pipeline achieves strong component-level performance: 93.2 F1 for initial screening, 96.5 F1 for multi-panel figure detection, 89.8 mAP for figure separation, and 81.4 F1 with 85.3 ROUGE-L for caption alignment. Manual validation confirmed 95.3% medical relevance versus 19.7% in prior datasets. When trained on MedPMC, a CLIP-style model outperformed the best biomedical baseline by 7.1 percentage points in zero-shot AUC across 26 benchmarks, and boosted medical visual question-answering by 1.9–16.9 percentage points, while improving dermatology image retrieval by 11.7 percentage points.
为解决医学多模态基础模型训练所需高质量数据稀缺的问题，MedPMC 自动化从610万篇PMC文献中提取并整理出1100万组医学图像-文本对。其流程涵盖初筛、多图检测、图像分割、标题对齐与分类，各项指标表现优异，如F1值达93.2–96.5，mAP为89.8。人工审核显示其图像医学相关性高达95.3%，远超前人数据集。用该数据训练的CLIP风格模型在26项医学基准测试中零样本AUC提升7.1个百分点（数据量仅为基线一半），并在医学视觉问答与皮肤科图像检索中分别提升1.9–16.9个百分点，验证了高保真文献数据对模型性能的显著增强作用。
PeTeR: Post-Training Robustification of Probabilistic Circuits
Authors: Adrian Ciotinga, Yeming Dai, YooJung Choi
First: 2026-07-08T17:25:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:25:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Probabilistic circuits (PCs) can model complex joint distributions while supporting exact and efficient computation of many inference queries. However, standard likelihood-based PC learning is vulnerable to overfitting and fragile generalization when confronted with data noise, small sample sizes, or distribution shifts. This can be mitigated using distributionally-robust optimization which consider worst-case distributions within a Wasserstein ball of the empirical distribution, but current methods are limited to training a model from scratch in this framework. Instead, we propose PeTeR: a novel, data-free post-training framework designed to robustify pre-trained PCs against distribution shifts without retraining from scratch. Empirical evaluations across multiple density estimation benchmarks demonstrate that PeTeR effectively robustifies baseline models against both random and adversarial perturbations, achieving competitive or superior performance to data-dependent robust learning baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PeTeR：概率电路的后训练鲁棒化
概率电路（PCs）能够建模复杂的联合分布，同时支持对多种推理查询进行精确且高效的计算。然而，标准的基于似然的PC学习易受过拟合影响，在面对数据噪声、样本量小或分布偏移时泛化能力脆弱。这可通过分布鲁棒优化缓解，该方法考虑经验分布的Wasserstein球内的最坏情况分布，但现有方法仅限于在此框架下从头训练模型。为此，我们提出PeTeR：一种新颖的无数据后训练框架，旨在无需从头训练的情况下，增强预训练PC对分布偏移的鲁棒性。在多个密度估计基准上的实证评估表明，PeTeR能有效增强基线模型对随机和对抗性扰动的鲁棒性，其性能可与依赖数据的鲁棒学习基线相媲美甚至更优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of standard probabilistic circuit learning to overfitting and poor generalization under distribution shifts, the authors propose PeTeR, a data-free post-training method that enhances pre-trained circuits' robustness without retraining. PeTeR applies distributionally-robust optimization principles by optimizing against worst-case distributions within a Wasserstein ball around the empirical distribution, but does so incrementally after initial training. Experimental results across density estimation benchmarks show PeTeR significantly improves robustness against both random and adversarial perturbations, often matching or exceeding the performance of data-dependent robust learning baselines.
为了解决标准概率电路学习在数据噪声、样本量小或分布偏移下易过拟合、泛化能力差的问题，作者提出PeTeR，一种无需重新训练的后训练框架，用于增强预训练概率电路对分布偏移的鲁棒性。该方法在不重新训练的前提下，通过在经验分布的Wasserstein球内优化最坏情况分布，实现分布鲁棒优化。实验表明，PeTeR在多个密度估计基准上能有效提升对随机和对抗性扰动的鲁棒性，性能常与或优于依赖数据的鲁棒学习基线方法。
CEVAR: Centerline Embedding Extraction for Endovascular Aneurysm Repair
Authors: Roman Naeem, Timo Niiniskorpi, Charlotte Sandström, Naman Desai, Anders Jeppsson, Ida Häggström, Fredrik Kahl, Håkan Roos, Jennifer Alvén
First: 2026-06-14T08:18:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:25:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-term mortality rates after endovascular aneurysm repair (EVAR) remain elevated due to post-EVAR rupture caused by loss of seal in stent graft sealing zones. Structured CT review using centerline measurements improves detection, but current workflows require manual centerline editing and expert operators. We propose a transformer framework for automated, protocol-driven sealing zone assessment that combines 3D centerline tracking with embedding-based geometric prediction. Two state-of-the-art image-to-graph models are evaluated for aorto-iliac centerline extraction from follow-up CT and for measurement of stent position, vessel diameters, and seal lengths according to EVAR4C protocol. Across the full test set and a challenging no-contrast subset, the proposed fully automatic method outperforms the commercial semi-automatic workflow.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CEVAR：用于血管内动脉瘤修复的中心线嵌入提取
由于支架移植物密封区密封失效导致的术后破裂，血管内动脉瘤修复（EVAR）后的长期死亡率仍居高不下。使用中心线测量的结构化CT检查可提高检测能力，但当前工作流程需人工编辑中心线并依赖专家操作。我们提出一种基于变换器的框架，用于自动化、协议驱动的密封区评估，结合3D中心线追踪与基于嵌入的几何预测。评估了两种最先进的图像到图模型，用于从随访CT中提取腹主动脉-髂动脉中心线，并根据EVAR4C协议测量支架位置、血管直径和密封长度。在完整测试集和具有挑战性的无对比剂子集上，所提出的全自动方法优于商业半自动工作流程。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce post-EVAR rupture risk through improved sealing zone assessment, this study proposes a transformer-based framework that automates centerline extraction and geometric measurement using 3D tracking and embedding prediction. Evaluated against a commercial semi-automatic system, the method leverages two state-of-the-art image-to-graph models to extract aorto-iliac centerlines and measure stent position, vessel diameters, and seal lengths per EVAR4C protocol. Results show the fully automatic approach outperforms the commercial tool across both full and no-contrast test sets, demonstrating robustness and accuracy in clinical follow-up CT analysis.
为降低术后破裂风险，该研究提出一种基于Transformer的自动化框架，结合3D中心线追踪与嵌入式几何预测，实现对EVAR术后密封区的自动评估。该方法采用两种前沿图像到图模型，从随访CT中提取髂动脉中心线，并按EVAR4C协议测量支架位置、血管直径和密封长度。实验表明，该全自动方法在完整数据集及无对比剂子集上均优于商用半自动系统，展现出在临床CT分析中的鲁棒性和准确性。
Allo{SR}$^2$: Rectifying One-Step Super-Resolution to Stay Real via Allomorphic Generative Flows
Authors: Zihan Wang, Xudong Huang, Junbo Qiao, Wei Li, Jie Hu, Xinghao Chen, Shaohui Lin
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-04-21T08:44:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:25:05+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-world image super-resolution (Real-SR) has been revolutionized by leveraging the powerful generative priors from Diffusion Models (DMs) and Flow Matching (FM). However, existing one-step methods typically replace Gaussian noise with degraded low-resolution (LR) latents at initialization, introducing a substantial distribution shift that further leads to trajectory deviation and prior collapse under extreme acceleration. To overcome these limitations, we propose Allo{SR}$^2$, a novel FM-based framework that rectifies one-step SR flows via allomorphic generative flows to maintain high-fidelity generative realism. Specifically, we utilize SNR-Guided Trajectory Initialization to identify a statistically aligned intermediate state along the pre-trained path to integrate LR representations into the generative flow. To ensure a stable, low-curvature path for one-step inference, we propose Flow-Anchored Trajectory Consistency (FATC), which explicitly regularizes the velocity field of the underlying probability flow. Furthermore, we develop Allomorphic Trajectory Matching (ATM), a self-adversarial distillation strategy that jointly models the SR flow and the generative flow within a unified velocity field, enabling one-step Real-SR while preserving the generative prior. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world benchmarks demonstrate that Allo{SR}$^2$ achieves state-of-the-art performance in one-step Real-SR, offering a superior balance between fidelity and realism while maintaining extreme efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Allo{SR}$^2$: 通过同形生成流修正一步超分辨率以保持真实感
现实世界图像超分辨率（Real-SR）因利用扩散模型（DMs）和流匹配（FM）的强大生成先验而得到革新。然而，现有一步法通常在初始化时用降质的低分辨率（LR）潜在表示替代高斯噪声，引入显著分布偏移，进一步导致在极端加速下轨迹偏离和先验崩溃。为克服这些限制，我们提出Allo{SR}$^2$，一种基于FM的新框架，通过同形生成流修正一步SR流以保持高保真生成真实感。具体而言，我们采用SNR引导轨迹初始化，沿预训练路径识别统计对齐的中间状态，将LR表示整合进生成流。为确保一步推理的稳定低曲率路径，我们提出流锚定轨迹一致性（FATC），显式正则化底层概率流的速度场。此外，我们开发同形轨迹匹配（ATM），一种自对抗蒸馏策略，联合建模SR流与生成流于统一速度场内，实现一步Real-SR并保留生成先验。在合成与真实世界基准上的大量实验表明，Allo{SR}$^2$在一步Real-SR中达到最先进性能，提供保真度与真实感间的优越平衡，同时保持极高效。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the distribution shift and prior collapse in one-step super-resolution methods that use degraded LR latents as initialization, Allo{SR}$^2$ introduces a novel Flow Matching framework that rectifies trajectory deviation via allomorphic generative flows. It employs SNR-Guided Trajectory Initialization to align LR representations with pre-trained paths, FATC to enforce low-curvature velocity fields for stable inference, and ATM to unify SR and generative flows through self-adversarial distillation. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show Allo{SR}$^2$ achieves state-of-the-art one-step Real-SR performance, balancing high fidelity and realism while maintaining computational efficiency.
为解决现有一步超分辨率方法因使用退化低分辨率潜变量初始化导致的分布偏移与先验崩溃问题，Allo{SR}$^2$ 提出一种基于流匹配的框架，通过同形生成流校正轨迹偏差。该方法采用信噪比引导轨迹初始化对齐LR表示与预训练路径，通过流锚定轨迹一致性稳定速度场，并利用同形轨迹匹配策略在统一速度场中联合建模超分与生成流。实验表明，Allo{SR}$^2$ 在合成与真实数据集上实现了一步超分的最先进性能，在保真度与真实感之间取得优越平衡，同时保持极高的效率。
Does Bielik Know What It Doesn't Know? Activation Dispersion Separates Entity Familiarity from Factual Reliability Across Model Scale
Authors: Grzegorz Brzezinka
First: 2026-07-08T17:24:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:24:43+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 6 figures and 7 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models hallucinate most about entities they have never seen. We ask whether a model's activations betray entity familiarity before a single answer token is generated, and whether that signal predicts the factual reliability of the answers. On four Polish Bielik models (1.5B-11B parameters), we probe four entity domains (athletes, cities, writers, musicians), each with 42 well-known, 42 obscure-but-real, and 42 fabricated entities addressed by a one-sentence question (504 prompts per model). Two unsupervised, single-forward-pass dispersion measures over post-SwiGLU MLP activations, inverse participation ratio and spectral entropy, separate known from fabricated entities at AUROC 0.95-1.00 across all domains and scales; a supervised linear probe reaches 0.99-1.00. Both clear selection-aware permutation floors of about 0.70-0.74 (empirical p<=1e-3), survive held-out layer selection (0.93-0.99), and persist on real names (known vs. obscure-but-real: 0.96-1.00). The signal transfers across entity types (mean off-diagonal AUROC 0.92-0.99); a matched-template counterfactual shows the only large drops are template-caused, not entity-type effects, and the signal is diffuse across heads. This representational signal is already at ceiling at 1.5B, whereas behavioral factual reliability scales sharply: 0, 2, 10, and 19 of 42 known athletes are answered fully correctly by the 1.5B, 4.5B, 7B, and 11B models under a strict judge. Within known entities, separating correct from hallucinated answers is much harder (probe 0.93; dispersion no better than a first-token-entropy baseline). A five-sample semantic-entropy baseline reaches only 0.71-0.83 at 5x the inference cost. Despite this internal awareness, the models almost never abstain: an audit of 2,520 answers finds 2 refusals and 1 hedge. Entity familiarity and factual reliability are distinct phenomena on different scaling curves.
中文标题/摘要
标题：比埃尔克模型是否知晓其认知边界？激活分散性可区分实体熟悉度与事实可靠性，且随模型规模变化
大型语言模型对从未见过的实体最易产生幻觉。我们探究模型在生成首个答案标记前，其激活是否暴露了对实体的熟悉度，以及该信号能否预测答案的事实可靠性。在四个波兰比埃尔克模型（1.5B-11B参数）上，我们针对四个实体领域（运动员、城市、作家、音乐家），每个领域包含42个知名实体、42个真实但生僻实体及42个虚构实体，每类实体均配以单句提问（每模型504个提示）。两种无监督、单次前向传播的激活分散性度量——逆参与比与谱熵，在所有领域和规模下均能以AUROC 0.95-1.00区分已知与虚构实体；监督线性探测器可达0.99-1.00。两种度量均具有约0.70-0.74的清晰选择感知置换下限（经验p≤1e-3），在保留层选择下仍保持0.93-0.99，且在真实姓名（已知vs.真实但生僻）上持续表现0.96-1.00。该信号可跨实体类型迁移（平均非对角AUROC 0.92-0.99）；匹配模板的反事实实验表明，仅模板导致的大幅下降，而非实体类型效应，且信号在多个注意力头间广泛分布。该表征信号在1.5B模型时已达上限，而行为层面的事实可靠性则随规模急剧上升：在严格评判下，1.5B、4.5B、7B、11B模型分别正确回答42名已知运动员中的0、2、10、19名。在已知实体中，区分正确与幻觉答案难度更高（探测器0.93；分散性不优于首标记熵基线）。五样本语义熵基线在五倍推理成本下仅达0.71-0.83。尽管模型内部已具备认知意识，却几乎从不拒绝回答：2520个答案审计发现仅2次拒绝和1次谨慎回应。实体熟悉度与事实可靠性是不同尺度上的独立现象。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models can distinguish between familiar entities and fabricated ones before generating answers, and whether such internal activation patterns predict factual reliability. Using four Polish Bielik models (1.5B–11B parameters) and 504 prompts per model across four entity domains, the authors measure activation dispersion via two unsupervised metrics—inverse participation ratio and spectral entropy—which achieve AUROC 0.95–1.00 in separating known from fabricated entities. A supervised linear probe reaches 0.99–1.00, and these signals remain robust across layer selection and real vs. obscure entities. The signal transfers across entity types and is diffuse across transformer heads, reaching ceiling at 1.5B scale, while factual accuracy scales sharply with model size. However, distinguishing correct from hallucinated answers among known entities remains challenging (probe AUROC 0.93), and models rarely abstain despite internal awareness.
该研究探讨大型语言模型在生成答案前是否能区分熟悉实体与虚构实体，以及这种内部激活模式是否可预测事实可靠性。通过对四个波兰Bielik模型（1.5B–11B参数）在四个实体领域中各42个知名、42个真实但生僻、42个虚构实体进行测试，作者使用两种无监督激活分散度指标（逆参与比与谱熵），在所有领域和规模上均达到0.95–1.00的AUROC，区分已知与虚构实体；监督线性探测器达到0.99–1.00，且在实体类型间具有泛化能力。尽管模型对实体熟悉度有明显感知，其事实准确性却增长缓慢：1.5B至11B模型仅分别正确回答0–19名知名运动员。在已知实体中区分正确与虚构答案仍较困难（探测器AUROC 0.93），且模型极少拒绝回答，2520个答案中仅3次拒绝。研究结果表明，实体熟悉度与事实可靠性是不同尺度下独立发展的现象。
DiaLLM: An Investigation into the Robustness-Generation Gap in English Dialect Adaptation
Authors: Jordan Painter, Dipankar Srirag, Adarsh Kappiyath, Diptesh Kanojia, Aditya Joshi, Lu Yin
First: 2026-07-08T17:24:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:24:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models increasingly \emph{understand} dialectal English, yet still \emph{produce} only standard, US-leaning English, leaving dialectal generation, the harder half of the problem, largely unaddressed. We introduce \textbf{DiaLLM}, which continually pretrains three open-weight language model families on the International Corpus of English and applies implicit and explicit post-training paradigms, each combined with three model alignment strategies, giving the first controlled comparison of these components across Australian, Indian, and Northern British English. Our results reveal that dialectal robustness and generation are \emph{dissociated}: benchmarks are shaped by continual pretraining and SFT, while alignment visibly reshapes generation in ways benchmarks do not capture. Explicit variety-targeted adaptation produces output reliably recognised as dialectal and preferred over broad alignment, yet the method that most aggressively optimises the dialectal reward is not preferred by human evaluators. Independent linguistic analysis corroborates this reward-quality gap, most clearly on two of the three families. No single alignment method dominates, and closing the gap will require richer reward designs and continued investment in dialectal resources. We release all code, checkpoints, and preference datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DiaLLM：对英语方言适应中鲁棒性-生成差距的探究
大型语言模型日益能够理解方言英语，但仍仅生成标准的、偏向美国英语的内容，使得生成方言这一问题的难解部分基本未被解决。我们引入了\textbf{DiaLLM}，该模型在国际英语语料库上持续预训练三个开源语言模型家族，并结合隐式与显式后训练范式，每种范式均搭配三种模型对齐策略，首次在澳大利亚、印度及英伦北部英语中对这些组件进行了可控比较。结果表明，方言鲁棒性与生成能力是\emph{分离的}：基准测试受持续预训练和监督微调影响，而对齐策略则明显重塑生成方式，而基准测试无法捕捉。显式针对方言的适应方法能稳定生成被识别为方言的内容，并优于广泛对齐，但最激进优化方言奖励的方法并未获得人类评估者青睐。独立的语言学分析证实了奖励质量差距，尤其在三个家族中的两个上表现明显。无单一对齐方法占优，弥合此差距需更丰富的奖励设计及持续投入方言资源。我们公开所有代码、检查点及偏好数据集。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that large language models understand dialectal English but fail to generate it reliably, the authors introduce DiaLLM, a framework that pretrains three open-weight language models on the International Corpus of English and then applies various post-training methods combined with alignment strategies. Experiments across Australian, Indian, and Northern British English reveal a dissociation between dialectal robustness and generation: pretraining and supervised fine-tuning improve understanding but not output quality, while explicit variety-targeted adaptation produces outputs more reliably recognized as dialectal and preferred by humans, despite the most aggressive reward-optimizing method being less favored. Linguistic analysis confirms this gap, especially for two model families, indicating that no single alignment method dominates and that richer reward designs and dialectal resources are needed to close the gap.

Data-Driven Forecasting of three-Component Seismograms Using Transformer Architectures
Authors: Waleed Esmail, Stuart Russell, Jana Klinge, Alexander Kappes, Christine Thomas
First: 2026-06-01T21:38:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:23:59+00:00
Comments: 35 pages, 13 figures and 4 tables. Submitted to JGR: Machine Learning and Computation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Forecasting seismic waveforms beyond observed data remains challenging due to the nonlinear, dispersive, and multi-scale nature of seismic wave propagation. In this work, we introduce \textsc{SeismoGPT}, a transformer-based autoregressive model designed to forecast three-component seismic waveforms directly in the time domain. Forecasting is formulated as a physically constrained continuation problem in which the model receives waveform context beginning at the P-wave arrival and extending a defined time beyond the S-wave arrival, after which future motion is generated recursively without access to ground-truth samples. Evaluation is performed on synthetic seismograms spanning source depths of 5--100\,km, epicentral distances of 10--90$^\circ$, and magnitudes $3 \leq M_w \leq 7$. To disentangle the effects of context length and prediction horizon, we define three evaluation configurations using a distance-normalized context ratio and fixed prediction horizons of 120 and 240\,s. Across all configurations, the model achieves a median normalized cross correlation of 0.93 or higher. Analysis of representative forecasts shows that successful predictions preserve both phase coherence and spectral energy distribution. Where failure cases arise, this is primarily due to gradual phase drift during autoregressive rollout rather than unphysical signal generation. These results demonstrate that transformer-based sequence models can learn stable dynamical continuation of seismic wavefields, highlighting the potential of foundation-model approaches for physics-driven time-series forecasting. There are potential applications of this methodology in seismic warning and hazard mitigation, particularly for next-generation gravitational-wave observatories, such as the Einstein Telescope.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于Transformer架构的数据驱动三通道地震波形预测
由于地震波传播具有非线性、色散和多尺度特性，超出观测数据的地震波形预测仍具挑战性。本文提出SeismoGPT，一种基于Transformer的自回归模型，直接在时域内预测三通道地震波形。预测被建模为物理约束的延续问题：模型接收从P波到达开始、延伸至S波到达后指定时间的波形上下文，随后递归生成未来运动，且不依赖真实样本。评估在合成地震图上进行，覆盖震源深度5–100 km、震中距10–90°、震级3 ≤ Mw ≤ 7。为分离上下文长度与预测时长的影响，定义三种评估配置，采用距离归一化上下文比例及固定预测时长120秒和240秒。所有配置下，模型均达到0.93或以上的归一化互相关中位数。代表性预测分析表明，成功预测保持相位相干性和频谱能量分布；失败案例主要源于自回归展开过程中的相位漂移，而非非物理信号生成。结果表明，基于Transformer的序列模型可学习地震波场的稳定动力延续，凸显基础模型在物理驱动时间序列预测中的潜力，该方法在地震预警与灾害缓解中具有应用前景，尤其适用于下一代引力波探测器（如爱因斯坦望远镜）。

The model is trained to generate future waveforms recursively after receiving context from P-wave arrival through S-wave arrival, without access to ground-truth data beyond that point. Guidance Breaks the Fitted Operator: A Terminal-Fitted Repair for Classifier-Free Guidance
Authors: Shiheng Zhang
First: 2026-07-08T17:21:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:21:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Classifier-free guidance (CFG) is the standard way to strengthen class-conditioning in diffusion and flow-matching samplers, yet at large guidance it oversaturates and destabilizes, symptoms practitioners suppress with more steps or limited-interval schedules. We analyze CFG through an asymptotic-preserving, numerical-analysis lens. Building on a recent result that the deterministic DDIM step is the unique fitted operator for the unguided terminal layer, exact on the final small-sigma stretch of sampling, we show that guidance re-stiffens exactly the discriminative subspace to an anomalous exponent 1+w. DDIM is therefore no longer fitted there, and on coarse meshes its guided residual diverges as sigma_min goes to zero. We prove a guided clock barrier with three ordered step-size thresholds, and read one-step oversaturation as its endpoint: a solver artifact on the calibration model rather than the continuous guided law. The same analysis yields a one-coefficient, zero-extra-NFE repair: replace CFG's w(r-1) by r^(1+w)-r on the guidance direction. On the calibration model's discriminative crossover, this removes CFG's sigma_min-divergent blow-up and is first-order accurate against the exact guided flow as sigma_min goes to zero. On learned CIFAR-10 checkpoints, and as a cross-domain smoke test on Stable Diffusion 1.5 DDIM, it acts as a high-guidance stabilizer at no extra cost rather than a universal quality knob: it cuts residual amplification and saturation, gives 9/9 point-FID wins over CFG on the tested grid, and preserves classifier-proxy target accuracy in the hard-cell blocks. We report the limits alongside: it is not a universal image-quality win, and against a dense vanilla-CFG reference it is not a uniformly better integrator of that field.
中文标题/摘要
标题：指导打破拟合算子：一种针对无分类器指导的终端拟合修复
无分类器指导（CFG）是增强扩散和流匹配采样器类别条件的标准方法，但在大指导强度下会过饱和并导致不稳定，从业者通常通过增加步骤或限制区间调度来缓解。我们从渐近保持的数值分析角度分析CFG。基于最近的一项成果——确定性DDIM步骤是未引导终端层的唯一拟合算子，且在采样末尾的小sigma区间内完全精确——我们证明指导会将判别子空间重新硬化至异常指数1+w。因此DDIM在该区域不再拟合，且在粗网格上，其引导残差随sigma_min趋于零而发散。我们证明了具有三个有序步长阈值的引导时钟屏障，并将一步过饱和视为其终点：这是校准模型上的求解器伪像，而非连续引导定律的固有特性。相同的分析得出一种仅需一个系数、无需额外NFE的修复方案：在引导方向上，将CFG的w(r-1)替换为r^(1+w)-r。在校准模型的判别交叉点，该修复消除了CFG的sigma_min发散现象，并在sigma_min趋于零时对精确引导流达到一阶精度。在学习的CIFAR-10检查点上，以及作为在Stable Diffusion 1.5 DDIM上的跨域测试，该修复在不增加额外成本的情况下充当高引导稳定器，而非通用质量调节器：它减少了残差放大和饱和，对测试网格实现了9/9点FID胜出，且在困难块中保持了分类器代理目标的准确性。我们同时报告其局限性：它并非通用图像质量提升，且相对于密集的原始CFG基准，它并非该领域的更优积分器。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of classifier-free guidance (CFG) at high guidance scales, this work analyzes CFG through an asymptotic-preserving numerical lens, revealing that guidance re-stiffens the discriminative subspace to an anomalous exponent 1+w, breaking DDIM’s fitted operator property and causing residual divergence as sigma_min approaches zero. The authors propose a one-coefficient repair: replacing w(r-1) with r^(1+w)-r on the guidance direction, which eliminates the sigma_min-divergent blow-up and achieves first-order accuracy against the exact guided flow. Experimentally, this repair stabilizes high-guidance sampling on CIFAR-10 and Stable Diffusion 1.5 DDIM without extra NFE, reducing residual amplification and saturation, achieving 9/9 point-FID wins over CFG, and preserving classifier-proxy accuracy in hard regions, though it is not universally superior in image quality or as a general integrator.
为解决高引导尺度下分类器自由引导（CFG）的不稳定问题，本文从渐近保持的数值分析角度剖析CFG，发现引导会将判别子空间重新硬化至异常指数1+w，破坏DDIM的拟合算子性质，导致sigma_min趋近零时残差发散。作者提出一种单系数修复方案：将CFG中的w(r-1)替换为r^(1+w)-r（沿引导方向），有效消除sigma_min发散并实现对精确引导流的一阶精度。实验表明，该修复在CIFAR-10和Stable Diffusion 1.5 DDIM上稳定高引导采样，无需额外NFE，降低残差放大与饱和，于测试网格上实现9/9点FID胜出，且保持硬区域分类器代理精度，但并非所有指标均优于原始CFG。
Recursive Self-Improvement in AI: From Bounded Self-Refinement to Autonomous Research Loops
Authors: Mingguang Chen, Licheng Wang, Bo Qu
First: 2026-07-08T17:19:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:19:50+00:00
Comments: 42 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI systems increasingly participate in their own improvement: revising their outputs, adapting their own harnesses during deployment, training on data they generate, and, increasingly, conducting AI research itself. This literature is described under a vocabulary ("self-refine," "self-reward," "self-play," "self-evolve") that conflates fundamentally different ambitions. We survey 1,250 arXiv papers (2024-2026) along two axes: what the system improves -- its behavior in deployment, its policy through training, its evaluator, or the research process itself -- and the degree of loop closure (human-in-the-loop to fully closed). The taxonomy separates bounded self-refinement -- convergent, evaluable, and already industrial practice -- from open-ended recursive self-improvement (RSI), which remains bounded by grounding requirements, collapse dynamics, and compute constraints on every measured axis. Its distinctive feature is a dedicated category for self-evaluation: every improvement loop is a claim that some signal can substitute for human judgment. We survey the evaluator design space -- judges, process reward models, verifiers, rubrics, meta-evaluation -- order the signals into a verification hierarchy from formal verifiers (strongest) to intrinsic self-assessment (weakest), and observe that demonstrated self-improvement strength tracks this hierarchy, that its failure modes (self-confirming loops, model collapse, diversity collapse) follow from its violations, and that the "research direction-setting" bottleneck keeping humans in the loop sits at the top of that hierarchy. We connect the technical literature to the theory of RSI limits and to the safety and governance questions raised by frontier-lab accounts of closing the loop, and identify governance-grade measurement of self-improvement as the field's most underpopulated niche.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能中的递归自我改进：从有界自我精炼到自主研究循环
人工智能系统日益参与自身改进：修订自身输出、部署期间调整自身架构、在自身生成的数据上进行训练，并越来越多地开展自身人工智能研究。该领域的文献使用一套术语（“自我精炼”、“自我奖励”、“自我对弈”、“自我演化”）混淆了根本不同的目标。我们沿两个维度调研了1250篇arXiv论文（2024-2026）：系统改进的对象——部署中的行为、训练中的策略、评估器或研究过程本身；以及循环闭合程度（人类参与至完全闭合）。该分类法将有界自我精炼——收敛、可评估、且已进入工业实践——与开放式的递归自我改进（RSI）区分开来，后者在每个衡量维度上仍受接地要求、崩溃动态和计算约束的限制。其独特之处在于设立了一个专门的自我评估类别：每个改进循环都是一种主张，即某种信号可替代人类判断。我们调研了评估器设计空间——裁判、过程奖励模型、验证器、评分标准、元评估——将信号按验证层级排序，从形式验证器（最强）到内在自我评估（最弱），并观察到已验证的自我改进能力与该层级一致，其失效模式（自我确认循环、模型崩溃、多样性崩溃）源于其违反，而“研究方向设定”瓶颈——使人类仍需参与——位于该层级顶端。我们将技术文献与RSI极限理论联系起来，并探讨前沿实验室关于闭合循环所引发的安全与治理问题，指出自我改进的治理级测量是该领域最缺乏的空白领域。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing trend of AI systems autonomously improving themselves through mechanisms like self-refinement and self-research, this paper surveys 1,250 arXiv papers to classify self-improvement methods along two dimensions: what is being improved (behavior, policy, evaluator, or research process) and the degree of loop closure (from human-in-the-loop to fully autonomous). It distinguishes bounded self-refinement — already industrial and convergent — from open-ended recursive self-improvement (RSI), which remains constrained by grounding, collapse risks, and compute limits. The study introduces a taxonomy that includes a dedicated category for self-evaluation, ordering evaluation signals from formal verifiers to intrinsic self-assessment, and finds that self-improvement strength correlates with this hierarchy, while failure modes like self-confirming loops or diversity collapse stem from violating it. The paper concludes that the bottleneck in closing the loop lies in setting research directions, and identifies governance-grade measurement of self-improvement as a critical, underdeveloped area.
鉴于AI系统日益通过自我修正、自我奖励、自我博弈和自我演化等方式实现自主改进，本文对2024-2026年间1250篇arXiv论文进行调研，按两个维度分类：改进对象（部署行为、训练策略、评估器或研究过程）与闭环程度（从人类参与至完全自主）。研究区分了收敛且已工业化的有界自我优化，与受接地性、崩溃风险和算力限制约束的开放递归自我改进（RSI）。文中提出一个分类体系，特别设立自我评估类别，将评估信号从形式验证器到内在自我评估排序，发现自我改进能力与该层级正相关，其失效模式（如自我确认循环、模型崩溃、多样性崩溃）源于层级违反。研究指出，研究方向设定是维持人类参与的关键瓶颈，并认为自我改进的治理级度量是该领域最亟待发展的空白领域。
Framing Instability in LLM Ethical Stance: Auditing Negation Sensitivity in Moral Dilemmas
Authors: Katherine Elkins, Jon Chun
First: 2026-01-29T09:09:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:11:16+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 5 figures (added gold labeled human datasets and additional statistical tests)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models are increasingly consulted on ethically consequential questions, yet the stance a model expresses may not survive a change in framing. We audit 16 models across 14 ethically fraught dilemmas using polarity-paired proposals ("They should X" / "They should not X"). A model's judgment of the underlying action should not reverse merely because the question is phrased as a prohibition rather than a prescription and yet, we find systematic deviations from this invariance including wholesale endorsement flips, indicating that ethical decisions are vulnerable to framing instability. Small open-weight models (1-4B parameters) endorse a proposed action 24% of the time under affirmative framing but up to 100% under negated framings, a swing of as much as 76 percentage points. Human coding of a response sample confirms the instability is genuine while showing that binary agree/disagree proxies over-state its magnitude, suggesting that an LLM judge cannot replace human coders because it silently collapses abstentions and mirrors the very forced-choice bias under study. Commercial models are for the most part more stable but still shift substantially, with cross-model agreement dropping from 73% on the bare affirmative framing to 59% under simple negation. We argue that because binary agree/disagree formats both inflate apparent endorsement and mask polarity-dependence, single-phrasing audits can misreport a model's ethical stance, and we propose the Negation Sensitivity Index (NSI) as a complement that measures stance stability directly. A model whose stance flips with phrasing cannot be relied upon in any high-stakes decision scenario.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型伦理立场的框架不稳定性：道德困境中否定敏感性的审计
语言模型日益被用于处理具有伦理重要性的决策，但模型所表达的立场可能因问题表述方式的改变而失效。我们对16个模型在14个道德困境中使用极性配对提案（“他们应该X”/“他们不应该X”）进行审计。模型对底层行为的判断不应因问题表述为禁止而非建议而发生逆转，但我们的研究发现存在系统性偏离这种不变性，包括全面立场反转，表明伦理决策易受框架不稳定性影响。小型开放权重模型（1-4B参数）在肯定性表述下支持提议行为的比例为24%，而在否定性表述下高达100%，波动幅度高达76个百分点。对样本回答的人类编码确认了这种不稳定性的真实性，同时表明二元同意/不同意代理夸大了其幅度，暗示LLM裁判无法替代人类编码员，因为它会静默合并弃权并反映所研究的强制选择偏差。商业模型总体上更稳定，但仍存在显著变化，从肯定性表述下的跨模型一致性73%降至简单否定表述下的59%。我们认为，由于二元同意/不同意格式既夸大了支持度又掩盖了极性依赖性，单表述审计可能错误报告模型的伦理立场，因此我们提出否定敏感性指数（NSI）作为补充指标，直接衡量立场稳定性。立场随表述方式变化的模型不能在任何高风险决策场景中被依赖。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how language models' ethical judgments shift when moral dilemmas are framed as prohibitions versus prescriptions, motivated by concerns that model stances may be fragile to phrasing. Using 16 models tested across 14 dilemmas with polarity-paired prompts, researchers found systematic reversals in ethical endorsements, especially in small open-weight models that flip from 24% to 100% support depending on framing—a 76-point swing. Human coding validated the instability and revealed that binary agree/disagree labels overstate the effect by collapsing abstentions and mirroring forced-choice bias. Commercial models showed greater stability but still exhibited significant shifts, reducing cross-model agreement from 73% to 59%. The authors argue that single-phrasing audits misrepresent ethical stance and propose the Negation Sensitivity Index (NSI) to directly measure framing stability, concluding that models whose stances flip cannot be trusted in high-stakes decisions.
该研究探讨了语言模型在道德困境中因提问方式（肯定句 vs 否定句）改变而产生的伦理立场不稳定性，动机源于模型立场可能随语境变化而动摇的担忧。研究人员对16个模型在14个道德困境中使用正负极性配对提示进行审计，发现系统性的立场反转——尤其在小型开源模型中，支持率可波动达76个百分点。人工标注证实这些变化真实存在，且二元同意/不同意格式会夸大一致性并掩盖中立情况。商业模型虽更稳定，但在否定句式下跨模型一致性仍从73%降至59%。作者指出单句式审计会误报模型伦理立场，提出“否定敏感指数”（NSI）作为直接衡量立场稳定性的补充指标，并强调立场随语境翻转的模型不可用于高风险决策。
VOTE: Vision-Language-Action Optimization with Trajectory Ensemble Voting
Authors: Juyi Lin, Amir Taherin, Arash Akbari, Arman Akbari, Lei Lu, Guangyu Chen, Taskin Padir, Xiaomeng Yang, Weiwei Chen, Yiqian Li, Xue Lin, David Kaeli, Pu Zhao, Yanzhi Wang
First: 2025-07-07T15:30:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:09:24+00:00
Comments: 12 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent large-scale Vision Language Action (VLA) models have shown superior performance in robotic manipulation tasks guided by natural language. However, current VLA models suffer from two drawbacks: (i) generation of massive tokens leading to high inference latency and increased training cost, and (ii) insufficient utilization of generated actions resulting in potential performance loss. To address these issues, we develop a training framework to finetune VLA models for generating significantly fewer action tokens with high parallelism, effectively reducing inference latency and training cost. Furthermore, we introduce an inference optimization technique with a novel voting-based ensemble strategy to combine current and previous action predictions, improving the utilization of generated actions and overall performance. Our results demonstrate that we achieve superior performance compared with state-of-the-art VLA models, achieving significantly higher success rates and 39$\times$ faster inference than OpenVLA with 46 Hz throughput on edge platforms, demonstrating practical deployability. The code is available at https://github.com/LukeLIN-web/VOTE.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VOTE：基于轨迹集成投票的视觉-语言-动作优化
近期大规模视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在自然语言引导的机器人操作任务中表现出色。然而，当前VLA模型存在两个缺点：(i) 生成大量token导致推理延迟高、训练成本增加；(ii) 生成动作利用率不足，可能造成性能损失。为解决这些问题，我们开发了一种训练框架，用于微调VLA模型以生成显著更少的动作token并实现高并行度，有效降低推理延迟和训练成本。此外，我们引入了一种基于投票的集成策略的推理优化技术，结合当前与先前的动作预测，提高生成动作的利用率和整体性能。实验结果表明，我们的方法相比现有最先进VLA模型表现更优，在边缘平台以46Hz吞吐量实现比OpenVLA快39倍的推理速度，且成功率显著提升，证明了其实际部署能力。代码开源于https://github.com/LukeLIN-web/VOTE。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and suboptimal action utilization in current Vision-Language-Action models, this work proposes VOTE, a framework that fine-tunes VLA models to generate fewer action tokens while maintaining high parallelism, thereby reducing inference latency and training cost. It further introduces an inference-time ensemble voting mechanism that combines current and prior action predictions to enhance action utilization and performance. Experiments show VOTE outperforms state-of-the-art models with significantly higher success rates and 39x faster inference speed than OpenVLA, achieving 46 Hz throughput on edge devices, demonstrating practical deployment potential.
为解决现有视觉-语言-动作模型生成冗余动作令牌、推理延迟高且动作利用率低的问题，本文提出VOTE框架，通过训练阶段优化减少动作令牌生成以降低推理延迟与训练成本，并在推理阶段引入基于轨迹投票的集成策略，结合当前与历史动作预测以提升动作利用率与整体性能。实验表明，VOTE在成功率上优于当前最先进模型，推理速度比OpenVLA快39倍，在边缘设备上达到46Hz吞吐量，具备实际部署能力。
LiveOIBench: Can Large Language Models Outperform Human Contestants in Informatics Olympiads?
Authors: Kaijian Zou, Aaron Xiong, Yunxiang Zhang, Frederick Zhang, Yueqi Ren, Jirong Yang, Ayoung Lee, Shitanshu Bhushan, Lu Wang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-10-10T17:54:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:08:31+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026 Camera Ready
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Competitive programming problems are increasingly used to evaluate the coding capabilities of large language models (LLMs) due to their complexity and ease of verification. Yet, current coding benchmarks face limitations such as a lack of exceptionally challenging problems, insufficient test case coverage, and reliance on online platform APIs that limit accessibility. To address these issues, we introduce LiveOIBench, a large-scale competitive programming benchmark featuring 403 expert-curated problems, averaging 60 official test cases each, drawn from 72 contests across 14 Informatics Olympiads held between 2023 and 2025. LiveOIBench has four key features: (1) expert-designed tasks with detailed subtask rubrics and extensive test cases; (2) direct comparison to elite human contestants; (3) continuous updates to reduce contamination risk; and (4) a fully offline, reproducible evaluation system. Benchmarking 34 popular general-purpose and reasoning LLMs, we find that GPT-5 achieves an 81.76th percentile, still falling short of top human contestants, while among the open-weight models, GPT-OSS-120B reaches only the 60th percentile. Reasoning-trace analyses indicate that robust reasoning models prioritize precise problem analysis over excessive exploration. Finally, analyses across release dates, task familiarity, and code similarity find minimal evidence of data contamination in our benchmark. Our leaderboard, code, and data are available at: https://liveoibench.github.io/.

标题：LiveOIBench：大语言模型能否在信息学奥林匹克竞赛中超越人类选手？
由于其复杂性和易于验证的特性，编程竞赛题目越来越多地被用于评估大语言模型（LLM）的编码能力。然而，当前的编码基准测试存在挑战性题目不足、测试用例覆盖不全、依赖在线平台API导致可访问性受限等问题。为解决这些问题，我们推出了LiveOIBench，这是一个大规模编程竞赛基准，包含403道由专家精心挑选的问题，每题平均有60个官方测试用例，来源于2023至2025年间14场信息学奥林匹克竞赛中的72场比赛。LiveOIBench具有四大特点：（1）专家设计的任务，配有详细子任务评分标准和大量测试用例；（2）可直接与顶尖人类选手对比；（3）持续更新以降低数据污染风险；（4）完全离线、可复现的评估系统。我们对34个流行的一般用途和推理型LLM进行了基准测试，发现GPT-5达到第81.76百分位，但仍未能超越顶尖人类选手；在开源模型中，GPT-OSS-120B仅达到第60百分位。推理轨迹分析表明，稳健的推理模型更注重精确的问题分析，而非过度探索。在发布日期、任务熟悉度和代码相似度方面的分析显示，我们的基准测试中几乎没有数据污染的证据。我们的排行榜、代码和数据可在：https://liveoibench.github.io/ 获取。

为评估大语言模型在编程竞赛中的能力，作者构建了LiveOIBench，包含来自14届信息学奥林匹克竞赛（2023–2025）的403道专家设计题目，每题配有60+测试用例和详细子任务评分标准，支持与顶尖人类选手直接对比，并提供离线可复现评估以避免数据污染。在34个主流通用及推理模型中，GPT-5得分81.76百分位，仍低于人类顶尖选手，而开源模型GPT-OSS-120B仅达60百分位。推理轨迹分析表明，表现优异的模型更注重精准问题分析而非广泛探索，且跨发布日期与代码相似性分析显示数据污染极小。
Modeling Failure Dynamics in Time-Constrained Authentication Systems: Evidence of a Success Cliff in USSD Workflows
Authors: Aklile Seyoum Mamo, Amanuel Kebede, Anny Christelle Irakoze, Jema Ndibwile
First: 2026-07-08T17:06:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:06:30+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 6 tables, 5 figures, 12 equations, IEEE International Conference on Cyber Security and Resilience (CSR)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Time-constrained interactive systems such as USSD (Unstructured Supplementary Service Data)-based financial services operate under strict session limits and sequential user interaction. While stronger authentication mechanisms improve security, they also increase interaction complexity and time burden, potentially reducing transaction completion. In this work, we model the failure dynamics of such systems and investigate how authentication complexity interacts with user response time and network round-trip time to influence session success rate. We propose and implement a simulation-based framework to investigate these failure dynamics and formally define a non-linear failure phenomenon, termed the \textit{Success Cliff}, where session success rates sharply decline beyond a critical complexity threshold. Through controlled experiments, we quantify the trade-off between security and usability and identify conditions under which secure authentication workflows become operationally unreliable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：时间受限认证系统中的失败动态建模：USSD工作流中成功悬崖的实证研究
基于USSD（非结构化补充服务数据）的金融服务等时间受限交互系统，受严格会话限制和顺序用户交互影响。虽然更强的认证机制提升安全性，但也增加交互复杂性和时间负担，可能降低交易完成率。本文建模此类系统的失败动态，研究认证复杂性如何与用户响应时间和网络往返时间相互作用，影响会话成功率。我们提出并实现了一种基于仿真的框架，正式定义了一种非线性失败现象——“成功悬崖”，即当复杂度超过临界阈值时，会话成功率急剧下降。通过受控实验，我们量化了安全与可用性之间的权衡，并识别出安全认证工作流在何种条件下变得操作不可靠。
