Motivated by the need to understand how authentication complexity impacts usability in time-sensitive systems like USSD-based financial services, this study models failure dynamics by simulating user interactions under constraints of response time and network latency. The authors introduce a simulation framework to analyze how increasing authentication steps affect session success rates, identifying a non-linear failure pattern called the 'Success Cliff'—a sharp drop in success beyond a critical complexity threshold. Experimental results confirm that while stronger authentication improves security, it also reduces completion rates significantly once complexity surpasses a tipping point, revealing a trade-off between security and operational reliability.
该研究探讨了在时间受限的系统（如基于USSD的金融服务）中，认证复杂度如何影响会话成功率，动机在于在严格时间限制下平衡安全与可用性。作者构建了一个基于仿真的框架，结合用户响应时间和网络往返时间，分析认证步骤增加对会话完成率的影响。实验结果揭示了一种非线性失败模式——“成功悬崖”，即当认证复杂度超过某一临界阈值时，成功率会急剧下降，表明过度复杂的认证流程虽提升安全性，却可能造成实际操作不可靠。
RL Post-Training Builds Compositional Reasoning Strategies
Authors: Azwar Abdulsalam, Nishil Patel, Andrew Saxe
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-07-08T17:04:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:04:42+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 6 figures. Accepted to the 2nd Workshop on Compositional Learning at ICML 2026, Seoul, South Korea
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Does RL post-training merely amplify primitive skills already latent in a base model, or can it compose primitive skills into new higher-level strategies? We study this question in a fully observable rewrite-grammar environment where the pretraining distribution is known and every generated rewrite can be audited. A Transformer is pretrained on primitive symbol-rewrite chains and post-trained on a Trace-based reasoning task with only a binary final-answer reward. RL solves held-out problems that remain rarely solved by the pretrained model even under much larger sampling budgets, while rejection fine-tuning improves early but plateaus. Trace analysis shows that RL reorganizes primitive competence through a phased compositional mechanism: it first strengthens primitive reductions, then discovers valid composed procedures. These include sequential compositions, which collapse ordered chains of primitive contractions, and parallel compositions, which combine independent primitive contractions in a single step. The composed procedures are not isolated samples; they are reused and consolidated into a stable repertoire. Comparing RL with rejection fine-tuning shows that the key difference is not exploration volume but selectivity: RFT produces many shortcut-like rewrites, much of them invalid, whereas RL concentrates exploration into valid reusable structure. Pretraining ablations show that the emergence of compositional strategies is gated not by primitive exposure alone, but by whether pretraining organizes primitive competence into reduction procedures that RL can later compress. The base model provides weak procedural ingredients; RL builds them into reliable higher-level strategies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：强化学习后训练构建组合推理策略
强化学习后训练仅仅是放大基础模型中已存在的原始技能，还是能够将原始技能组合成新的高级策略？我们在一个完全可观测的重写语法环境中研究了这一问题，该环境中的预训练分布已知，且每个生成的重写均可审计。Transformer在原始符号重写链上进行预训练，随后在基于轨迹的推理任务上进行后训练，仅使用二元最终答案奖励。强化学习解决了预训练模型即使在更大采样预算下也极少解决的保留问题，而拒绝微调则仅在早期有效后趋于平缓。轨迹分析表明，强化学习通过分阶段的组合机制重组原始能力：首先强化原始简化，然后发现有效的组合程序。这些组合程序包括顺序组合（将原始收缩的有序链压缩）和并行组合（在单步内结合独立的原始收缩）。这些组合程序并非孤立样本，而是被重用并整合为稳定的能力库。对比强化学习与拒绝微调发现，关键差异不在于探索量，而在于选择性：拒绝微调产生大量类似捷径的重写，其中大部分无效，而强化学习将探索集中于有效且可重用的结构。预训练消融实验表明，组合策略的出现不仅取决于原始技能的暴露，更取决于预训练是否将原始能力组织为强化学习可压缩的简化程序。基础模型提供弱的程序性成分，强化学习将其构建为可靠的高级策略。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether reinforcement learning (RL) post-training can compose primitive skills into higher-level strategies or merely amplify existing ones. Using a fully observable rewrite-grammar environment with known pretraining distribution and auditable outputs, a Transformer pretrained on symbol rewrite chains is post-trained via RL on a trace-based reasoning task with binary reward. Results show RL outperforms rejection fine-tuning on held-out problems, even with smaller sampling budgets, by reorganizing primitive competence through a phased compositional mechanism: first strengthening primitive reductions, then discovering sequential and parallel compositions that collapse or combine primitive steps. These composed strategies are reused and consolidated into a stable repertoire, contrasting with rejection fine-tuning’s invalid shortcuts. Pretraining ablations reveal that compositional strategy emergence depends on whether pretraining organizes primitives into reduction procedures, not just exposure volume.
该研究探讨强化学习（RL）后训练能否将原始能力组合成更高层次策略，还是仅放大已有能力。在具备已知预训练分布和可审计输出的完全可观测重写语法环境中，一个在符号重写链上预训练的Transformer通过基于轨迹的推理任务进行RL后训练（二元奖励）。实验表明，RL在未见过的问题上表现优于拒绝微调，即使采样预算更小，其通过分阶段组合机制重组原始能力：先强化原始约简，再发现可复用的顺序与并行组合。轨迹分析显示RL探索更具选择性，聚焦有效可复用结构而非无效捷径；消融实验表明，组合策略的出现依赖于预训练是否将原始能力组织为RL可压缩的约简过程，而非单纯暴露原始能力。
FDRMFL: Multimodal Federated Feature Extraction Model Based on Information Maximization and Contrastive Learning
Authors: Haozhe Wu
Venue: Applied Soft Computing 202 (2026) 115874
First: 2025-11-30T17:13:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T17:04:31+00:00
Comments: Accepted author manuscript; published version DOI: 10.1016/j.asoc.2026.115874
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose FDRMFL, a task-driven multimodal feature extraction framework for federated regression under non-IID data distributions. Extracting predictive features from high-dimensional multimodal inputs is particularly challenging in this setting: data cannot leave each client, local samples are scarce and heterogeneously distributed, and unsupervised dimensionality reduction discards task-relevant information while federated training introduces representation drift across communication rounds. FDRMFL addresses these challenges through a unified four-term local objective: MSE prediction loss, a correlation-based mutual information surrogate that preserves dependence between the fused representation and the continuous target, a symmetric KL penalty that aligns cross-modal latent distributions before fusion, and an InfoNCE-style contrastive loss that anchors local representations to the global consensus. Experiments on three synthetic and two real-world near-infrared spectroscopy datasets under non-IID federated partitions, with comprehensive ablation and sensitivity analyses, demonstrate that each component contributes to the framework's effectiveness. FDRMFL reduces mean MSE by 33.8% relative to the best traditional baseline (PCA) and by 43.0% relative to VAE in simulation, and attains the lowest overall mean MSE among six federated algorithms including FedAvg, FedProx, MOON, SCAFFOLD, and FedBN.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FDRMFL：基于信息最大化与对比学习的多模态联邦特征提取模型
我们提出FDRMFL，这是一种面向联邦回归任务、在非独立同分布（non-IID）数据分布下的任务驱动型多模态特征提取框架。在该场景下，从高维多模态输入中提取预测性特征尤为困难：数据不能离开各客户端，本地样本稀缺且异质分布，无监督降维会丢弃任务相关的信息，而联邦训练则会在通信轮次中引入表示漂移。FDRMFL通过一个统一的四项局部目标函数应对这些挑战：均方误差预测损失、基于相关性的互信息代理函数以保持融合表示与连续目标之间的依赖关系、对称KL惩罚以在融合前对齐跨模态潜在分布，以及InfoNCE风格的对比损失以将局部表示锚定到全局共识。在三个合成及两个真实近红外光谱数据集上的非IID联邦划分实验中，通过全面的消融与敏感性分析表明，各组件均对框架有效性有贡献。FDRMFL在模拟实验中相较最佳传统基线（PCA）降低均方误差33.8%，相较VAE降低43.0%，并在包括FedAvg、FedProx、MOON、SCAFFOLD和FedBN在内的六种联邦算法中取得最低的整体均方误差。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of extracting predictive features from high-dimensional, multimodal data under non-IID federated settings where data cannot leave clients, FDRMFL introduces a unified four-term local objective combining MSE prediction loss, a mutual information surrogate to preserve target dependence, a symmetric KL divergence penalty to align cross-modal distributions, and an InfoNCE contrastive loss to anchor local representations to global consensus. Experiments across synthetic and real-world near-infrared spectroscopy datasets show FDRMFL reduces mean MSE by 33.8% compared to PCA and 43.0% compared to VAE in simulation, and achieves the lowest overall mean MSE among six federated algorithms including FedAvg, FedProx, MOON, SCAFFOLD, and FedBN.
为了解决非独立同分布联邦学习环境下高维多模态数据特征提取困难的问题——本地数据稀缺且异构，FDRMFL提出一个统一的四项局部目标函数，结合MSE预测损失、互信息代理以保持目标相关性、对称KL散度惩罚以对齐跨模态潜在分布，以及InfoNCE对比损失以锚定局部表示到全局共识。在合成与真实近红外光谱数据集上的实验表明，各组件均提升性能，FDRMFL相较PCA降低均方误差33.8%，相较VAE降低43.0%，并在包括FedAvg、FedProx在内的六种联邦算法中取得最低整体均方误差。
ALER-TI: Aligned Latent Embedding Retrieval for Time Series Imputation
Authors: Xuan-Thong Truong, Trung-Kien Le, Tung Kieu, Thi-Thu Nguyen, Nhat-Hai Nguyen
First: 2026-07-08T16:59:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:59:38+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 2 figures, 12 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep learning has significantly advanced time series imputation, yet most existing architectures primarily rely on localized temporal context within the corrupted input sequence. This reliance can be limiting in real-world scenarios, where time series often exhibit non-stationary dynamics, weak temporal correlations, and infrequent patterns that are difficult to reconstruct from nearby observations alone. In this paper, we propose ALER-TI, Aligned Latent Embedding Retrieval for Time Series Imputation, a retrieval-augmented framework that explicitly leverages historical patterns to supplement degraded local context for more reliable missing-value reconstruction. The core of ALER-TI is Latent Embedding Alignment (LEA), which mitigates the representation mismatch between corrupted queries and complete historical candidates. By applying post-hoc masking in the latent space, LEA aligns candidates with the query's missingness pattern while allowing historical embeddings to be pre-computed and cached for efficient retrieval. ALER-TI is model-agnostic and can be integrated with various imputation backbones through a lightweight adaptation module. Extensive experiments on six real-world datasets under different missing rates demonstrate that ALER-TI consistently improves strong baseline models and enhances robustness across diverse imputation settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ALER-TI：用于时间序列填补的对齐潜在嵌入检索
深度学习显著推动了时间序列填补的发展，但现有架构大多仅依赖于受损输入序列内的局部时间上下文。这种依赖在现实场景中可能受限，因为时间序列常表现出非平稳动态、弱时间相关性和稀疏模式，难以仅凭邻近观测重建。本文提出ALER-TI（用于时间序列填补的对齐潜在嵌入检索），一种检索增强框架，通过显式利用历史模式补充受损局部上下文，以实现更可靠的缺失值重建。ALER-TI的核心是潜在嵌入对齐（LEA），通过在潜在空间中进行后处理掩码，缓解受损查询与完整历史候选之间的表示不匹配，同时允许历史嵌入预先计算并缓存以实现高效检索。ALER-TI模型无关，可通过轻量级适配模块集成到多种填补骨干网络中。在六个真实数据集、不同缺失率下的大量实验表明，ALER-TI持续提升强基线模型性能，并增强其在多样化填补设置中的鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of localized temporal context in deep learning-based time series imputation, especially under non-stationary and sparse patterns, ALER-TI introduces a retrieval-augmented framework that supplements corrupted input with historical patterns. Its core, Latent Embedding Alignment, aligns historical embeddings with the query’s missingness pattern via post-hoc masking in latent space, enabling efficient retrieval of relevant past sequences. Experiments across six real-world datasets show ALER-TI consistently outperforms strong baselines and improves robustness under varying missing rates, without requiring modifications to the underlying imputation model.
针对深度学习模型在时间序列填补中依赖局部时序上下文的局限性，尤其在非平稳和稀疏模式下表现不足，ALER-TI提出一种检索增强框架，通过历史模式补充局部上下文缺失。其核心机制“潜在嵌入对齐”（LEA）在潜在空间中通过后处理掩码对齐历史嵌入与损坏查询的缺失模式，同时支持预计算和缓存以提升效率。在六个真实数据集、不同缺失率下的实验表明，ALER-TI持续优于强基线模型，并在多种填补场景下提升鲁棒性。
SOMtime the World Ain$'$t Fair: Violating Fairness Using Self-Organizing Maps
Authors: Joseph Bingham, Netanel Arussy, Dvir Aran
First: 2026-02-20T13:25:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:55:51+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 2 figures, preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Unsupervised representations are widely assumed to be neutral with respect to sensitive attributes when those attributes are withheld from training. We show that this assumption is false. Using SOMtime, a topology-preserving representation method based on high-capacity Self-Organizing Maps, we demonstrate that sensitive attributes such as age and income emerge as dominant latent axes in purely unsupervised embeddings, even when explicitly excluded from the input. On two large-scale real-world datasets (the World Values Survey across five countries and the Census-Income dataset), SOMtime recovers monotonic orderings aligned with withheld sensitive attributes, achieving Spearman correlations of up to 0.85, whereas PCA and UMAP typically remain below 0.23 (with a single exception reaching 0.31), and against t-SNE and autoencoders which achieve at most 0.34. Furthermore, unsupervised segmentation of SOMtime embeddings produces demographically skewed clusters, demonstrating downstream fairness risks without any supervised task. These findings establish that \textit{fairness through unawareness} fails at the representation level for ordinal sensitive attributes and that fairness auditing must extend to unsupervised components of machine learning pipelines. We have made the code available at~ https://github.com/JosephBingham/SOMtime
中文标题/摘要
标题：有时世界并不公平：使用自组织映射违反公平性
当敏感属性从训练数据中排除时，普遍认为无监督表示对其是中立的。我们证明这一假设不成立。使用基于高容量自组织映射的拓扑保持表示方法SOMtime，我们表明，即使明确排除在输入中，敏感属性如年龄和收入仍会在纯无监督嵌入中成为主导的潜在轴。在两个大规模真实世界数据集（五个国家的世界价值观调查和人口普查收入数据集）上，SOMtime恢复了与排除敏感属性一致的单调排序，Spearman相关性高达0.85，而PCA和UMAP通常低于0.23（仅有一个例外达到0.31），与t-SNE和自编码器最高仅0.34相比。此外，SOMtime嵌入的无监督聚类会产生人口统计学偏斜的簇，表明在无监督任务下即存在下游公平性风险。这些发现表明，对于序数敏感属性，‘通过无意识实现公平’在表示层面失败，公平性审计必须扩展到机器学习流水线中的无监督组件。代码已发布于https://github.com/JosephBingham/SOMtime
Summary / 总结
This paper challenges the assumption that unsupervised representations remain neutral to sensitive attributes when those attributes are withheld from training. Using SOMtime, a topology-preserving method based on Self-Organizing Maps, the authors demonstrate that sensitive attributes like age and income emerge as dominant latent dimensions in purely unsupervised embeddings, even without explicit input. On two real-world datasets, SOMtime recovered monotonic orderings aligned with withheld sensitive attributes, achieving Spearman correlations up to 0.85, far exceeding PCA (≤0.23) and UMAP (≤0.31), and surpassing t-SNE and autoencoders (≤0.34). Additionally, unsupervised clustering of SOMtime embeddings produced demographically skewed clusters, revealing downstream fairness risks without any supervised task. These results show that fairness through unawareness fails at the representation level for ordinal sensitive attributes and necessitate extending fairness audits to unsupervised components of ML pipelines.
该论文质疑了当敏感属性从训练数据中剔除时，无监督表示仍能保持中立的假设。作者使用基于自组织映射的拓扑保持方法SOMtime，证明即使敏感属性（如年龄和收入）未被输入，它们仍会作为主导的潜在维度在纯无监督嵌入中浮现。在两个大规模真实数据集上，SOMtime在与敏感属性对齐的单调排序中达到高达0.85的斯皮尔曼相关系数，远超PCA和UMAP（通常低于0.23），并优于t-SNE和自编码器（最高仅0.34）。此外，对SOMtime嵌入的无监督聚类产生了人口统计学上偏斜的簇，揭示了无需监督任务即可引发的公平性风险，表明“通过无知实现公平”在有序敏感属性的表示层面失效，公平性审计必须涵盖无监督组件。
PB-OEL: A Performance-Bounded Online Ensemble Learning Framework With Mixed Feedback for Real-Time Safety Assessment
Authors: Songqiao Hu, Zeyi Liu, Lufeng Hao, Yinzhong Cheng, Xiao He
First: 2025-03-19T14:57:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:55:30+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-time safety assessment is critical for ensuring the reliable operation of complex dynamic systems. However, obtaining full safety labels in real time is often prohibitively expensive, resulting in a challenging mixed-feedback scenario dominated by partial feedback, especially under concept drift. Furthermore, existing online ensemble methods typically rely on heuristic weight allocation, lacking provable performance guarantees under such limited-feedback conditions. To address these challenges, we propose PB-OEL, a performance-bounded online ensemble learning framework designed for real-time safety assessment under mixed feedback. At the ensemble level, a theoretical framework is established to bound the performance of the ensemble classifier relative to its base classifiers across varying feedback ratios. By formally defining the form of expert advice, the bound guarantees that the ensemble outperforms any individual base classifier over a sufficiently large data stream. At the base-classifier level, a penalty-based update strategy is introduced, enabling base models to explicitly leverage misclassified samples rather than simply discarding them. Extensive experiments on the real-world Jiaolong manned submersible dataset demonstrate that PB-OEL maintains robust predictive performance and outperforms state-of-the-art methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PB-OEL：一种面向实时安全评估的混合反馈性能有界在线集成学习框架
实时安全评估对于确保复杂动态系统的可靠运行至关重要。然而，在实时获取完整安全标签往往成本过高，导致在概念漂移情况下以部分反馈为主导的混合反馈场景。此外，现有在线集成方法通常依赖启发式权重分配，在此类有限反馈条件下缺乏可证明的性能保证。为应对这些挑战，我们提出PB-OEL框架，专为混合反馈下的实时安全评估设计。在集成层面，建立理论框架以界定集成分类器相对于其基分类器在不同反馈比例下的性能上限；通过形式化专家建议的定义，该界保证在足够大的数据流上，集成性能优于任何单个基分类器。在基分类器层面，引入基于惩罚的更新策略，使基模型能够显式利用误分类样本而非简单丢弃。在真实世界蛟龙号载人潜水器数据集上的大量实验表明，PB-OEL保持了稳健的预测性能，并优于现有最先进方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for real-time safety assessment in dynamic systems where full feedback is costly and concept drift is common, PB-OEL introduces a performance-bounded online ensemble learning framework that handles mixed feedback. It establishes a theoretical guarantee bounding ensemble performance relative to base classifiers across varying feedback ratios, and employs a penalty-based update strategy at the base classifier level to utilize misclassified samples effectively. Experiments on the Jiaolong submersible dataset show PB-OEL maintains strong predictive performance and surpasses existing state-of-the-art methods.
为应对复杂动态系统实时安全评估中因标注成本高导致的混合反馈问题，特别是概念漂移下的部分反馈挑战，本文提出PB-OEL框架。该框架在理论层面建立性能上界，保证在不同反馈比例下，集成分类器优于任何单个基分类器；在基分类器层面，采用基于惩罚的更新策略，主动利用误分类样本提升学习效果。在蛟龙号载人潜水器真实数据集上的实验表明，PB-OEL在混合反馈下保持稳定性能并优于现有最先进方法。
Trust, but Don't Verify: Epistemic Blind Spots in LLM Source Evaluation
Authors: Rohan N. Pradhan, Steve Goley
First: 2026-06-03T20:15:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:55:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models increasingly act as epistemic proxies, synthesizing evidence from multiple sources to inform decisions. Whether they evaluate the quality of that evidence, or merely aggregate it based on surface presentation, remains poorly understood. We show that models possess the capability to detect fabricated statistics in isolation but do not recruit this capability during multi-source synthesis, producing similar numeric estimates whether the statistics are fabricated or valid. Specifically, source influence is governed by a methodology-register gate that responds to the distributional register of analytical text but not to numeric validity: for example, statistically impossible confidence intervals receive the same weight as valid ones. The behavioral dissociation replicates across six models from four families (Anthropic Claude, Qwen, OLMo, and OpenAI GPT-5.4) and three professional domains. Mechanistic analyses, including causal tracing, linear probes, and component-level attribution, converge on the same account: the model encodes and causally uses a methodology-register representation that transfers across domains, while numeric-validity signals, decodable in isolation, are suppressed to chance during multi-source synthesis. Prompting-based mitigations, even an oracle checklist naming the exact statistical checks, produce blanket skepticism rather than selective discernment, and the post-training pipelines we examine reinforce the shortcut without building numeric verification. Unlike sycophancy, which tracks user preference, this failure tracks whether a source presents as analytically credible, not whether its claims are consistent. We term this \textit{epistemic alignment}: like preference and safety alignment, the question is not capability but deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：信任但不验证：大语言模型源评估中的认识论盲点
语言模型日益充当认识论代理，从多个来源综合证据以辅助决策。它们是否评估证据质量，或仅依据表面呈现聚合证据，仍不甚明了。我们表明，模型虽能独立检测伪造统计数据，但在多源综合时却未调用此能力，无论统计数据是伪造还是真实，均产生相似的数值估计。具体而言，源影响力受方法论-语境门控机制调控，该机制响应分析性文本的分布语境，而非数值有效性：例如，统计上不可能的置信区间与有效区间获得同等权重。此行为分离在四个家族（Anthropic Claude、Qwen、OLMo、OpenAI GPT-5.4）的六个模型及三个专业领域中重复出现。机制分析（包括因果追溯、线性探针与组件级归因）均指向同一解释：模型编码并因果性使用跨领域迁移的方法论-语境表征，而可独立解码的数值有效性信号在多源综合时被压制至随机水平。基于提示的缓解措施，即使提供精确统计检验的“神谕清单”，也仅产生泛化怀疑而非选择性辨别，且我们考察的后训练流程强化了捷径而未构建数值验证机制。与迎合用户偏好不同，此失败追踪的是来源是否呈现分析可信度，而非其主张是否一致。我们称此为“认识论对齐”：与偏好和安全对齐类似，问题不在于能力，而在于部署。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how large language models evaluate source credibility during multi-source synthesis, the study investigates whether models detect fabricated statistics when aggregating evidence. The authors find that while models can identify invalid statistics in isolation, they fail to apply this capability during synthesis, assigning equal weight to fabricated and valid numeric claims. This behavior stems from a 'methodology-register gate' that responds to the stylistic register of analytical text rather than numeric validity, as demonstrated across six models from four families and three domains. Mechanistic analyses confirm that numeric validity signals are suppressed during synthesis, and prompting or post-training pipelines fail to restore selective discernment, instead inducing blanket skepticism. The phenomenon, termed 'epistemic alignment,' reflects a deployment failure—not a capability gap—where models align with perceived analytical credibility rather than factual accuracy.
该研究探讨了大语言模型（LLM）在多源信息合成时为何无法验证统计有效性，尽管它们能独立识别伪造数据。作者发现，模型更关注来源的‘方法论注册’——即其分析可信度，而非数值真实性，对不可能的置信区间与有效区间赋予同等权重。这一行为在六种模型（来自四个家族）和三个领域中一致出现，机制分析证实数值有效性信号在合成过程中被抑制。提示干预和后训练流程未能纠正此问题，反而强化了这一捷径。作者称此现象为‘认识论对齐’，强调问题不在于能力，而在于部署——模型对来源可信度的对齐，而非事实准确性。
An optimal control approach for neural network architecture adaptation with a posteriori error estimation
Authors: C G Krishnanunni, Thomas Scott, Tan Bui-Thanh
First: 2026-07-08T16:55:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:55:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work presents a novel approach for adapting neural network architecture along the depth based on a posteriori error estimation. By formulating neural network training as a continuous-time optimal control problem, we derive rigorous error estimates that quantify how approximation error distributes across network layers. This error decomposition enables a principled depth adaptation strategy: new layers are inserted at locations of maximum estimated error, allowing the network to efficiently capture complex, nonlinear variations in the underlying problem. Our framework introduces a novel network architecture that treats weights and biases as piecewise linear functions varying across layers, with the error estimator bounding the discrepancy between this discrete representation and the true continuous optimal control solution. The approach leverages dual weighted residual methodology from finite element analysis to derive computable upper bounds on the functional error. A key theoretical contribution is the derivation of explicit error bounds that decompose the total approximation error into interval-wise contributions, providing a rigorous basis for targeted architecture refinement. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our method on scientific datasets, including learning the observable-to-parameter map for the Navier-Stokes equation. Numerical results reveal that our approach consistently outperforms existing architecture adaptation methods in terms of generalization performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于后验误差估计的神经网络架构深度自适应的最优控制方法
本文提出了一种基于后验误差估计的神经网络架构深度自适应新方法。通过将神经网络训练建模为连续时间最优控制问题，我们推导出严格的误差估计，量化了近似误差在各网络层的分布。该误差分解支持了一种原理性的深度自适应策略：在估计误差最大的位置插入新层，使网络能高效捕捉底层问题的复杂非线性变化。我们的框架引入了一种新颖的网络架构，将权重和偏置视为在各层间分段线性变化的函数，误差估计器则界定了该离散表示与真实连续最优控制解之间的偏差。该方法借鉴有限元分析中的双重加权残差方法，推导出可计算的函数误差上界。一个关键理论贡献是推导出显式的误差界，将总近似误差分解为各区间贡献，为针对性架构优化提供了严谨基础。我们在科学数据集上验证了该方法的有效性，包括学习Navier-Stokes方程的可观测量到参数映射。数值结果表明，我们的方法在泛化性能上持续优于现有架构自适应方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient neural network architecture adaptation, this work formulates training as a continuous-time optimal control problem to enable a posteriori error estimation across network layers. By modeling weights and biases as piecewise linear functions and applying dual weighted residual techniques from finite element analysis, the method derives computable error bounds that decompose total approximation error into layer-specific contributions. Experimental results on scientific datasets, including learning the Navier-Stokes observable-to-parameter map, show that inserting new layers at locations of maximum estimated error leads to superior generalization performance compared to existing architecture adaptation methods.
为实现神经网络结构的高效自适应，该研究将训练建模为连续时间最优控制问题，通过后验误差估计量化各层误差分布。方法将权重与偏置建模为分段线性函数，并引入有限元分析中的双重加权残差技术，推导出可计算的误差上界，将总近似误差分解为各层贡献。在包括Navier-Stokes方程参数恢复等科学数据集上的实验表明，按高误差位置插入新层可显著提升泛化性能，优于现有结构自适应方法。
RIMRULE: Improving Tool-Using Language Agents via MDL-Guided Rule Learning
Authors: Xiang Gao, Yuguang Yao, Qi Zhang, Kaiwen Dong, Avinash Baidya, Ruocheng Guo, Hilaf Hasson, Kamalika Das
Venue: ACL 2026 long
First: 2025-12-31T19:40:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:51:12+00:00
Comments: Published as a long paper in the main conference of ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) often struggle to use tools reliably in domain-specific settings, where APIs may be idiosyncratic, under-documented, or tailored to private workflows. This highlights the need for effective adaptation to task-specific tools. We propose RIMRULE, a neuro-symbolic approach for LLM adaptation based on dynamic rule injection. Compact, interpretable rules are distilled from failure traces and injected into the prompt during inference to improve task performance. These rules are proposed by the LLM itself and consolidated using a Minimum Description Length (MDL) objective that favors generality and conciseness. Each rule is stored in both natural language and a structured symbolic form, supporting efficient retrieval at inference time. Experiments on tool-use benchmarks show that this approach improves accuracy on both seen and unseen tools without modifying LLM weights. It outperforms prompting-based adaptation methods and complements finetuning. Moreover, rules learned from one LLM can be reused to improve others, including long reasoning LLMs, highlighting the portability of symbolic knowledge across architectures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RIMRULE：通过MDL引导的规则学习提升工具使用语言代理性能
大语言模型（LLMs）在领域特定场景中常难以可靠使用工具，因API可能具有独特性、文档不足或专为私有工作流定制。这凸显了针对任务特定工具进行有效适应的必要性。我们提出RIMRULE，一种基于动态规则注入的神经符号方法，用于LLM适应。从失败轨迹中蒸馏出紧凑、可解释的规则，并在推理时注入提示以提升任务性能。这些规则由LLM自身提出，并通过最小描述长度（MDL）目标进行整合，该目标偏好通用性和简洁性。每条规则以自然语言和结构化符号形式存储，支持推理时高效检索。在工具使用基准测试中，该方法在不修改LLM权重的情况下提升了对已见和未见工具的准确率，优于基于提示的适应方法，并可与微调互补。此外，从一个LLM学习到的规则可复用于改进其他LLM，包括长推理LLM，凸显了符号知识在不同架构间的可移植性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of LLMs struggling with reliable tool usage in domain-specific settings due to idiosyncratic or under-documented APIs, RIMRULE introduces a neuro-symbolic method that adapts LLMs via dynamic rule injection. Rules are distilled from failure traces, proposed by the LLM, and consolidated using a Minimum Description Length (MDL) objective to favor general and concise rules. These rules, stored in both natural language and symbolic form, are injected into prompts during inference. Experiments show RIMRULE improves accuracy on both seen and unseen tools without altering LLM weights, outperforms prompting-based methods, complements fine-tuning, and enables cross-LLM rule transfer, demonstrating symbolic knowledge portability.
为了解决大语言模型在领域特定场景中因API特性不一或文档不足而难以可靠使用工具的问题，RIMRULE提出一种神经符号方法，通过动态规则注入实现模型适应。该方法从失败轨迹中蒸馏出简洁、可解释的规则，由模型自身生成并经最小描述长度（MDL）目标优化以提升通用性和简洁性。这些规则以自然语言和结构化符号形式存储，推理时注入提示中以提升性能。实验表明，该方法在不修改模型权重的情况下，提升对已见与未见工具的准确率，优于基于提示的适应方法，可与微调协同，且所学规则可跨模型迁移，体现符号知识的架构可移植性。
QCNN with Rough Path Signature Kernels
Authors: Leonardo Nogueira Falabella, Vasily Sazonov
First: 2026-07-08T16:50:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:50:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Time series analysis plays a vital role across a wide range of scientific and engineering domains but poses substantial computational challenges. A major difficulty arises from the time reparameterization invariance of time series data, which complicates the extraction of meaningful temporal features. In this work, we address the problem of time series classification by exploring the application of quantum computation techniques. We propose a hybrid quantum-classical architecture that integrates recent advances in quantum neural networks with the mathematical framework of path signatures, mitigating the impact of time reparametrization invariance. The architecture employs feature layers that compute a signature kernel between pairs of input paths, consisting of a reference path and a target path for classification, using either classical or quantum variational linear solvers (VQLS). These feature layers are followed by a Quantum Convolutional Neural Network (QCNN) to perform downstream learning tasks. We evaluate several realizations of the proposed architecture, differing in QCNN configurations, on a binary classification task involving time series representations of handwritten digits. Our experiments demonstrate the potential advantages of implementing path signature kernel layers within quantum circuits and provide an analysis of the computational limitations associated with the VQLS component.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于粗路径签名核的量子卷积神经网络
时间序列分析在众多科学与工程领域中发挥着关键作用，但面临显著的计算挑战。主要难点在于时间序列数据的时间重参数化不变性，这使得提取有意义的时间特征变得复杂。本文通过探索量子计算技术的应用，解决时间序列分类问题。我们提出一种混合量子-经典架构，结合量子神经网络的最新进展与路径签名的数学框架，缓解时间重参数化不变性的影响。该架构采用特征层，通过经典或量子变分线性求解器（VQLS）计算参考路径与目标路径（用于分类）之间的签名核。特征层后接量子卷积神经网络（QCNN）以执行下游学习任务。我们在手写数字的时间序列表示二分类任务中，评估了多种不同QCNN配置的架构实现。实验结果展示了在量子电路中实现路径签名核层的潜在优势，并分析了VQLS组件相关的计算限制。

Motivated by the computational challenges in time series analysis due to time reparameterization invariance, this work proposes a hybrid quantum-classical architecture that combines quantum neural networks with path signature kernels to extract invariant temporal features. The method computes signature kernels between reference and target paths using classical or quantum variational linear solvers, followed by a Quantum Convolutional Neural Network (QCNN) for classification. Experiments on binary classification of handwritten digit time series show that integrating path signature kernels within quantum circuits offers potential performance advantages, while also revealing computational bottlenecks in the variational linear solver component.
为应对时间序列分析中因时间重参数化不变性带来的计算挑战，本文提出一种混合量子-经典架构用于时间序列分类。该方法结合量子神经网络与路径签名核，通过经典或量子变分线性求解器计算参考路径与目标路径间的签名核以提取不变时序特征，随后由量子卷积神经网络（QCNN）进行下游分类。实验在手写数字时间序列二分类任务中表明，将路径签名核嵌入量子电路可带来性能优势，同时揭示了变分线性求解器相关的计算瓶颈。
VFM-Loc: Training-Free Cross-View Geo-Localization via Aligning Discriminative Visual Hierarchies
Authors: Jun Lu, Zehao Sang, Haoqi Wei, Xiangyun Liu, Kun Zhu, Haitao Guo, Zhihui Gong, Lei Ding
First: 2026-03-14T09:23:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:47:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Cross-View Geo-Localization (CVGL) in remote sensing aims to locate a drone-view query by matching it to geo-tagged satellite images. Although supervised methods have achieved strong results on close-set benchmarks, they often fail to generalize to unconstrained, real-world scenarios due to severe viewpoint differences and dataset bias. To overcome these limitations, we present VFM-Loc, a training-free CVGL framework that leverages the generalizable visual representations from vision foundational models (VFMs). VFM-Loc identifies and matches discriminative visual clues across different viewpoints through a progressive alignment strategy. First, we design a hierarchical clue extraction mechanism using Generalized Mean pooling and Scale-Weighted R-MAC to preserve distinctive visual clues across scales while maintaining hierarchical confidence. Second, we introduce a statistical manifold alignment pipeline based on domain-wise PCA and Orthogonal Procrustes analysis, linearly aligning heterogeneous feature distributions in a shared metric space. Experiments demonstrate that VFM-Loc exhibits high accuracy on standard benchmarks and surpasses supervised methods by over 20\% in Recall@1 on the challenging LO-UCV dataset with large oblique angles. This work highlights that principled alignment of pre-trained features can effectively bridge the cross-view gap, establishing a robust and training-free paradigm for real-world CVGL. The relevant code is made available at: github.com/DingLei14/VFM-Loc.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VFM-Loc：通过对齐判别性视觉层次实现无训练跨视角地理定位
遥感中的跨视角地理定位（CVGL）旨在通过匹配无人机视角查询与带有地理标签的卫星图像来定位目标。尽管监督方法在封闭集基准上表现优异，但常因视角差异显著和数据集偏差而难以泛化到无约束的真实场景。为克服这些限制，我们提出VFM-Loc，一种无训练的CVGL框架，利用视觉基础模型（VFMs）提供的可泛化视觉表示。VFM-Loc通过渐进式对齐策略识别并匹配不同视角下的判别性视觉线索。首先，我们设计基于广义均值池化与尺度加权R-MAC的层次线索提取机制，以在多尺度下保留独特视觉线索并维持层次置信度。其次，我们引入基于域内PCA与正交普罗克斯特分析的统计流形对齐流程，将异质特征分布线性对齐至共享度量空间。实验表明，VFM-Loc在标准基准上表现高精度，并在具有大倾斜角挑战性的LO-UCV数据集上以Recall@1指标超越监督方法20%以上。本工作表明，预训练特征的原理性对齐可有效弥合跨视角差距，建立稳健且无训练的现实世界CVGL范式。相关代码见：github.com/DingLei14/VFM-Loc。

Motivated by the poor generalization of supervised methods in real-world cross-view geo-localization due to viewpoint differences and dataset bias, VFM-Loc introduces a training-free framework that aligns discriminative visual hierarchies from vision foundational models. It extracts multi-scale visual clues via Generalized Mean pooling and Scale-Weighted R-MAC, then aligns heterogeneous feature distributions using domain-wise PCA and Orthogonal Procrustes analysis in a shared metric space. Experiments show VFM-Loc achieves high accuracy on standard benchmarks and outperforms supervised methods by over 20% in Recall@1 on the challenging LO-UCV dataset with large oblique angles, demonstrating that principled alignment of pre-trained features effectively bridges the cross-view gap.
为解决监督方法在真实世界跨视角地理定位中因视角差异和数据偏差导致的泛化能力不足问题，VFM-Loc 提出一种无需训练的框架，利用视觉基础模型对无人机与卫星图像中的判别性视觉层次进行对齐。该方法首先通过广义均值池化与尺度加权R-MAC构建层次化视觉线索提取机制，保留跨尺度的显著视觉特征；随后采用基于域内PCA与正交普罗克斯特分析的统计流形对齐流程，将异构特征分布线性映射至共享度量空间。实验表明，VFM-Loc 在标准基准上表现优异，并在具有大倾斜角挑战性的LO-UCV数据集上以超过20%的Recall@1超越监督方法，证明预训练特征的原理性对齐可有效弥合跨视角差距，无需训练即可实现鲁棒的地理定位。
Future Confidence Distillation in Large Language Models
Authors: Sahil Kale
First: 2026-07-08T16:43:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:43:11+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable confidence estimation is essential for deploying large language models (LLMs) in confidence-aware systems, where downstream decisions such as retrieval, tool use, and adaptive computation depend on accurately estimating answer reliability. Existing approaches, however, largely treat confidence as a property of completed responses, overlooking how confidence-related information evolves throughout the answering process. In this work, we investigate confidence from a temporal perspective by comparing pre-solution Feeling-of-Knowing (FOK) and post-solution Judgement-of-Learning (JOL) confidence estimates across frontier and open-source LLMs. We show that post-solution confidence is consistently better calibrated and more discriminative than pre-solution confidence, while linear probes trained on hidden representations recover substantially richer confidence-related information than models explicitly verbalise. Building on this observation, we introduce future confidence distillation, which trains predictors operating on pre-solution hidden representations using teacher confidence estimates produced by post-solution correctness probes. Despite requiring only pre-solution representations for inference, distilled predictors recover much of the calibration improvement achieved by post-solution confidence, remain highly sample efficient, and transfer across datasets within the same domain. Together, our findings demonstrate that confidence-related information evolves throughout the answering process and can be anticipated before answer generation is complete, enabling significantly more reliable yet low-cost confidence estimation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型中的未来置信度蒸馏
可靠的置信度估计对于在置信度感知系统中部署大语言模型（LLMs）至关重要，因为下游决策（如检索、工具使用和自适应计算）依赖于准确估计答案的可靠性。然而，现有方法大多将置信度视为完成响应的属性，忽视了置信度相关信息在回答过程中的演变。本研究从时间角度考察置信度，比较前沿和开源LLMs在回答前的“感觉知道”（FOK）和回答后的“学习判断”（JOL）置信度估计。我们发现，回答后的置信度比回答前的置信度更准确校准且更具区分性，而基于隐藏表示训练的线性探针比显式表达置信度的模型能恢复更丰富的置信度相关信息。基于此观察，我们提出未来置信度蒸馏：利用回答后正确性探针生成的教师置信度估计，训练在回答前隐藏表示上操作的预测器。尽管推理仅需回答前表示，蒸馏预测器仍能恢复大部分回答后置信度带来的校准改进，保持高度样本效率，并在相同领域内跨数据集迁移。我们的研究结果表明，置信度相关信息在回答过程中持续演变，可在答案生成完成前进行预测，从而实现更可靠且低成本的置信度估计。

The authors compare pre-solution Feeling-of-Knowing and post-solution Judgement-of-Learning confidence estimates across multiple LLMs, finding that post-solution confidence is more accurate and discriminative. They further show that linear probes trained on hidden representations capture richer confidence signals than explicit verbalized confidence. 该论文旨在提升大语言模型的置信度估计可靠性，通过分析答案生成过程中置信度的时序演化。研究对比了生成前的“感觉知道”与生成后的“学习判断”置信度，发现后者更准确且更具区分度。进一步表明，基于隐藏状态的线性探针能捕捉比显式置信度更丰富的置信信息。在此基础上，提出“未来置信度蒸馏”方法：以生成后正确性为教师信号，训练作用于生成前隐藏状态的预测器。蒸馏模型在保持低样本开销和跨数据集迁移能力的同时，显著提升了置信度校准效果，证明置信度可在答案生成完成前可靠预测。
Bifidelity Parameter Estimation Using Conditional Diffusion Models
Authors: Caroline Tatsuoka, Minglei Yang, Dongbin Xiu, Guannan Zhang
First: 2025-04-02T16:54:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:41:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a bifidelity method for uncertainty quantification of parameter estimates in complex systems, leveraging generative models trained to sample the target conditional distribution. In the Bayesian inference setting, traditional parameter estimation methods rely on repeated simulations of potentially expensive forward models to determine the posterior distribution of the parameter values, which may result in computationally intractable workflows. Furthermore, methods such as Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) necessitate rerunning the entire algorithm for each new data observation, further increasing the computational burden. Hence, we propose a novel method for efficiently obtaining posterior distributions of parameter estimates for high-fidelity models given data observations of interest. The method first constructs a low-fidelity, conditional generative model capable of amortized Bayesian inference and hence rapid posterior density approximation over a wide-range of data observations. When higher accuracy is needed for a specific data observation, the method employs adaptive refinement of the density approximation. It uses outputs from the low-fidelity generative model to refine the parameter sampling space, ensuring efficient use of the computationally expensive high-fidelity solver. Subsequently, a high-fidelity, unconditional generative model is trained to achieve greater accuracy in the target posterior distribution. Both low- and high- fidelity generative models enable efficient sampling from the target posterior and do not require repeated simulation of the high-fidelity forward model. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method on several numerical examples, including cases with multi-modal densities, as well as an application in plasma physics for a runaway electron simulation model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用条件扩散模型的双保真参数估计
我们提出了一种用于复杂系统参数估计不确定性量化的新双保真方法，该方法利用训练好的生成模型采样目标条件分布。在贝叶斯推断框架下，传统参数估计方法依赖于重复模拟可能昂贵的正向模型以确定参数值的后验分布，可能导致计算不可行。此外，如马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）等方法需对每个新观测数据重新运行整个算法，进一步增加计算负担。因此，我们提出了一种高效获取高保真模型参数后验分布的新方法。该方法首先构建一个低保真条件生成模型，实现可扩展的贝叶斯推断，从而快速逼近广泛数据观测下的后验密度。当特定数据观测需要更高精度时，方法采用密度逼近的自适应细化。它利用低保真生成模型的输出来细化参数采样空间，确保高效利用计算昂贵的高保真求解器。随后，训练一个高保真无条件生成模型，以在目标后验分布中实现更高精度。低保真和高保真生成模型均能高效采样目标后验分布，无需重复模拟高保真正向模型。我们在多个数值示例中验证了该方法的有效性，包括多模态密度情况，以及等离子体物理中用于失控电子模拟模型的应用。

Motivated by the computational expense of traditional Bayesian inference methods that require repeated high-fidelity simulations, this work introduces a bifidelity approach using conditional diffusion models to efficiently estimate parameter posteriors. The method first trains a low-fidelity conditional generative model to enable amortized inference and rapid posterior approximation across diverse data observations. For specific cases needing higher accuracy, it adaptively refines the posterior using outputs from the low-fidelity model to guide sampling and reduce reliance on expensive high-fidelity simulations. A second high-fidelity unconditional model is then trained to improve accuracy without requiring repeated high-fidelity runs. Experimental results across numerical benchmarks—including multi-modal distributions—and a plasma physics runaway electron model confirm the method’s efficiency and accuracy in approximating posterior distributions without repeated costly simulations.
为解决传统贝叶斯推断方法因反复调用高保真模拟而计算成本过高的问题，本文提出一种基于条件扩散模型的双保真参数估计方法。该方法首先训练一个低保真条件生成模型，实现参数后验分布的快速近似与跨数据观测的 amortized 推断；当特定观测需要更高精度时，利用低保真模型输出引导高保真模型的采样空间，避免重复调用昂贵的高保真求解器。随后训练一个高保真无条件生成模型以进一步提升后验精度。实验在多个数值基准（含多模态分布）及等离子体物理中的 runaway 电子模拟中验证，结果表明该方法在保持后验精度的同时显著降低计算开销。
Higher-Order Geometric Updates for Levenberg-Marquardt Method via Riemann Normal Coordinates
Authors: Jianing Liu, Dong H. Zhang
First: 2026-07-08T16:39:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:39:03+00:00
Comments: 34 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Nonlinear least-squares optimization is central to regression, physics-informed neural networks, and other machine-learning tasks. Such problems have a natural geometric interpretation, model predictions form a manifold in data space, while the chosen parameterization can introduce parameter-effects curvature that becomes a dominant source of nonlinearity. This exposes a limitation of the Levenberg-Marquardt (LM) method, its tangent-space step is applied as a straight update in parameter coordinates. Geodesic acceleration gives a second-order correction, but its removal of parameter-effect curvature is exact only in the infinitesimal-step limit. We propose a Riemann-normal-coordinate Levenberg-Marquardt method (RNC-LM) to improve this consistency for finite optimization steps. By reformulating the geodesic equation, RNC-LM extends geodesic acceleration to arbitrary-order corrections and constructs finite-step updates with progressively higher reparameterization consistency. A line search along the resulting RNC curve controls the traveled distance while keeping the cost close to standard LM. The method eliminates the tangential component of residual acceleration order by order in a moving tangent frame, making the actual objective reduction more consistent with the linear model prediction of LM. On classical nonlinear least-squares benchmarks, RNC-LM improves convergence and robustness in curved valleys and rank-deficient problems. On a reaction-diffusion PINN failure-mode benchmark, it reduces the relative L2 error to the order of 1e-3 and recovers a physically meaningful solution. On a large-scale machine-learning potential-energy-surface fitting task, it achieves a 34-fold speedup over standard LM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过黎曼正交坐标实现Levenberg-Marquardt方法的高阶几何更新
非线性最小二乘优化是回归、物理信息神经网络及其他机器学习任务的核心。此类问题具有天然的几何解释：模型预测在数据空间中形成流形，而所选参数化会引入参数效应曲率，成为非线性主要来源。这揭示了Levenberg-Marquardt（LM）方法的局限性：其切空间步长在参数坐标中以直线更新。测地线加速提供二阶修正，但其消除参数效应曲率仅在无穷小步长极限下精确。我们提出黎曼正交坐标Levenberg-Marquardt方法（RNC-LM），以提升有限优化步长下的这种一致性。通过重构测地线方程，RNC-LM将测地线加速扩展为任意阶修正，并构建具有逐步更高重参数化一致性的有限步长更新。沿所得RNC曲线的线搜索控制行进距离，同时保持成本接近标准LM。该方法在移动切向坐标系中逐阶消除残差加速度的切向分量，使实际目标减少更一致地符合LM的线性模型预测。在经典非线性最小二乘基准测试中，RNC-LM提升了在曲谷和秩亏问题中的收敛性和鲁棒性。在反应扩散PINN失效模式基准测试中，其将相对L2误差降至1e-3量级并恢复物理意义解。在大规模机器学习势能面拟合任务中，其相较标准LM提速34倍。

Motivated by the geometric nature of nonlinear least-squares problems, where parameterization curvature limits the accuracy of standard Levenberg-Marquardt updates, this paper introduces RNC-LM—a method that applies higher-order geometric corrections via Riemann normal coordinates. By reformulating the geodesic equation, RNC-LM constructs finite-step updates that progressively eliminate parameter-effect curvature, improving consistency with the linearized model. Experimental results show RNC-LM enhances convergence and robustness in curved valleys and rank-deficient cases, reduces L2 error in physics-informed neural networks to 1e-3, and achieves a 34-fold speedup in large-scale potential-energy surface fitting compared to standard LM.
为解决Levenberg-Marquardt方法在非线性最小二乘问题中因参数效应曲率导致的局限性，本文提出RNC-LM方法，利用黎曼正交坐标构建高阶有限步更新。通过重写测地线方程，RNC-LM在移动切空间框架中逐阶消除残差加速度的切向分量，提升与LM线性模型预测的一致性。实验表明，在经典基准上收敛性与鲁棒性显著提升，尤其在曲谷与秩亏问题中；在PINN反应扩散问题上，L2误差降至1e-3并恢复物理解；在大规模机器学习势能面拟合任务中，速度提升34倍。
Continuous and large-scale: ELEANOR, the soft architected arm inspired by the elephant trunk
Authors: Giovanna A. Naselli, Anderson B. Nardin, Seonggun Joe, Ryan Drinkwater, Enrico Donato, Diego Bianchi, Egidio Falotico, Michel C. Milinkovitch, Lucia Beccai
First: 2026-07-08T16:38:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:38:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The elephant trunk is a dexterous and versatile manipulator whose performance is still unmatched in robotics. In previous works, modularity was prioritized and relatively small-scale continuum robots were built. We take the natural proboscis of the *Loxodonta africana* species as a model and propose a different design approach which favors structural continuity and dynamic properties that plausibly emulate those of the natural trunk, while conferring high adaptability to the environment and humans. Instead of targeting prescribed behaviors, we show that a biomimetic design based on the macroscopic properties of the natural system enables elephant-like movements and grasping. We build by 3D printing an 85 cm long, compliant, tapered, volumetrically tessellated continuum arm, which is combined with tendon-driven actuation mimicking the longitudinal and oblique muscles of the natural model. We demonstrate whole-body grasping of objects having different shapes and dimensions and discuss a comparison to the biological trunk highlighting aspects of both biology and robotics.

受象鼻卓越的灵巧性与适应性启发，本研究提出一种以结构连续性为核心的设计方法，而非模块化，旨在模拟象鼻的动态特性。该85厘米长的3D打印柔性臂具有锥形、体积分割的结构，通过模拟象鼻纵向与斜肌的肌腱驱动系统实现运动。实验表明，该臂可完成不同形状和尺寸物体的全身抓取，验证了仿生设计的有效性，同时揭示了生物机制与机器人实现之间的对比与权衡。
Effective Strategies for Asynchronous Software Engineering Agents
Authors: Jiayi Geng, Graham Neubig
First: 2026-03-23T02:26:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:30:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI agents have become increasingly capable at isolated software engineering (SWE) tasks such as resolving issues on Github. Yet long-horizon tasks involving multiple interdependent subtasks still pose challenges both with respect to accuracy, and with respect to timely completion. A natural approach to solving these long-horizon tasks in a timely manner is asynchronous multi-agent collaboration, where multiple agents work on different parts of the task at the same time. But effective application of multi-agent systems has proven surprisingly difficult: concurrent edits by multiple agents interfere with each other, dependencies are difficult to synchronize, and combining partial progress into a coherent whole is challenging. On the other hand, human developers have long relied on mature collaboration infrastructure to manage these challenges in large software projects. Inspired by these collaboration primitives, we introduce Centralized Asynchronous Isolated Delegation (CAID), a structured multi-agent coordination paradigm grounded in three core SWE primitives: centralized task delegation, asynchronous execution, and isolated workspaces. CAID constructs dependency-aware task plans through a central manager, executes subtasks concurrently in isolated workspaces, and consolidates progress via structured integration with executable test-based verification. In empirical evaluation, we find that CAID improves accuracy over single-agent baselines by 25.6% absolute on paper reproduction tasks (PaperBench) and 14.7% on Python library development tasks (Commit0). Through systematic analysis, we find that branch-and-merge is a central coordination mechanism for multi-agent collaboration, and that SWE primitives such as git worktree, git commit, and git merge enable it to be realized in a reliable and executable manner.
中文标题/摘要
标题：异步软件工程代理的有效策略
AI代理在孤立的软件工程（SWE）任务（如解决GitHub上的问题）方面能力日益增强。然而，涉及多个相互依赖子任务的长期任务在准确性和及时完成方面仍面临挑战。一种自然的解决方法是异步多代理协作，多个代理同时处理任务的不同部分。但多代理系统的有效应用却异常困难：多个代理的并发编辑会相互干扰，依赖关系难以同步，将部分进展整合为一个连贯整体也颇具挑战。另一方面，人类开发者长期以来依赖成熟的协作基础设施来管理大型软件项目中的这些挑战。受这些协作原语启发，我们引入了集中式异步隔离委托（CAID），这是一种基于三个核心SWE原语的结构化多代理协调范式：集中式任务委托、异步执行和隔离工作区。CAID通过中央管理器构建依赖感知的任务计划，使子任务在隔离工作区中并发执行，并通过结构化集成与可执行测试验证来整合进展。实证评估表明，CAID在论文复现任务（PaperBench）上比单代理基线方法绝对准确率提升25.6%，在Python库开发任务（Commit0）上提升14.7%。通过系统性分析，我们发现分支与合并是多代理协作的核心协调机制，而SWE原语如git worktree、git commit和git merge使其得以可靠且可执行地实现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of coordinating multiple AI agents for long-horizon software engineering tasks, this paper introduces CAID, a structured multi-agent paradigm inspired by human collaboration practices. CAID employs centralized task delegation, asynchronous execution, and isolated workspaces to manage interdependent subtasks without interference. A central manager generates dependency-aware plans, agents execute tasks concurrently in isolated environments, and progress is integrated via executable test-based verification. Experiments show CAID improves accuracy by 25.6% on PaperBench and 14.7% on Commit0 compared to single-agent baselines, with branch-and-merge mechanisms identified as critical for reliable multi-agent coordination.
为解决涉及相互依赖子任务的长周期软件工程任务，本文提出CAID框架，该框架受人类协作实践启发，采用集中任务委派、异步执行与隔离工作区机制，通过中央管理者构建依赖感知计划，并借助可执行测试验证整合各子任务进展。实验表明，CAID在PaperBench任务上比单智能体基线准确率提升25.6%，在Commit0任务上提升14.7%，验证了分支合并机制及git相关工具（如git worktree、git commit、git merge）在实现可靠多智能体协作中的核心作用。
Towards Agentic AI Governance: A Preliminary Assessment
Authors: Mubarak Raji, Masooda Bashir
First: 2026-07-08T16:29:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:29:18+00:00
Comments: International Conference on the AI Revolution: Research, Ethics, and Society (AIR-RES 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artificial intelligence is rapidly evolving from generative systems to agentic AI capable of autonomously planning and executing tasks. Widely characterized as the Year of Agentic AI, 2025 marked accelerated development and deployment, introducing new ethical and governance challenges. This paper presents a systematic review of the emerging literature on agentic AI governance. Our analysis identifies features that distinguish agentic AI from traditional systems and why it warrants targeted governance attention. We synthesize prevailing governance priorities, proposed mechanisms, and stakeholder roles shaping this evolving domain. As an initial scholarly effort, this review lays the preliminary groundwork for developing a structured roadmap to guide responsible and adaptive agentic AI governance.

Asymmetric Focal Loss Improves Graph Neural Network Prediction of Drug-Drug Interactions
Authors: Faranak Hatami, Mousa Moradi
First: 2026-07-08T16:28:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:28:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Background: Graph neural networks improve computational prediction of polypharmacy side effects, but standard binary cross-entropy training allocates equal capacity to well-classified and difficult examples, potentially missing clinically significant interactions. We evaluated whether an asymmetric focal objective could improve multi-relational drug-drug interaction (DDI) prediction by emphasizing difficult positive interactions. Methods: ClinicalFocal loss was integrated into a relation-aware graph convolutional network using molecular fingerprints, physicochemical descriptors, and learned embeddings. The model was evaluated on TWOSIDES using five-fold cross-validation with identical experimental conditions (architecture, features, data partitions, hyperparameters, and random seeds) for ClinicalFocal loss and binary cross-entropy baseline. Results: ClinicalFocal loss increased accuracy from 0.699 to 0.892 (+19.3 percentage points) and F1 score from 0.700 to 0.894 (+19.4 percentage points). AUROC increased from 0.766 to 0.914, and AUCPR increased from 0.714 to 0.860. The false-negative rate decreased from 29.8% to 9.1%, while specificity increased from 69.6% to 87.5%. Overall classification error decreased from 30.1% to 10.8%, corresponding to a 64.1% relative reduction. Improvements were consistent across all five folds. Conclusions: Asymmetric focal optimization improved classification and ranking performance while achieving 90.9% recall for observed interaction triples, without modifying the underlying architecture. Loss-function design is a direct, tunable lever for improving graph-based DDI prediction.
