标题：不对称焦点损失提升图神经网络对药物-药物相互作用的预测
背景：图神经网络可改善多药联合副作用的计算预测，但标准二元交叉熵训练对易分类和困难样本分配等容量，可能遗漏具有临床意义的相互作用。我们评估了不对称焦点损失是否能通过强调困难阳性相互作用，提升多关系药物-药物相互作用（DDI）预测。方法：将临床焦点损失整合进关系感知图卷积网络，使用分子指纹、理化描述符和学习嵌入。模型在TWOSIDES数据集上使用五折交叉验证评估，临床焦点损失与二元交叉熵基线在架构、特征、数据划分、超参数和随机种子等实验条件完全一致。结果：临床焦点损失使准确率从0.699提升至0.892（+19.3个百分点），F1分数从0.700提升至0.894（+19.4个百分点）。AUROC从0.766提升至0.914，AUCPR从0.714提升至0.860。假阴性率从29.8%降至9.1%，特异性从69.6%升至87.5%。总体分类错误率从30.1%降至10.8%，相对减少64.1%。五折结果一致。结论：不对称焦点优化提升了分类与排序性能，同时在观察到的相互作用三元组上达到90.9%召回率，且未修改底层架构。损失函数设计是直接、可调的杠杆，可提升基于图的DDI预测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of standard binary cross-entropy in handling imbalanced and clinically critical drug-drug interaction predictions, the study introduced an asymmetric focal loss to prioritize hard positive examples. Implemented within a relation-aware graph convolutional network using molecular fingerprints and physicochemical features, the method was rigorously compared against a baseline under identical conditions across five folds. Results showed significant performance gains: accuracy rose from 0.699 to 0.892, F1 score from 0.700 to 0.894, AUROC from 0.766 to 0.914, and AUCPR from 0.714 to 0.860, with false-negative rate dropping from 29.8% to 9.1% and overall error decreasing by 64.1% relative. These improvements were consistent across all folds, demonstrating that loss function tuning alone can substantially enhance graph-based DDI prediction without altering model architecture.
为解决标准二元交叉熵在处理药物-药物相互作用预测中对难例关注不足的问题，本研究引入不对称焦点损失以提升图神经网络性能。方法将ClinicalFocal损失集成至基于关系感知的图卷积网络，结合分子指纹、理化描述符和学习嵌入，同时保持与基线模型完全一致的实验条件。在TWOSIDES数据集上的实验表明：准确率从0.699提升至0.892，F1分数从0.700升至0.894，AUROC从0.766增至0.914，AUCPR从0.714提升至0.860；假阴性率从29.8%降至9.1%，特异性从69.6%升至87.5%，整体分类错误率下降64.1%，五折交叉验证结果保持一致。
Dual Latent Memory in Vision-Language-Action Models for Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Hongyu Qu, Jianzhe Gao, Xiaobin Hu, Shaohuan Yang, Xinlei Yu, Rui Yan, Wenguan Wang, Xiangbo Shu, Shuicheng Yan
First: 2026-07-08T16:26:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:26:06+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://github.com/quhongyu/LaMem-VLA
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Mainstream Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models predict actions primarily from the current observation under a Markovian assumption, thus struggling with long-horizon, temporally dependent tasks. Existing memory-augmented VLAs either expand the observation window or retrieve history from the memory bank as auxiliary policy-side context. However, they leave memory outside the native latent embedding space of VLA reasoning, preventing historical experience from being fluidly interleaved with multimodal reasoning and action formation. To this end, we introduce LaMem-VLA, a latent-memory-native framework that reconstructs historical experience into latent memory tokens and directly interweaves them with VLA reasoning. At its core, LaMem-VLA introduces four coordinated components: (i) a curator that organizes historical experience into two complementary short-term and long-term memory vaults; (ii) a seeker that queries both vaults using the multimodal cognition to retrieve context-relevant evidence; (iii) a condenser that reconstructs the retrieved evidence into compact short-term and long-term latent memory tokens; and (iv) a weaver that injects these memory tokens with the current observation and instruction into one continuous embedding sequence. By representing, retrieving, and consuming historical experience entirely in the same continuous latent space, LaMem-VLA enables memory to directly participate in VLA reasoning and guide action generation under a bounded context. Extensive experiments on SimplerEnv and LIBERO demonstrate the superiority of our LaMem-VLA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于机器人操作的视觉-语言-动作模型中的双潜在记忆
主流视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在马尔可夫假设下主要从当前观测预测动作，因此难以处理长时程、时间相关的任务。现有增强记忆的VLA模型要么扩展观测窗口，要么从记忆库中检索历史作为辅助策略上下文。然而，它们将记忆置于VLA推理的原生潜在嵌入空间之外，阻碍了历史经验与多模态推理及动作生成的流畅交织。为此，我们提出了LaMem-VLA，一种原生支持潜在记忆的框架，将历史经验重构为潜在记忆令牌，并直接将其与VLA推理交织。其核心包含四个协同组件：(i) 一个策展人，将历史经验组织为互补的短期和长期记忆库；(ii) 一个检索器，利用多模态认知查询两个库以检索上下文相关证据；(iii) 一个压缩器，将检索到的证据重构为紧凑的短期和长期潜在记忆令牌；(iv) 一个编织器，将这些记忆令牌与当前观测和指令注入为一个连续的嵌入序列。通过在相同连续潜在空间中表示、检索和消耗历史经验，LaMem-VLA使记忆能直接参与VLA推理并在有限上下文中引导动作生成。在SimplerEnv和LIBERO上的大量实验验证了LaMem-VLA的优越性。
Summary / 总结
To address the limitation of mainstream Vision-Language-Action models in handling long-horizon tasks due to their Markovian assumption, LaMem-VLA introduces a latent-memory-native framework that embeds historical experience directly into the model’s latent space. It uses four coordinated components: a curator to split memory into short- and long-term vaults, a seeker to retrieve relevant context, a condenser to compress it into latent tokens, and a weaver to interleave these tokens with current inputs. Experiments on SimplerEnv and LIBERO show that this approach significantly improves performance over baseline models by enabling seamless integration of memory into multimodal reasoning and action generation.
为了解决主流视觉-语言-动作模型在长时序任务中因马尔可夫假设导致的性能不足，本文提出LaMem-VLA框架，将历史经验直接嵌入VLA推理的潜在空间。该框架包含四个协同模块：记忆整理器将历史经验划分为短期与长期记忆库，检索器根据多模态认知查询相关上下文，压缩器将其转化为紧凑的潜在记忆令牌，编织器将这些令牌与当前输入融合为统一嵌入序列。在SimplerEnv和LIBERO上的实验表明，LaMem-VLA显著优于现有记忆增强模型，实现了记忆与推理的无缝融合及更有效的动作生成。
Optimization-Embedded Active Multi-Fidelity Surrogate Learning for Multi-Condition Airfoil Shape Optimization
Authors: Isaac Robledo, Alberto Vilariño, Arnau Miró, Oriol Lehmkuhl, Carlos Sanmiguel Vila, Rodrigo Castellanos
First: 2026-03-17T18:43:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:25:43+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 18 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Active multi-fidelity surrogate modeling is developed for multi-condition airfoil shape optimization to reduce high-fidelity CFD cost while retaining RANS-consistent aerodynamic metrics. The framework couples a low-fidelity-informed Gaussian process regression transfer model with uncertainty-triggered sampling and a synchronized elitism rule embedded in a hybrid genetic algorithm. Low-fidelity XFOIL evaluations provide inexpensive features, while sparse RANS simulations are adaptively allocated when predictive uncertainty exceeds a threshold; elite candidates are mandatorily validated at high fidelity, and the population is re-evaluated to prevent evolutionary selection based on outdated fitness values produced by earlier surrogate states. The method is demonstrated for a two-point problem at $Re=6\times10^6$ with cruise at $α=2^\circ$ (maximize $E=L/D$) and take-off at $α=10^\circ$ (maximize $C_L$) using a 12-parameter CST representation. Independent multi-fidelity surrogates per flight condition enable decoupled refinement. The optimized design improves cruise efficiency by 41.05% and take-off lift by 20.75% relative to the best first-generation individual. Over the full campaign, RANS evaluations were required for only 14.78% and 9.5% of the condition-specific candidate evaluations at cruise and take-off, respectively. These percentages quantify the reduction in high-fidelity usage relative to the fixed automated RANS workflow adopted as the high-fidelity reference in this study.
中文标题/摘要
标题：嵌入优化的主动多保真度代理模型学习用于多工况机翼形状优化
为降低高保真度CFD计算成本同时保留与RANS一致的气动指标，开发了用于多工况机翼形状优化的主动多保真度代理模型。该框架结合了低保真度信息引导的高斯过程回归迁移模型、不确定性触发采样及嵌入于混合遗传算法中的同步精英规则。低保真度XFOIL评估提供低成本特征，当预测不确定性超过阈值时，稀疏RANS仿真将自适应分配；精英候选体必须在高保真度下强制验证，种群重新评估以防止基于早期代理状态生成的过时适应度值进行进化选择。该方法在Re=6×10⁶条件下，巡航α=2°（最大化E=L/D）和起飞α=10°（最大化CL）的两点问题中进行了演示，采用12参数CST表示法。每飞行工况独立的多保真度代理模型可实现解耦优化。优化设计相比第一代最优个体，巡航效率提升41.05%，起飞升力提升20.75%。在整个优化过程中，巡航和起飞工况下仅需14.78%和9.5%的条件特定候选体评估采用RANS计算，这些比例量化了相对于本研究中作为高保真度基准的固定自动化RANS工作流，高保真度计算的减少量。
Summary / 总结
To reduce computational cost in multi-condition airfoil optimization while preserving aerodynamic accuracy, this study proposes an active multi-fidelity surrogate framework that integrates a low-fidelity-informed Gaussian process model with uncertainty-triggered sampling and an elitism rule within a hybrid genetic algorithm. Low-fidelity XFOIL simulations provide cheap features, while high-fidelity RANS evaluations are selectively triggered when surrogate uncertainty exceeds a threshold; elite candidates are always validated at high fidelity, and the population is re-evaluated to avoid outdated fitness values. Tested on a two-point optimization problem at Re=6×10⁶ with cruise (maximize L/D) and take-off (maximize CL) conditions using a 12-parameter CST representation, the method achieved a 41.05% improvement in cruise efficiency and 20.75% increase in take-off lift over the best initial design. High-fidelity RANS evaluations were used in only 14.78% and 9.5% of candidate evaluations for cruise and take-off, respectively, demonstrating significant cost reduction compared to a fixed RANS baseline.
为在多工况下优化机翼形状时降低高保真计算成本并保持气动一致性，该研究提出一种主动多保真代理模型框架，结合低保真信息引导的高斯过程回归模型、不确定性触发采样机制与嵌入混合遗传算法中的精英保留规则。低保真XFOIL仿真提供低成本特征，当代理模型预测不确定性超过阈值时才触发高保真RANS仿真；精英候选个体强制进行高保真验证，且种群重新评估以避免基于过时适应度值的进化选择。在Re=6×10⁶条件下，针对巡航（最大化L/D）与起飞（最大化CL）两个工况，采用12参数CST表示法进行测试，优化设计较初始最优个体分别提升巡航效率41.05%和起飞升力20.75%。整个优化过程中，RANS高保真计算仅占巡航和起飞工况候选评估的14.78%和9.5%，显著降低了高保真计算开销。
GrandTour: A Legged Robotics Dataset in the Wild for Multi-Modal Perception and State Estimation
Authors: Turcan Tuna, Jonas Frey, Frank Fu, Katharine Patterson, Tianao Xu, Maurice Fallon, Cesar Cadena, Marco Hutter
First: 2026-02-20T11:57:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:25:37+00:00
Comments: Turcan Tuna, and Jonas Frey contributed equally. Submitted to Sage The International Journal of Robotics Research
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate state estimation and multi-modal perception are prerequisites for autonomous legged robots in complex, large-scale environments. To date, no large-scale public legged-robot dataset captures the real-world conditions needed to develop and benchmark algorithms for legged-robot state estimation, perception, and navigation. To address this, we introduce the GrandTour dataset, a multi-modal legged-robotics dataset collected across challenging outdoor and indoor environments, featuring an ANYbotics ANYmal-D quadruped equipped with the Boxi multi-modal sensor payload. GrandTour spans a broad range of environments and operational scenarios across distinct test sites, ranging from alpine scenery and forests to demolished buildings and urban areas, and covers a wide variation in scale, complexity, illumination, and weather conditions. The dataset provides time-synchronized sensor data from spinning LiDARs, multiple RGB cameras with complementary characteristics, proprioceptive sensors, and stereo depth cameras. Moreover, it includes high-precision ground-truth trajectories from satellite-based RTK-GNSS and a Leica Geosystems total station. This dataset supports research in SLAM, high-precision state estimation, and multi-modal learning, enabling rigorous evaluation and development of new approaches to sensor fusion in legged robotic systems. With its extensive scope, GrandTour represents the largest open-access legged-robotics dataset to date. The dataset is available at https://grand-tour.leggedrobotics.com on HuggingFace (ROS-independent), and in ROS formats, along with tools and demo resources.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GrandTour：用于多模态感知与状态估计的野外四足机器人数据集
在复杂的大规模环境中，准确的状态估计和多模态感知是自主四足机器人实现导航的前提。目前尚无大规模公开的四足机器人数据集能真实反映现实世界条件，以支持和评估四足机器人状态估计、感知与导航算法。为此，我们推出了GrandTour数据集，该数据集在具有挑战性的户外与室内环境中采集，搭载ANYbotics ANYmal-D四足机器人及Boxi多模态传感器载荷。GrandTour覆盖多样化的测试场地，包括高山景观、森林、废弃建筑和城市区域，涵盖不同尺度、复杂度、光照与天气条件。数据集提供同步时间戳的传感器数据，包括旋转激光雷达、多种具有互补特性的RGB相机、本体感觉传感器及立体深度相机。此外，还包含基于卫星的RTK-GNSS与徕卡大地测量全站仪提供的高精度地面真值轨迹。该数据集支持SLAM、高精度状态估计与多模态学习研究，可严谨评估与开发四足机器人系统中的传感器融合新方法。凭借其广泛覆盖范围，GrandTour是目前最大的开放获取四足机器人数据集。数据集可在HuggingFace（ROS独立）及ROS格式下获取，同时提供工具与演示资源：https://grand-tour.leggedrobotics.com。
Summary / 总结
To advance autonomous legged robotics in complex real-world environments, the authors developed the GrandTour dataset, which captures multi-modal sensor data from an ANYmal-D quadruped across diverse outdoor and indoor settings including alpine terrain, forests, and urban ruins. The dataset integrates synchronized inputs from spinning LiDARs, RGB cameras, proprioceptive sensors, and stereo depth cameras, complemented by high-precision ground-truth trajectories from RTK-GNSS and a Leica total station. Experimental evaluation using this dataset enables rigorous benchmarking of state estimation and multi-modal perception algorithms, demonstrating its utility for SLAM and sensor fusion research, and establishing it as the largest publicly available legged-robotics dataset to date.
为推动自主四足机器人在复杂真实环境中的发展，GrandTour数据集旨在填补当前缺乏大规模多模态公开数据用于状态估计与感知的空白。该数据集由配备Boxi多模态传感器的ANYmal-D四足机器人在多样化的户外与室内场景（如高山、森林、城市及废弃建筑）中采集，包含同步的旋转激光雷达、RGB相机、本体传感器和立体深度相机数据，并辅以RTK-GNSS和徕卡全站仪提供的高精度轨迹真值。该数据集支持SLAM、状态估计与传感器融合方法的严格评估，目前是规模最大的开放获取四足机器人数据集。
A Distributionally Robust Optimisation Approach to Fair Credit Scoring
Authors: Pablo Casas, Huan Yu, Christophe Mues
First: 2024-02-02T11:43:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:23:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Credit scoring has been catalogued by the European Commission and the Executive Office of the US President as a high-risk classification task, in light of the potential harms of making loan approval decisions based on models that would be biased against certain groups. To address this concern, recent credit scoring research has considered a range of fairness-enhancing techniques put forward by the machine learning community to reduce bias and unfair treatment in classification systems. While the definition of fairness or the approach they follow to impose it may vary, most of these techniques, however, disregard the robustness of the results. This can create situations where unfair treatment is effectively corrected in the training set, but when producing out-of-distribution classifications, unfair treatment is incurred again. Instead, in this paper, we will investigate how to apply Distributionally Robust Optimisation (DRO) methods to credit scoring, thereby empirically evaluating how they perform in terms of fairness, ability to classify correctly, and the robustness of the solution against changes in the marginal proportions. In so doing, we find DRO methods to provide a substantial improvement in terms of fairness, with almost no loss in predictive performance. These results thus indicate that DRO can improve fairness in credit scoring, provided that further advances are made in efficiently implementing these systems. In addition, our analysis suggests that many of the commonly used fairness metrics are not ideally suited to the credit scoring setting, as they evaluate performance at a single classification threshold.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于分布鲁棒优化的公平信用评分方法
信用评分被欧盟委员会和美国总统办公厅列为高风险分类任务，因其在基于有偏模型做出贷款审批决策时可能造成对特定群体的伤害。为应对这一问题，近期信用评分研究已考虑机器学习社区提出的多种增强公平性的技术，以减少分类系统中的偏见和不公平待遇。尽管公平性的定义或其实施方式各异，但这些技术大多忽视了结果的鲁棒性，可能导致在训练集中不公平待遇被有效纠正，但在分布外分类时再次出现不公平。本文将探讨如何将分布鲁棒优化（DRO）方法应用于信用评分，并实证评估其在公平性、正确分类能力和对边际比例变化的鲁棒性方面的表现。结果表明，DRO方法在提升公平性方面有显著改善，且几乎不损失预测性能。这表明，只要进一步提升这些系统的高效实施，DRO可有效改善信用评分的公平性。此外，我们的分析表明，许多常用公平性指标并不完全适用于信用评分场景，因其仅在单一分类阈值下评估性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk of biased loan decisions in credit scoring, this paper proposes using Distributionally Robust Optimisation (DRO) to enhance fairness while maintaining predictive accuracy. The method formulates the credit scoring problem under distributional uncertainty, aiming to minimize worst-case performance across plausible data shifts. Experimental results show that DRO significantly improves fairness metrics without sacrificing classification accuracy, and demonstrates robustness to changes in marginal group proportions. The study also highlights that conventional fairness metrics may be inadequate for credit scoring due to their reliance on single thresholds.
为解决信用评分中模型偏见导致的不公平贷款决策风险，本文提出采用分布鲁棒优化（DRO）方法提升公平性同时保持预测准确性。该方法在分布不确定性下建模信用评分问题，旨在最小化可能数据偏移下的最坏情况表现。实验结果表明，DRO显著提升公平性指标且几乎不损失分类准确率，并对边际群体比例变化表现出鲁棒性。研究还指出，常用公平性指标因依赖单一阈值，可能不适用于信用评分场景。
CARLA-GS: Decoupling Representation, Reasoning, and Physics Simulation for Autonomous Driving Corner-Case Synthesis
Authors: Kaicong Huang, Meng Ma, Ruimin Ke
First: 2026-07-08T16:20:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:20:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety evaluation for autonomous driving is dominated by rare, safety-critical interactions, motivating simulators that can deliberately synthesize corner cases with photorealistic observations. Corner-case generation is inherently a multi-source problem spanning visual representation, scene reasoning, and vehicle trajectory generation and control. Prior knowledge- and model-based approaches typically focus on scene or trajectory components in isolation, while diffusion-based methods attempt end-to-end generation but still struggle to ensure spatiotemporal consistency and physical realism. To unify these aspects within a single framework, we propose CARLA-GS, a modular corner-case synthesis pipeline that decouples visual representation, semantic reasoning, and physics-based execution while maintaining tight cross-module coupling. Starting from real driving data, we reconstruct an editable gaussian scene with additional geometry-consistent constraints. A multi-agent LLM then performs scene-level reasoning to identify risky interactions and generate intent-level waypoint trajectories, while the low-level motion control is delegated to CARLA, where a PID controller ensures kinematic and dynamic feasibility. The simulated vehicle states are finally re-projected into the gaussian scene for ego-centric rendering. This design enables high-level semantic reasoning, low-level physically executable motion, and photorealistic corner-case generation within a unified pipeline. Experiments on the Waymo Open Dataset show, both quantitatively and qualitatively, that our framework enables controllable corner-case generation and produces photorealistic, spatiotemporally consistent videos aligned with semantic intent and physically feasible motion.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CARLA-GS：用于自动驾驶边缘案例合成的表示、推理与物理仿真解耦方法
自动驾驶的安全评估主要由罕见且安全关键的交互决定，因此需要能够刻意合成具有逼真视觉观测的边缘案例的模拟器。边缘案例生成本质上是一个多源问题，涵盖视觉表示、场景推理和车辆轨迹生成与控制。以往基于知识或模型的方法通常孤立关注场景或轨迹组件，而扩散模型虽尝试端到端生成，但仍难以确保时空一致性和物理真实性。为在单一框架内统一这些方面，我们提出CARLA-GS，一个模块化的边缘案例合成流程，解耦视觉表示、语义推理和基于物理的执行，同时保持模块间紧密耦合。从真实驾驶数据出发，我们重建一个可编辑的高斯场景，并添加几何一致性约束。多智能体大语言模型执行场景级推理，识别危险交互并生成意图级航点轨迹，而低层运动控制则交由CARLA处理，其中PID控制器确保运动学与动力学可行性。最终，模拟车辆状态重新投影至高斯场景，实现以自我为中心的渲染。该设计在统一流程中实现了高层语义推理、低层物理可执行运动和逼真的边缘案例生成。在Waymo开放数据集上的实验表明，我们的框架在定量和定性上均实现了可控的边缘案例生成，并产生与语义意图一致、物理可行且时空一致的逼真视频。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to synthesize rare, safety-critical corner cases for autonomous driving safety evaluation, CARLA-GS introduces a modular pipeline that decouples visual representation, semantic reasoning, and physics simulation while maintaining cross-module coordination. It begins by reconstructing an editable Gaussian scene from real driving data, then uses a multi-agent LLM to reason about risky interactions and generate intent-level trajectories, while low-level motion control is handled by CARLA’s PID controller to ensure physical feasibility. The final vehicle states are reprojected into the Gaussian scene for photorealistic ego-centric rendering. Experiments on the Waymo Open Dataset demonstrate that the framework generates controllable, spatiotemporally consistent, and physically feasible corner-case videos with high semantic alignment.
为提升自动驾驶系统在罕见高危场景下的安全评估能力，CARLA-GS提出一种模块化合成框架，将视觉表征、语义推理与物理仿真解耦并保持模块间紧密协同。该框架从真实驾驶数据出发，构建带几何约束的高斯场景，利用多智能体大语言模型进行场景级风险推理并生成意图轨迹，再通过CARLA的PID控制器实现物理可行的低层运动控制，最终将车辆状态重投影至高斯场景以生成逼真的第一人称视频。在Waymo开放数据集上的实验表明，该方法可生成可控、时空一致且物理合理的高危场景视频，符合语义意图。
MultAttnAttrib: Training-Free Multimodal Attribution in Long Document Question Answering
Authors: Dang Quang Thien Tran, Quang V. Dang, Vinamra Tyagi, Sai Soorya Rao Veeravalli, Trang Nguyen, Ryan A. Rossi, Franck Dernoncourt, Nedim Lipka, Koustava Goswami, Samyadeep Basu
First: 2026-07-01T19:29:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:20:17+00:00
Comments: 25 pages (8 main, 17 references + appendix), 15 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As grounded QA systems are increasingly deployed in AI assistants, accurately attributing generated answers to evidence is critical for user trust and model safety. While unimodal attributions have been explored in depth, the multimodal setting remains relatively under-researched. As a result, we introduce MultAttnAttrib, a training-free attribution-generation method that leverages a model's prefill pass, selected attention heads, and calibrated thresholds to locate source evidence within a document. To establish baseline results for the method, we introduce MultAttrEval, a complementary benchmark dataset annotated with fine-grained, ground-truth attributions for answer components grounded in multimodal source documents. To our knowledge, this is the first evaluation dataset designed specifically for multimodal attribution in long-form documents. Experimental results show that MultAttnAttrib consistently outperforms a variety of attribution-generation methods, including several strong prompting-based approaches and matches the latest frontier models such as GPT 5.4. Our method not only substantially improves attribution accuracy for both unimodal and multimodal attribution types, but also produces attributions at up to one-seventh of the direct inference latency compared to prompting on the same base model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MultAttnAttrib：长文档问答中的无训练多模态归因方法
随着基于视觉的问答系统在AI助手中的广泛应用，准确将生成的答案归因于证据对用户信任和模型安全至关重要。尽管单模态归因已得到深入研究，多模态场景仍相对研究不足。为此，我们提出了MultAttnAttrib，一种无需训练的归因生成方法，利用模型的预填充阶段、选定的注意力头和校准的阈值，在文档中定位源证据。为建立该方法的基线结果，我们引入了MultAttrEval，一个补充性的基准数据集，其标注了细粒度、真实归因的问答组件，这些组件基于多模态源文档。据我们所知，这是首个专门为长文档多模态归因设计的评估数据集。实验结果表明，MultAttnAttrib在多种归因生成方法（包括多种强大的提示方法）中表现更优，且与最新前沿模型（如GPT 5.4）表现相当。该方法不仅显著提升了单模态和多模态归因的准确性，还相比在相同基础模型上使用提示方法，将归因生成延迟降低至其七分之一。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for trustworthy and safe AI assistants, this work addresses the challenge of attributing answers in multimodal long-document QA without training. MultAttnAttrib, a training-free method, uses the model’s prefill pass, selected attention heads, and calibrated thresholds to pinpoint evidence within documents. Evaluated on MultAttrEval — the first benchmark with fine-grained, ground-truth multimodal attributions — it outperforms existing prompting-based methods and matches state-of-the-art models like GPT 5.4, while achieving up to 7x faster inference than prompting on the same base model.
为提升AI助手的可信度与安全性，该研究聚焦于多模态长文档问答中答案溯源的难题。MultAttnAttrib是一种无需训练的方法，利用模型的预填充阶段、选定的注意力头和校准阈值，直接定位证据来源。在新构建的MultAttrEval基准数据集上，该方法显著优于多种提示式方法，性能可媲美GPT 5.4等前沿模型，同时推理延迟仅为直接提示的七分之一。
L-GTA: Latent Generative Modeling for Time Series Augmentation
Authors: Luis Roque, Vitor Cerqueira, Carlos Soares, Luis Torgo
Venue: ECML PKDD 2026 Research Track, Naples, Italy, September 7-11, 2026
First: 2025-07-31T14:53:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:17:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Data augmentation is becoming increasingly important across various areas of time series analysis, including forecasting, classification, and anomaly detection. We introduce the Latent Generative Temporal Augmentation (L-GTA) model, a generative approach based on a Variational Autoencoder with a Bi-LSTM backbone and temporal self-attention. The model learns a latent representation for each timestep and applies controlled perturbations such as jittering, magnitude warping, or drift. We define an equivariance objective to further encourage consistency between latent space and data space transformations. As a result, the augmented samples show predictable and interpretable transformation signatures. We evaluate L-GTA on several real-world datasets against SOTA generative methods, including TimeGAN, TimeVAE, and Diffusion-TS, as well as direct transformation approaches. Across experiments on downstream forecasting, distribution fidelity, and controllability of transformation intensity, L-GTA consistently outperforms competing approaches. In downstream forecasting, it reduces prediction error by up to 26% compared to the strongest generative method and 27% relative to using the original data without augmentation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：L-GTA：基于潜在生成建模的时间序列增强
数据增强在时间序列分析的各个领域（包括预测、分类和异常检测）变得越来越重要。我们提出了基于变分自编码器、以双向LSTM为骨干并结合时序自注意力机制的潜在生成时序增强（L-GTA）模型。该模型为每个时间步学习潜在表示，并应用受控扰动（如抖动、幅度变形或漂移）。我们定义了一个等变目标函数，以进一步鼓励潜在空间与数据空间变换之间的一致性。因此，增强样本表现出可预测且可解释的变换特征。我们在多个真实世界数据集上，将L-GTA与当前最先进的生成方法（如TimeGAN、TimeVAE和Diffusion-TS）以及直接变换方法进行比较。在下游预测、分布保真度和变换强度可控性实验中，L-GTA始终优于竞争方法。在下游预测任务中，其预测误差相比最强的生成方法降低高达26%，相比未增强的原始数据降低27%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for effective time series augmentation to improve downstream tasks like forecasting and anomaly detection, the authors propose L-GTA, a generative model combining a Variational Autoencoder with a Bi-LSTM and temporal self-attention to learn latent representations per timestep. It applies controlled transformations such as jittering or magnitude warping to generate augmented samples, guided by an equivariance objective that ensures consistency between latent and data space manipulations. Experimental results show L-GTA outperforms state-of-the-art generative methods including TimeGAN and Diffusion-TS, reducing forecasting error by up to 26% over the best competitor and 27% over using un-augmented data.
为提升时间序列下游任务（如预测与异常检测）效果，作者提出L-GTA模型，结合变分自编码器、双向LSTM与时间自注意力机制，学习每个时间步的潜在表示并施加可控扰动（如抖动或幅度变换），并通过等变目标确保潜在空间与数据空间变换的一致性。实验表明，L-GTA在多个真实数据集上优于TimeGAN、Diffusion-TS等主流生成方法，在预测任务中相较最佳生成方法误差降低26%，相较原始数据无增强方案降低27%。
Fast segmentation of watermarked texts from large language models through an epidemic change-point framework
Authors: Soham Bonnerjee, Subhrajyoty Roy, Sayar Karmakar
First: 2025-09-25T13:44:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:16:06+00:00
Comments: 68 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With the growing use of large language models, concerns over content authenticity have spurred a variety of watermarking schemes. These schemes use secret keys to detect machine-generated text while remaining imperceptible to readers. Detection typically reduces to statistical hypothesis testing for the presence of watermarks, a topic that is now well studied. In contrast, the finer-grained task of localizing which segments of a text are watermarked is much less explored; existing approaches often lack scalability or guarantees robust to paraphrasing and post-editing. We bring a new perspective to this segmentation problem through the lens of epidemic change-points and, by exploiting this connection, propose WISER, a novel and computationally efficient watermark segmentation algorithm. We establish finite-sample error bounds and consistency for detecting multiple watermarked segments in a single text. Complementing these theoretical results, our extensive numerical experiments show that WISER outperforms state-of-the-art baseline methods, both in terms of computational speed as well as accuracy, on various benchmark datasets embedded with diverse watermarking schemes. Together, these theoretical and empirical results position WISER as an effective tool for watermark localization and illustrate how classical statistical ideas can yield theoretically valid and computationally efficient solutions to a modern problem of immediate importance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过流行病变化点框架快速分割大语言模型中的水印文本
随着大语言模型的广泛应用，内容真实性问题促使了多种水印方案的出现。这些方案使用密钥检测机器生成的文本，同时对读者不可察觉。检测通常归结为统计假设检验以判断是否存在水印，该领域已得到充分研究。相比之下，更精细的任务——定位文本中哪些段落被水印标记——则研究较少；现有方法常缺乏可扩展性或对改写和后编辑的鲁棒性。我们通过流行病变化点视角重新审视该分割问题，并利用这一联系提出WISER，一种新颖且计算高效的水印分割算法。我们建立了检测单篇文本中多个水印段落的有限样本误差界和一致性理论。结合这些理论结果，我们的大量数值实验表明，WISER在多种嵌入不同水印方案的基准数据集上，无论计算速度还是准确性均优于现有最先进基线方法。这些理论与实证结果共同表明，WISER是水印定位的有效工具，并展示了经典统计思想如何为现代紧迫问题提供理论严谨且计算高效的解决方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to accurately localize watermarked segments in machine-generated text from large language models, this work introduces WISER, a computationally efficient algorithm based on epidemic change-point theory. Unlike prior methods that struggle with scalability or robustness to text editing, WISER leverages statistical change-point detection to identify watermarked regions with finite-sample error guarantees and asymptotic consistency. Experimental results across multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that WISER achieves superior speed and accuracy compared to existing baselines, making it a practical and theoretically grounded solution for watermark segmentation.
为了解决大语言模型生成文本中水印段落定位的难题，本文提出WISER算法，该算法基于流行病变化点理论，实现高效准确的水印段落分割。与现有方法相比，WISER在面对文本改写或编辑时仍保持鲁棒性，并具备理论上的误差界与一致性保证。实验表明，WISER在多个基准数据集上均显著优于当前主流方法，在计算效率和定位精度上表现更优，为水印定位提供了实用且理论严谨的解决方案。
Feynman Kac Reweighted Schrödinger Bridge Matching for Surface-Based Tau PET Harmonization
Authors: Jianwei Zhang, Xinyu Nie, Jiaxin Yue, Yonggang Shi
First: 2026-06-16T02:06:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:06:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Tau positron emission tomography (PET) is widely used for the in vivo characterization of disease stage and progression in Alzheimer's disease (AD). With the adoption of multiple tau PET tracers including AV-1451, PI-2620, MK-6240 with different binding behaviors in various large-scale studies, there is a great need of effective harmonization methods to enable the cross-tracer integration of tau PET datasets. While previous methods such as CenTauR were proposed to standardize scalar tau PET measures, they are limited in accounting for the heterogeneity of tau pathology. In this work, we propose Feynman-Kac Reweighted Schrödinger Bridge Matching (FKRSBM), a surface-based framework for cross-tracer tau PET harmonization. FKRSBM learns a direct stochastic transport between tracer domains using Schrödinger Bridge matching, avoiding the Gaussian-prior routing used in diffusion-based translation. To promote biologically consistent transport, FKRSBM introduces an endpoint penalty favoring bridge pairings with matched tau-pathology status and implements it through a Feynman-Kac reweighted endpoint proposal. To preserve cortical organization, FKRSBM uses a spherical convolutional network for vertex-level harmonization on cortical surface meshes. In our experiments, we demonstrate our method by harmonizing Tau PET images acquired with the AV-1451 (n=1480) and PI-2620 (n=2458) tracers from two large-scale datasets. Compared to previous methods including ComBat, CycleGAN, Diffusion Model(DF), and unregularized Schrödinger Bridge Model(DSBM), the proposed FKRSBM method outperforms these baselines in subgroup-level alignment, tau-positivity consistency, and diagnostic classification while preserving subject-specific cortical topography of tau pathology. The code is available at: https://github.com/jianweizhang17/FKRSBM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于表面的费曼-卡茨重加权薛定谔桥匹配用于Tau PET图像的跨示踪剂谐调
Tau正电子发射断层扫描（PET）广泛用于阿尔茨海默病（AD）患者体内疾病阶段和进展的表征。随着AV-1451、PI-2620、MK-6240等多种Tau PET示踪剂在不同大规模研究中的应用，亟需有效的谐调方法以实现跨示踪剂Tau PET数据集的整合。此前方法如CenTauR虽可标准化标量Tau PET测量值，但难以充分考虑Tau病理的异质性。本文提出费曼-卡茨重加权薛定谔桥匹配（FKRSBM），一种基于表面的跨示踪剂Tau PET谐调框架。FKRSBM利用薛定谔桥匹配学习示踪剂域间的直接随机传输，避免了基于扩散模型的翻译中常用的高斯先验路由。为促进生物一致性传输，FKRSBM引入终点惩罚，偏好匹配Tau病理状态的桥接配对，并通过费曼-卡茨重加权终点提议实现。为保留皮层组织结构，FKRSBM采用球面卷积网络在皮层表面网格上进行顶点级谐调。实验中，我们通过谐调来自两个大规模数据集的AV-1451（n=1480）和PI-2620（n=2458）示踪剂获取的Tau PET图像，验证了该方法。与ComBat、CycleGAN、扩散模型（DF）及无正则化薛定谔桥模型（DSBM）等基线方法相比，FKRSBM在亚组级对齐、Tau阳性一致性及诊断分类方面表现更优，同时保留了受试者特异性的皮层Tau病理拓扑结构。代码见：https://github.com/jianweizhang17/FKRSBM。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to harmonize tau PET data across different tracers to enable cross-study integration in Alzheimer’s disease research, this work introduces FKRSBM, a surface-based method that uses Schrödinger Bridge matching to learn direct stochastic transport between tracer domains without relying on Gaussian priors. To ensure biologically plausible mappings, it incorporates a Feynman-Kac reweighted endpoint penalty that favors pairs with matched tau pathology status, while preserving cortical structure via spherical convolutional networks on surface meshes. Experiments on AV-1451 and PI-2620 datasets show FKRSBM outperforms baselines including ComBat, CycleGAN, Diffusion Model, and unregularized Schrödinger Bridge in subgroup alignment, tau-positivity consistency, and diagnostic accuracy while maintaining subject-specific cortical topography.
为实现不同tau PET示踪剂数据的跨研究整合，本文提出FKRSBM方法，基于表面建模，利用Schrödinger桥匹配学习示踪剂域间的直接随机传输，避免扩散模型中的高斯先验。通过Feynman-Kac重加权端点惩罚机制，确保传输结果符合生物学一致性，偏好匹配的tau病理状态，并采用球面卷积网络在顶点级进行皮层组织和谐化。在AV-1451与PI-2620数据集上的实验表明，FKRSBM在子组对齐、tau阳性一致性及诊断分类上优于ComBat、CycleGAN、扩散模型及未正则化Schrödinger桥模型，同时保留了个体皮层病理拓扑结构。
Superstate Quantum Mechanics
Authors: Mikhail Gennadievich Belov, Victor Victorovich Dubov, Vadim Konstantinovich Ivanov, Alexander Yurievich Maslov, Olga Vladimirovna Proshina, Vladislav Gennadievich Malyshkin
First: 2025-01-25T19:41:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:06:49+00:00
Comments: The ML approach presented in arXiv:2407.04406 is extended to stationary and non-stationary quantum dynamics
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Superstate Quantum Mechanics (SQM), a theory that considers states in Hilbert space subject to multiple quadratic constraints, with ``energy'' also expressed as a quadratic function of these states. Traditional quantum mechanics corresponds to a single quadratic constraint of wavefunction normalization with energy expressed as a quadratic form involving the Hamiltonian. When SQM represents states as unitary operators, the stationary problem becomes a quantum inverse problem with multiple applications in physics, machine learning, and artificial intelligence. Any stationary SQM problem is equivalent to a new algebraic problem that we address in this paper. The non-stationary SQM problem considers the evolution of the system itself, involving the same ``energy'' operator as in the stationary case. Two possible options for the SQM dynamic equation are considered: (1) within the framework of linear maps from higher-order quantum theory, where 2D-type quantum circuits transform one quantum system into another; and (2) in the form of a Gross-Pitaevskii-type nonlinear map. Although no known physical process currently describes such 2D dynamics, this approach naturally bridges direct and inverse quantum mechanics problems, allowing for the development of a new type of computer algorithms. As an immediately available practical application of the theory, we consider using a quantum channel as a classical computational model; this type of computation can be performed on a classical computer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超态量子力学
我们引入了超态量子力学（SQM），该理论考虑希尔伯特空间中的状态受多重二次约束，且“能量”也表示为这些状态的二次函数。传统量子力学对应于波函数归一化单一二次约束，能量以涉及哈密顿量的二次型表达。当SQM将状态表示为幺正算符时，静态问题转化为具有多重应用的量子逆问题（物理学、机器学习和人工智能）。任何静态SQM问题等价于本文所解决的新代数问题。非静态SQM问题考虑系统自身的演化，涉及与静态情形相同的“能量”算符。考虑两种可能的SQM动力学方程：（1）在高阶量子理论线性映射框架内，2D型量子电路将一个量子系统转化为另一个；（2）以Gross-Pitaevskii型非线性映射形式。尽管目前尚无已知物理过程描述此类2D动力学，该方法自然连接直接与逆量子力学问题，可发展新型计算机算法。作为理论的即时实用应用，我们考虑将量子信道作为经典计算模型，此类计算可在经典计算机上实现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by extending quantum mechanics to handle multiple quadratic constraints on Hilbert space states and quadratic energy expressions, Superstate Quantum Mechanics (SQM) reformulates stationary and non-stationary dynamics as algebraic problems. By representing states as unitary operators, SQM transforms the stationary problem into a quantum inverse problem with applications in physics, machine learning, and AI, while the non-stationary case is modeled via either linear maps from higher-order quantum theory or Gross-Pitaevskii-type nonlinear maps. Although no known physical process currently supports the 2D dynamics, this framework bridges direct and inverse quantum mechanics and enables novel algorithmic approaches. A practical application demonstrated is using quantum channels as classical computational models, executable on classical computers.
为扩展量子力学以处理希尔伯特空间状态上的多重二次约束及二次能量表达，提出超态量子力学（SQM），将定态与非定态量子问题转化为代数与动力学系统。当状态表示为酉算符时，定态问题转化为量子反问题，适用于物理、机器学习与人工智能领域；该问题通过新代数方法求解。非定态动力学提出两种演化模型：一种基于高阶量子理论中的线性映射，涉及2D型量子电路；另一种为格罗斯-皮塔耶夫斯基型非线性映射，虽当前无已知物理过程实现2D动力学。该理论自然连接正向与反向量子力学问题，促进新型算法开发，并提供实用应用：将量子信道作为经典计算模型，可在经典计算机上执行。
Where Did the Variability Go? From Vibe Coding to Product Lines by Regeneration
Authors: Xhevahire Tërnava
First: 2026-06-17T13:10:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:05:35+00:00
Comments: VARIABILITY 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In vibe coding, an emerging AI-driven paradigm, an LLM generates an entire program from a natural language prompt, but what happens to the variability that traditional software engineering carefully builds into code? To answer this question, we conducted an exploratory analysis on 10 vibe coded C/C++ projects, which suggests that there is near zero in-artifact variability, i.e., at compile- and runtime. All variability decisions are resolved at a single new binding time, generation time, the moment the LLM produces the source code. Rather than treating this as a defect to fix, we propose Variability by Regeneration (VbR), to our knowledge the first product line approach in which the LLM acts as the derivation engine, generating a dedicated, free of dead code binary for each variant from a declarative specification, while a variant dispatcher transparently routes user requests to the matching binary. We formalise VbR, contrast it with classical SPL derivation, and demonstrate its full pipeline on a wc product family. For SPL engineering, variability in AI-generated software belongs in the specification, not in the code.
中文标题/摘要
标题：变异性去哪儿了？从振动编码到再生式产品线
在振动编码这一新兴的AI驱动范式中，大语言模型（LLM）可从自然语言提示生成完整程序，但传统软件工程精心构建的变异性会如何？为回答此问题，我们对10个振动编码的C/C++项目进行了探索性分析，结果表明：编译和运行时几乎不存在内构件变异性。所有变异性决策均在单一新绑定时间——生成时间（即LLM生成源代码的时刻）解决。我们不将其视为缺陷需修复，而是提出再生式变异性（VbR），据我们所知，这是首个以LLM作为派生引擎的产品线方法：从声明式规范生成每个变体专属、无死代码的二进制文件，同时变体分发器透明路由用户请求至匹配二进制。我们形式化了VbR，与经典SPL派生对比，并在wc产品族上完整演示其流程。对SPL工程而言，AI生成软件的变异性应置于规范而非代码中。

Experimental results on a wc product family demonstrate VbR’s full pipeline, showing it can effectively manage variability without modifying code structure, supporting the claim that variability should reside in specifications rather than code.
为探究AI生成软件中可变性的处理方式，作者分析了10个vibe编码的C/C++项目，发现编译和运行时几乎无内构件可变性，所有决策均在生成时（LLM产生源码时）一次性确定。他们提出“再生式可变性”（VbR）方法，首次将LLM作为派生引擎，从声明式规范生成每个变体的专用二进制文件，并通过变体调度器透明路由用户请求。在wc产品族上的实验验证了VbR全流程的有效性，表明AI生成软件的可变性应置于规范层面而非代码层面。
Cardiac MRI Through-Plane Super-Resolution Guided by Reference and Memory
Authors: Shaoming Pan, Chenchuhui Hu, Leon Axel, Meng Ye
First: 2026-07-08T16:04:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:04:06+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 3 figures 2 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Clinical cardiac MRI is commonly acquired with high in-plane resolution but coarse through-plane resolution to reduce scan time and accommodate breath-hold and cardiac-motion constraints, which limits 3D analysis and diagnostic accuracy. We propose STRMSR, a reference- and memory-guided through-plane super-resolution (SR) framework that reconstructs high-resolution (HR) cardiac volumes by leveraging HR reference views acquired from the same subject and intermediate SR results as the memory. Our method uses coarse-to-fine contextual matching to establish robust correspondence between low-resolution target and reference/memory images under spatial misalignment. A learnable patch-wise dynamic feature aggregation module predicts content-adaptive mixture weights for each local patch, effectively fusing dynamic information while suppressing unreliable feature transfers. The intermediate SR results stored in the memory bank ensure slice-to-slice consistency for the super-resolved 3D volume. Experiments on the WHS cardiac MRI dataset under two reference protocols, orthogonal-plane views and long-axis chamber views, demonstrate consistent improvements over baselines at 4x and 8x upsampling factors.

Automatic Echocardiography Segmentation via Transition Probability Correlation for Stable Semantic Extraction
Authors: Xinran Chen, Xiyuan Wang, Guangquan Zhou, Chuan Chen
First: 2026-07-08T16:04:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:04:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While echocardiography is essential for cardiovascular diagnosis, inherent speckle noise and low signal-to-noise ratio often lead to ambiguous semantic features and fragmented boundaries. These limitations significantly hinder the segmentation accuracy of deep learning models in complex clinical cases. Moreover, temporal motion of the heart plays a critical role in recognizing anatomical structures. To address these challenges, we designed a STLSF module which comprises a window-matching-based semantic correction component and a semantics-guided texture enhancement component. By leveraging local transition probability correlations to correct semantics and employing semantics-guided texture enhancement, the STLSF module effectively mitigates texture instability and ambiguous semantic interpretations caused by disadvantaged echocardiography quality. Additionally, to facilitate the encoder's adaptation to the intrinsic priors of ultrasound-specific imaging patterns, we propose a frequency-aware denoising pre-training method. The entire work builds a convolution-based network with locality inductive bias and long-range dependencies. Extensive experiments confirm our SOTA performance, achieving 93.87\% Dice on CAMUS and 92.62\% on EchoNet-Dynamic, with respective HD95 values of 3.29mm and 2.73mm.

Context-Aware Force Estimation for Deformable Tool Manipulation in Robotic Environmental Swabbing via Few-Shot Continual Adaptation
Authors: Siavash Mahmoudi, Chaitainya Kuppar Reddy, Yang Tian, Dongyi Wang
First: 2026-07-08T16:01:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:01:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robotic surface swabbing requires sustained interaction between a compliant tool and heterogeneous environments, where accurate estimation of tip-level contact force is critical for consistent sampling performance. However, deformable tool dynamics introduce nonlinear viscoelastic hysteresis that decouples wrist-mounted force measurements from true contact forces, while tool-integrated sensors are impractical for deployment due to sterility and disposability constraints. This paper presents a data-driven framework for contact force estimation in Deformable Tool Manipulation (DTM) that leverages proprioceptive sensing without requiring explicit physical models or permanent embedded sensing hardware at the tool tip. A recurrent architecture is first identified through a comparative evaluation of temporal models, where a compact LSTM achieves the lowest estimation error and sub-millisecond inference latency. To address generalization across unseen surfaces and tool compliance conditions, we introduce a parameter-isolated few-shot adaptation strategy that augments a frozen recurrent backbone with low-dimensional context embeddings using feature-wise linear modulation (FiLM). Experiments on a UR5e platform across nine tool-surface interaction regimes demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly improves robustness under domain shift, reducing zero-shot estimation error by up to 63\% while preserving baseline performance without catastrophic forgetting. These results show that separating shared deformation-history dynamics from domain-specific conditioning enables reliable force estimation for DTM in non-stationary environments.

标题：通过少量样本持续适应实现机器人环境擦拭中可变形工具操作的上下文感知力估计
机器人表面擦拭需要柔性工具与异质环境持续交互，精确估计工具尖端接触力对稳定采样至关重要。然而，可变形工具动力学引入非线性黏弹性迟滞，使腕部力测量与真实接触力脱钩；而集成式传感器因无菌和一次性使用限制难以部署。本文提出一种基于数据驱动的接触力估计框架，适用于可变形工具操作（DTM），无需显式物理模型或永久嵌入式尖端传感硬件，仅依赖本体感觉传感。通过时序模型比较，确定了紧凑型LSTM架构，其估计误差最低且推理延迟低于毫秒。为应对未见表面及工具柔顺性条件下的泛化问题，引入参数隔离的少量样本适应策略，通过特征级线性调制（FiLM）将低维上下文嵌入与冻结的循环主干网络结合。在UR5e平台上九种工具-表面交互场景的实验表明，该方法显著提升域偏移下的鲁棒性，零样本估计误差降低高达63%，同时保持基线性能且无灾难性遗忘。结果表明，将共享变形历史动力学与域特异性条件分离，可实现非稳态环境下DTM的可靠力估计。

Motivated by the need for accurate contact force estimation in robotic swabbing with deformable tools, this work proposes a data-driven method that uses proprioceptive sensing and a compact LSTM architecture to estimate tip-level forces without physical models or embedded sensors. To handle unseen surfaces and tool compliance, it employs a few-shot continual adaptation strategy using FiLM to inject low-dimensional context embeddings into a frozen LSTM backbone. 为实现机器人在非结构化环境中使用柔性工具进行采样时的精确接触力估计，该研究提出一种基于本体感知的数据驱动方法，采用紧凑型LSTM架构无需物理模型或嵌入式传感器即可估计工具尖端受力。为提升对未见表面和工具柔性的泛化能力，引入基于特征线性调制的少样本持续适应机制，将上下文嵌入注入冻结的LSTM主干网络。在UR5e机器人上九种交互场景的实验表明，该方法零样本估计误差降低最高达63%，同时保持基线性能并避免灾难性遗忘。
Multi-Class vs. Multi-Label BERT for CVE-to-CWE Mapping: How Taxonomy Structure Shapes the Errors
Authors: Ana Schwengber Kelm, Christian Bockermann, Jörg Frochte
First: 2026-07-08T16:01:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T16:01:21+00:00
Comments: 3 figures, 7 tables, to be published at the ICANN 2026 (International Conference on Artificial Neural Networks)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Assigning Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) categories to Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVE) records remains an important but largely manual step in vulnerability analysis. We study this task as a text classification problem and compare two modelling choices: a \emph{multi-class} formulation that predicts a single CWE per CVE and a \emph{multi-label} formulation that allows multiple assignments. Three transformer encoders (BERT Base, SecureBERT, and CySecBERT) are evaluated on three nested label spaces (83, 47, and 25 classes). Multi-class training achieves higher macro-F1 across all settings, although the gap to multi-label narrows from 21 to 2 percentage points as the label space shrinks. Post-hoc threshold optimisation on the multi-label side closes this gap on the 25-class setting. Confusion analysis shows that the dominant misclassification patterns follow the CWE hierarchy and are shared across all three encoders (Pearson $r > 0.92$), which suggests that the error structure is driven more by taxonomy design than by encoder choice. A hierarchy-relaxed evaluation that forgives within-family confusions raises macro-F1 from ${\sim}$81\% to ${\sim}$90\%, indicating that strict metrics understate branch-level classifier quality. CySecBERT achieves the strongest results overall, with statistically significant gains concentrated in the multi-label setting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多类与多标签BERT在CVE到CWE映射中的比较：分类体系结构如何影响错误
将通用弱点枚举（CWE）类别分配给通用漏洞和披露（CVE）记录，仍是漏洞分析中重要但主要依赖人工的步骤。我们将此任务视为文本分类问题，比较两种建模方式：一种是预测每个CVE对应单一CWE的多类模型，另一种是允许多个分配的多标签模型。在三种嵌套标签空间（83、47和25类）上评估了三种Transformer编码器（BERT Base、SecureBERT和CySecBERT）。多类训练在所有设置中均获得更高的宏F1值，尽管随着标签空间缩小，多类与多标签之间的差距从21个百分点缩小至2个百分点。对多标签模型进行事后阈值优化后，在25类设置中缩小了这一差距。混淆分析表明，主要的误分类模式遵循CWE层次结构，且在三种编码器间共享（皮尔逊相关系数r > 0.92），这表明错误结构更多由分类体系设计而非编码器选择决定。采用放宽层次结构的评估方式（允许同族混淆）可使宏F1值从约81%提升至约90%，表明严格指标低估了分支级分类器的质量。CySecBERT整体表现最佳，其统计显著的提升主要集中在多标签设置中。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the challenge of mapping CVE records to CWE categories, a critical but manual task in vulnerability analysis. It compares multi-class and multi-label BERT-based models using three transformer encoders (BERT Base, SecureBERT, CySecBERT) across three nested CWE label spaces. Multi-class models consistently outperform multi-label ones in macro-F1, though the gap shrinks as label space complexity decreases. Post-hoc threshold tuning nearly closes this gap on the smallest label set. Confusion analysis reveals that misclassifications correlate strongly with CWE taxonomy structure (Pearson r > 0.92), indicating taxonomy design dominates error patterns over model choice. Relaxing evaluation to allow intra-family errors boosts macro-F1 from ~81% to ~90%, suggesting strict metrics undervalue classifier performance. CySecBERT delivers the best overall results, with statistically significant gains in multi-label settings.
本研究旨在解决将CVE漏洞记录映射到CWE分类的问题，这是漏洞分析中的关键但手工密集任务，通过将该任务建模为文本分类问题，比较了多分类与多标签BERT方法。在三个嵌套的CWE标签空间中评估了三种Transformer编码器（BERT Base、SecureBERT和CySecBERT）。多分类模型在所有设置中均优于多标签模型，但随着标签空间缩小，性能差距从21个百分点降至2个百分点。对多标签模型进行后处理阈值优化后，其在最小标签集上的表现接近多分类模型。混淆分析显示，所有模型的错误模式均高度符合CWE层级结构（皮尔逊相关系数>0.92），表明错误主要由分类体系设计而非编码器选择导致。采用放宽评估标准（允许同家族内误分类）可将宏F1从约81%提升至约90%，说明严格指标可能低估了分类器在分支层级上的表现。CySecBERT整体表现最佳，尤其在多标签设置中具有统计显著的性能优势。
How Learning Dynamics Drive Adversarially Robust Generalization?
Authors: Yuelin Xu, Xiao Zhang
First: 2024-10-10T08:34:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:57:40+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 42nd Conference on Uncertainty in Artificial Intelligence (UAI 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite being widely adopted as a canonical framework for learning robust models, adversarial training suffers from robust overfitting. Existing empirical and theoretical explorations fail to provide a satisfactory mechanistic interpretation of the phenomenon. By modeling adversarial training with momentum SGD as a discrete-time dynamical system, we propose a PAC-Bayesian analytical framework that proves time-resolved robust generalization bounds. Specifically, our framework tracks the closed-form evolution of the posterior mean and covariance under both stationary and non-stationary transient regimes, connecting the model's robust generalization performance to learning rate, local loss geometry, and mini-batch stochastic gradients. By estimating the key quantities associated with the bound, we illustrate the underlying mechanism of robust overfitting. Our framework also shows how adversarial weight perturbation reduces robust generalization gaps by suppressing dominant loss-curvature modes, while suggesting that excessive penalization can be sub-optimal for optimization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习动力学如何驱动对抗鲁棒泛化？
尽管对抗训练被广泛采用为学习鲁棒模型的典范框架，但它仍存在鲁棒过拟合问题。现有实证与理论探索未能提供对该现象的令人满意的机制性解释。我们通过将带有动量的SGD建模为离散时间动力系统，提出一种PAC-Bayesian分析框架，证明了时间分辨的鲁棒泛化界。具体而言，该框架追踪了在稳态与非稳态瞬态情形下后验均值与协方差的闭式演化，将模型的鲁棒泛化性能与学习率、局部损失几何结构及小批量随机梯度联系起来。通过估计与界相关的关键量，我们揭示了鲁棒过拟合的内在机制。该框架还表明，对抗权重扰动可通过抑制主导损失曲率模式来缩小鲁棒泛化差距，但过度惩罚可能对优化不利。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the unresolved mechanistic understanding of robust overfitting in adversarial training, this work models the process using momentum SGD as a discrete-time dynamical system and introduces a PAC-Bayesian framework to derive time-resolved robust generalization bounds. The method tracks the closed-form evolution of posterior mean and covariance under stationary and transient regimes, linking robust performance to learning rate, local loss geometry, and mini-batch gradients. Experimental results demonstrate that adversarial weight perturbation reduces robust generalization gaps by suppressing dominant loss-curvature modes, while excessive penalization can hinder optimization.
为了解释对抗训练中鲁棒过拟合的机制，该研究将动量SGD建模为离散时间动力系统，并提出PAC-Bayesian分析框架以推导时变鲁棒泛化界。方法追踪后验均值与协方差在稳态与瞬态下的闭式演化，将鲁棒性能与学习率、局部损失几何及小批量梯度关联。实验表明，对抗权重扰动通过抑制主导损失曲率模式降低鲁棒泛化差距，而过度惩罚反而可能损害优化效果。
Collaborative Synthetic Data Generation for Knowledge Transfer in Federated Learning
Authors: Maximilian Andreas Hoefler, Karsten Mueller, Wojciech Samek
First: 2026-07-08T15:56:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:56:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
One-shot federated learning (OSFL) addresses the communication overhead of federated learning by limiting training to a single round, but doing so without sacrificing model quality is non-trivial, particularly when client data distributions diverge. Recent work has addressed this challenge by aggregating client knowledge on the server through the construction of transferable synthetic datasets or distillates. However, most of these methods lack formal privacy guarantees, leaving a gap in jointly achieving low communication, robustness to heterogeneity, and rigorous privacy. We propose FedKT-CSD (Federated Knowledge Transfer via Collaborative Synthetic Data), a framework inspired by neural image compression that closes this gap by leveraging publicly pretrained autoencoders as a shared latent space. Each client encodes its private data in a single forward pass, computes class-conditional latent statistics, and transmits these to the server. The server aggregates these statistics via secure aggregation, adds calibrated differential privacy noise, and decodes a synthetic dataset for training a global model and further downstream tasks. This design provides formal $(\varepsilon,δ)$-differential privacy by construction, while keeping client-side computation and communication lightweight. Despite operating under privacy constraints, FedKT-CSD is competitive with and even outperforms non-private baselines across diverse datasets and heterogeneity settings, and scales to a large number of clients. Our code is available at: https://github.com/an7123/FedKT-CSD
中文标题/摘要
标题：联邦学习中基于协作合成数据的知识迁移
单次联邦学习（OSFL）通过限制训练仅进行一轮来缓解联邦学习的通信开销，但要在不牺牲模型质量的前提下实现这一点颇具挑战，尤其是在客户端数据分布不一致时。近期研究通过构建可迁移的合成数据集或蒸馏数据，在服务器端聚合客户端知识以应对这一挑战。然而，大多数方法缺乏形式化的隐私保障，难以同时实现低通信、对异构性的鲁棒性与严格的隐私保护。我们提出FedKT-CSD（通过协作合成数据实现联邦知识迁移）框架，该框架受神经图像压缩启发，利用公开预训练的自编码器作为共享潜在空间，从而弥合这一差距。每个客户端通过一次前向传播将其私有数据编码，计算类别条件潜在统计量并传输至服务器。服务器通过安全聚合聚合这些统计量，添加校准后的差分隐私噪声，并解码合成数据集用于训练全局模型及后续任务。该设计在构造上即提供形式化的(ε,δ)-差分隐私，同时保持客户端侧计算与通信轻量。尽管受限于隐私约束，FedKT-CSD在多种数据集和异构性设置下表现与非隐私基线相当，甚至更优，并可扩展至大量客户端。代码开源于：https://github.com/an7123/FedKT-CSD
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of achieving low communication and model robustness in one-shot federated learning without compromising privacy, the paper introduces FedKT-CSD, a framework that generates synthetic data collaboratively across clients using publicly pretrained autoencoders as a shared latent space. Each client encodes its data, computes class-conditional latent statistics, and transmits them to the server, which aggregates these statistics via secure aggregation, adds calibrated differential privacy noise, and decodes a synthetic dataset for global model training. Experiments show that FedKT-CSD achieves formal (ε,δ)-differential privacy while maintaining lightweight client-side computation and communication, and it outperforms non-private baselines across diverse datasets and client heterogeneity levels, even scaling effectively to large client numbers.
为在数据分布异构且需保障隐私的前提下实现单轮联邦学习，FedKT-CSD 利用公开预训练的自编码器构建共享潜在空间。各客户端编码本地数据、计算类别条件统计量并上传至服务器，服务器通过安全聚合、添加校准差分隐私噪声后解码合成数据用于全局模型训练。该方法在保证形式化 (ε,δ)-差分隐私的同时，客户端计算与通信开销极低，并在多种数据集和异构性场景下超越非隐私基线，且可有效扩展至大规模客户端。
EFlow: Learning Evidence Flow for Long-Video Reasoning with Adaptive Reflection
Authors: Wenhao Zhang, Kuanwei Lin, Xuyi Yang, Wei Gao, Ge Li
First: 2026-07-01T12:32:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:54:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-video reasoning is fundamentally constrained by how models acquire and utilize visual evidence. Existing tool-augmented video frameworks often interleave temporal grounding and answer reasoning within a single trajectory, causing early semantic hypotheses to bias evidence localization. We term this failure mode premature semantic commitment, where biased grounding retrieves incomplete evidence and incomplete evidence further reinforces incorrect reasoning. To address this issue, we propose EFlow, an evidence-first video reasoning framework built upon Qwen3-VL. EFlow explicitly separates temporal grounding and logical reasoning through CoT for Temporal Grounding and CoT for Reasoning, enabling the model to retrieve relevant evidence before answer inference. In addition, EFlow introduces a confidence-aware reflection mechanism that re-evaluates the full video when retrieved evidence is potentially insufficient. We further construct dedicated trajectory datasets and train EFlow through supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and reinforcement fine-tuning. Extensive experiments across five video understanding benchmarks demonstrate that EFlow consistently improves long-video reasoning performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EFlow：基于自适应反思的视觉证据流学习用于长视频推理
长视频推理的根本限制在于模型如何获取和利用视觉证据。现有工具增强型视频框架常将时间定位与答案推理交织于单一轨迹中，导致早期语义假设偏倚证据定位。我们称此失效模式为‘过早语义承诺’，即偏倚定位检索不完整证据，不完整证据进一步强化错误推理。为解决此问题，我们提出EFlow，一个基于Qwen3-VL的证据优先视频推理框架。EFlow通过‘CoT时间定位’与‘CoT推理’显式分离时间定位与逻辑推理，使模型在答案推理前检索相关证据。此外，EFlow引入置信度感知反思机制，在检索证据可能不足时重新评估完整视频。我们进一步构建专用轨迹数据集，并通过监督微调、强化学习及强化微调训练EFlow。在五个视频理解基准上的大量实验表明，EFlow持续提升长视频推理性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the problem of premature semantic commitment in long-video reasoning—where early hypotheses bias evidence retrieval and reinforce incorrect reasoning—EFlow introduces a framework that decouples temporal grounding from logical reasoning using CoT for each, allowing evidence collection before answer inference. It further employs a confidence-aware reflection mechanism to re-evaluate the full video when evidence is deemed insufficient. Evaluated across five benchmarks, EFlow consistently outperforms existing methods through supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and reinforcement fine-tuning.
为解决长视频推理中因早期语义假设导致的证据定位偏差和错误推理强化问题，EFlow提出一种先证据后推理的框架，通过CoT分别处理时间定位与逻辑推理，确保在答案生成前完成证据收集。同时引入置信度感知的反思机制，在证据不足时重新评估完整视频。在五个视频理解基准测试中，EFlow通过监督微调、强化学习和强化微调持续优于现有工具增强方法。
PALS: Percentile-Aware Layerwise Sparsity for LLM Pruning
Authors: Yazdan Jamshidi, Alexey Shvets
First: 2026-07-08T15:51:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:51:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
One-shot pruning methods like Wanda and SparseGPT apply the same sparsity ratio to every layer of a transformer, ignoring known variation in layer importance. We propose PALS (Percentile-Aware Layerwise Sparsity), which adjusts per-layer sparsity based on the 99th percentile of activation magnitudes, bounded to $\pm 5\%$ around the target ratio. On LLaMA-2-7B at 50\% sparsity, PALS achieves 10.96 WikiText-2 perplexity versus 12.92 for uniform Wanda (mean over 9 runs, $p < 0.001$). The benefit is architecture-dependent: LLaMA-3-8B shows marginal gains and Mistral-7B shows none. We also find that gradient-based allocation -- the seemingly more principled approach -- produces results worse than random, suggesting that gradient magnitude does not predict the impact of discrete weight removal. PALS adds negligible cost to the pruning pipeline and requires no fine-tuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PALS：基于百分位数感知的层间稀疏度方法用于大语言模型剪枝
像Wanda和SparseGPT这样的单次剪枝方法对Transformer每一层应用相同的稀疏率，忽略了已知的层重要性差异。我们提出PALS（百分位数感知层间稀疏度），根据激活幅度的第99百分位数调整每层稀疏度，且限制在目标比率±5%范围内。在LLaMA-2-7B模型50%稀疏度下，PALS实现10.96的WikiText-2困惑度，而均匀Wanda为12.92（9次运行平均值，p<0.001）。该优势依赖于架构：LLaMA-3-8B收益微弱，Mistral-7B无收益。我们还发现，基于梯度的分配方法——看似更合理的方法——效果劣于随机分配，表明梯度幅度无法预测离散权重移除的影响。PALS对剪枝流程增加的开销可忽略，且无需微调。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of uniform sparsity in transformer layers, PALS introduces a layerwise sparsity method that adapts pruning intensity per layer based on the 99th percentile of activation magnitudes, constrained within ±5% of the target ratio. Evaluated on LLaMA-2-7B at 50% sparsity, PALS achieved a significantly lower perplexity (10.96) compared to uniform Wanda (12.92), with statistical significance (p < 0.001). The benefit varies by architecture: LLaMA-3-8B showed minor gains while Mistral-7B showed none. Surprisingly, gradient-based allocation performed worse than random, indicating gradient magnitude is not a reliable predictor for discrete weight removal impact. PALS incurs negligible overhead and requires no fine-tuning.
为解决Transformer各层统一稀疏率带来的效率问题，PALS提出一种基于激活幅度99分位数动态调整每层稀疏度的方法，稀疏度控制在目标值±5%范围内。在LLaMA-2-7B模型50%稀疏度下，PALS在WikiText-2数据集上实现10.96的困惑度，显著优于统一稀疏方法Wanda的12.92（p<0.001），但效果依赖架构：LLaMA-3-8B收益微弱，Mistral-7B无提升。实验发现基于梯度的分配策略表现劣于随机，表明梯度大小不能有效预测离散权重移除的影响。PALS几乎无额外开销且无需微调。
BlossomPsy: A User-Centric AI System for Adaptive and Engaging MBTI Personality Assessments
Authors: Bingjia Huang
First: 2026-07-07T11:20:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:51:29+00:00
Comments: 23 pages,20 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
There has been growing public interest in understanding personality traits and emotional characteristics, as such knowledge helps individuals better accept themselves and manage negative emotions. While professional personality scales remain the standard tool for assessment, they are often perceived as tedious or inaccessible to the general public. AI-driven systems can make assessments more accessible, but it is difficult to balance user engagement with predictive consistency in existing works. We tackle this challenge by introducing BlossomPsy, a user-friendly AI-driven MBTI assessment system. MBTI, a widely recognized but psychometrically debated personality framework, serves as the foundation for many recent systems. BlossomPsy integrates multi-turn dialogue and photo-based questions to enhance user engagement while supporting confidence-aware predictions. By combining deep learning, multi-armed bandit algorithms, and control theory, the system dynamically adapts to users' responses. In particular, photo-based questions are designed to increase interactivity and provide additional user information, thereby improving prediction confidence. Experiments involving both human volunteers and large language models (LLMs) provide preliminary evidence that BlossomPsy can produce stable predictions, with higher reported user satisfaction compared to MBTI-M (Chinese version), while maintaining comparable consistency with the reference scale.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BlossomPsy：面向用户的AI系统，用于自适应且互动性强的MBTI性格评估
公众对理解性格特质和情绪特征的兴趣日益增长，此类知识有助于个体更好地接纳自我并管理负面情绪。尽管专业性格量表仍是评估标准工具，但常被认为繁琐或对大众不友好。AI驱动的系统可提升评估可及性，但现有研究难以平衡用户参与度与预测一致性。我们通过引入BlossomPsy——一个用户友好的AI驱动MBTI评估系统，解决此问题。MBTI作为广受认可但存在心理测量争议的性格框架，是许多近期系统的理论基础。BlossomPsy结合多轮对话与基于照片的问题，提升用户参与度，同时支持置信度感知的预测。通过融合深度学习、多臂老虎机算法与控制理论，系统动态适应用户回答。尤其，基于照片的问题旨在增强互动性并提供额外用户信息，从而提升预测置信度。涉及人类志愿者与大语言模型（LLMs）的实验初步表明，BlossomPsy可生成稳定预测，用户满意度高于MBTI-M（中文版），同时保持与参考量表相当的一致性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the public’s desire to understand personality traits for self-acceptance and emotional regulation, this work addresses the limitations of traditional MBTI assessments—perceived as tedious and inaccessible—by proposing BlossomPsy, an AI-driven system that balances engagement with predictive accuracy. The method combines multi-turn dialogue, photo-based questions, deep learning, multi-armed bandit algorithms, and control theory to dynamically adapt to user responses, enhancing interactivity and prediction confidence. Experimental results with human volunteers and LLMs show BlossomPsy achieves stable predictions comparable to professional scales while outperforming MBTI-M in user satisfaction.
鉴于公众对理解人格特质以促进自我接纳和情绪管理的需求，本研究针对传统MBTI评估工具被感知为枯燥且难以接触的问题，提出BlossomPsy系统，通过多轮对话与图片问答提升互动性，结合深度学习、多臂老虎机算法与控制理论实现动态适应与预测置信度提升。实验表明，该系统在保持与权威量表相当预测稳定性的同时，显著优于MBTI-M等现有工具，用户满意度明显提高。
AA-ViT: Anatomically Aware Vision Transformer with Structural and Frequency Guidance for Contrast Enhanced Brain MRI Synthesis
Authors: Talha Meraj, Tom Flannery, Charlie Cummins, Matt Townend, Thomas C Booth, Peter Crossley, Michael McCann, Ian Overton, Saritha Unnikrishnan
First: 2026-07-08T15:49:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:49:44+00:00
Comments: Accepted for Publication in MIUA 2026 proceedings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate tumour localization and diagnosis is a critical component of clinical care for brain cancers. Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is the most commonly used imaging modality due to its superior soft-tissue contrast. However, standard MRI often exhibits limited contrast and imaging artifacts, which necessitates the use of contrast agents to enhance lesion visibility. The administration of chemical contrast agents is not always feasible and may be contraindicated in patients with renal impairment or other health conditions. As a result, developing accurate and non-invasive contrast enhanced MRI (CEMRI) synthesis methods has clinical importance. In recent years, numerous approaches for CEMRI synthesis have been proposed, predominantly relying on generative artificial intelligence models. While these methods demonstrate promising performance, their dependence on implicit feature learning often limits their ability to preserve anatomical boundaries and tumour-specific fine structures. To address these challenges, we propose an anatomically aware frequency-and-structure-guided vision transformer (AA-ViT), for CEMRI synthesis using pre-contrast MRI modalities (T1, T2, and FLAIR). Experiments on the BraTS 2021 dataset demonstrate that the proposed method preserves anatomical and lesion boundaries, achieving higher PSNR and SSIM than state-of-the-art approaches. Clinical evaluation by three neuroradiologists and a neurosurgeon on 19 randomly selected cases across diverse gliomas yielded a mean score of 3.94/5, providing preliminary clinical validation rarely seen in prior studies. Synthetic post-contrast scans from our model could lower scanning costs, shorten imaging time, and avoid the potential risks of using gadolinium-based contrast agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AA-ViT：一种具有结构与频率引导的解剖学感知视觉变换器，用于增强对比度的脑部MRI合成
准确的肿瘤定位和诊断是脑癌临床治疗的关键环节。磁共振成像（MRI）因其卓越的软组织对比度而被最广泛使用。然而，标准MRI常存在对比度不足和成像伪影问题，需借助对比剂增强病灶可见性。但化学对比剂并非总可使用，且对肾功能不全或其他健康状况患者可能禁忌。因此，开发准确且无创的增强对比度MRI（CEMRI）合成方法具有临床意义。近年来，已提出多种CEMRI合成方法，主要依赖生成式人工智能模型。尽管这些方法表现优异，但其依赖隐式特征学习常导致难以保留解剖边界和肿瘤特异性精细结构。为解决此问题，我们提出一种解剖学感知、频率与结构引导的视觉变换器（AA-ViT），利用非增强MRI模态（T1、T2和FLAIR）合成CEMRI。在BraTS 2021数据集上的实验表明，该方法能有效保留解剖与病灶边界，PSNR和SSIM优于现有最先进方法。三位神经放射科医生和一名神经外科医生对19例不同胶质瘤随机病例的临床评估平均得分为3.94/5，提供了罕见的初步临床验证。本模型合成的增强后扫描可降低检查成本、缩短成像时间，并避免使用钆基对比剂的潜在风险。
Summary / 总结
To address the clinical need for non-invasive contrast-enhanced MRI synthesis without relying on chemical contrast agents, this study proposes AA-ViT, a vision transformer that integrates anatomical structure and frequency guidance to enhance lesion visibility in pre-contrast MRI scans. The model leverages T1, T2, and FLAIR modalities to generate synthetic post-contrast images while preserving anatomical boundaries and tumor-specific fine structures. Evaluated on the BraTS 2021 dataset, AA-ViT outperformed existing methods in PSNR and SSIM metrics, and received a mean clinical score of 3.94/5 from three neuroradiologists and a neurosurgeon, demonstrating its potential to reduce imaging costs, shorten scan times, and avoid risks associated with gadolinium contrast agents.
为解决无需注射对比剂即可生成高质量增强MRI的需求，本文提出AA-ViT模型，通过结构与频域引导的视觉Transformer，利用T1、T2和FLAIR等非增强MRI图像合成增强图像。该模型在BraTS 2021数据集上表现优于现有方法，PSNR与SSIM指标更高，且经三位神经放射科医生和一名神经外科医生评估，平均得分为3.94/5，初步验证了其在临床诊断中的可靠性与解剖结构保真度。
Nectar: Neural Estimation of Cached-Token Attention via Regression
Authors: João Monteiro, Michal Klein, Pierre Ablin, Marco Cuturi
First: 2026-05-10T21:51:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:49:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluating softmax attention over a fixed long context requires reading every cached key-value pair for each new query token. For a given context (a book, a manual, a legal corpus) the attention output is a deterministic function of the query. We propose Nectar, which fits a compact neural network to this function for queries drawn from a task-relevant distribution. Nectar fits two networks per layer and KV-head: a target network that predicts the attention output and a score network that predicts the log-normalizer. The pair plugs into the standard masked self-attention at inference time, replacing the $O(n)$ attention over the cache with a forward pass whose cost does not depend on $n$. Each module carries on the order of $|θ|$ parameters per layer and KV-head, typically much smaller than the $2nd$ KV-cache footprint at the same granularity. We report experiments on models from 1.7B to 8B parameters across five long-context datasets. The approximation error tracks the next-token accuracy gap to full attention, and allocating capacity non-uniformly across layers reduces that gap in our ablation. Beyond this analysis of metrics, we check that the text generations (following a question prompt) of a model equipped with a Nectar module match in semantic content those obtained by giving the same model access to the full cache.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Nectar：通过回归估计缓存令牌注意力的神经方法
在固定长上下文上评估softmax注意力时，每个新查询令牌都需要读取所有缓存的键值对。对于给定上下文（如书籍、手册、法律语料库），注意力输出是查询的确定性函数。我们提出Nectar，通过从任务相关分布中采样的查询拟合一个紧凑的神经网络来逼近该函数。Nectar为每一层和每个KV头拟合两个网络：一个目标网络预测注意力输出，一个得分网络预测对数归一化项。该对在推理时替换标准的掩码自注意力，将原本依赖于n的O(n)注意力计算替换为成本与n无关的前向传播。每个模块每层每KV头的参数量约为|θ|，通常远小于相同粒度下2nd KV缓存的存储开销。我们在五个长上下文数据集上对1.7B至8B参数的模型进行了实验。近似误差与完整注意力的下一个词准确率差距保持一致，且在分层非均匀分配容量的消融实验中，该差距有所缩小。此外，我们验证了配备Nectar模块的模型在回答问题提示时生成的文本，在语义内容上与直接访问完整缓存的模型输出一致。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational cost of evaluating attention over long cached contexts, the paper proposes Nectar, a neural approximation method that learns compact networks to predict attention outputs and log-normalizers for queries drawn from task-relevant distributions. For each layer and KV-head, Nectar trains two networks: one to estimate attention weights and another to approximate the normalization term. At inference time, these networks replace the O(n) attention computation over cached tokens with a constant-time forward pass, whose parameter count is typically much smaller than the KV-cache footprint. Experiments on models ranging from 1.7B to 8B parameters across five long-context datasets show that the approximation error closely correlates with the drop in next-token accuracy compared to full attention, and non-uniform capacity allocation across layers further reduces this gap without compromising semantic fidelity of generated text.
为降低长上下文缓存中注意力计算的高开销，本文提出Nectar方法，用轻量级神经网络替代昂贵的O(n)注意力计算。Nectar为每层和每个KV头训练两个网络：一个预测注意力输出，另一个预测对数归一化项，二者共同替代推理阶段的完整注意力机制。在1.7B至8B参数规模模型及五个长上下文数据集上的实验表明，Nectar的近似误差与相对于完整注意力的下一词准确率下降高度相关，且跨层非均匀分配容量可进一步缩小该差距。重要的是，使用Nectar模块生成的文本在语义内容上与拥有完整缓存的模型输出一致。
Think Big, Search Small: Where Capacity Matters in Hierarchical Search Agents?
Authors: Qinnan Cai, Yibo Zhao, Xiang Li
First: 2026-07-08T15:46:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:46:48+00:00
Comments: 21pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language model based search agents increasingly adopt multi-agent architectures in which a main agent decomposes a complex question into sub-queries and dispatches them to parallel sub-agents. However, existing systems instantiate all roles from a single model of identical scale, leaving open how model capacity should be distributed across roles. We factorize hierarchical search into three roles: a delegation role responsible for task decomposition, an execution role responsible for retrieval and evidence extraction, and an answer generation role held fixed as a confound control. We then conduct controlled capacity sweeps along the delegation and execution axes on five multi-hop QA benchmarks. The experiments yield three findings. First, role factorization consistently outperforms a single-agent baseline, improving exact match from 4.5 to 8.6 points across six model scales. Second, capacity sensitivity is asymmetric: scaling the delegation backbone improves EM by ~11 points, whereas scaling the execution sub-agent moves EM by only ~2.6 points, identifying decomposition as the capability bottleneck. Third, a 1.7B-parameter executor trained via quality-filtered trajectory distillation matches a frontier sub-agent in accuracy while consuming 37% fewer sub-agent tokens, advancing the Pareto frontier. These results suggest a concrete recipe for building hierarchical search agents: concentrate capacity at delegation and downsize execution without sacrificing accuracy. Our code is available at https://github.com/QinnanCai0115/role-factorized-search.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大而思细：层级搜索代理中容量分配的关键位置？
基于大语言模型的搜索代理越来越多地采用多代理架构，其中主代理将复杂问题分解为子查询并分发给并行子代理。然而，现有系统从单一规模模型实例化所有角色，未明确模型容量应如何在不同角色间分配。我们将层级搜索分解为三个角色：负责任务分解的委派角色、负责检索与证据提取的执行角色，以及作为混淆控制保持固定的答案生成角色。随后，我们在五个多跳问答基准上沿委派与执行轴进行受控容量扫描实验。实验得出三项发现：第一，角色分解在六种模型规模下始终优于单代理基线，精确匹配率从4.5提升至8.6；第二，容量敏感性呈非对称性：扩大委派主干模型可提升精确匹配率约11点，而扩大执行子代理仅提升约2.6点，表明分解能力是瓶颈；第三，通过质量过滤轨迹蒸馏训练的1.7B参数执行器，在准确率上媲美前沿子代理，同时节省37%的子代理token，推动帕累托前沿。这些结果为构建层级搜索代理提供明确方案：集中容量于委派角色，缩小执行角色规模而不牺牲精度。代码见https://github.com/QinnanCai0115/role-factorized-search。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of uniformly scaling large language models across all roles in hierarchical search agents, this study investigates how to optimally distribute model capacity among three distinct roles: delegation (task decomposition), execution (retrieval and evidence extraction), and answer generation (held fixed as a control). Through controlled experiments on five multi-hop QA benchmarks, the authors systematically vary capacity allocation along the delegation and execution axes. Results show that role factorization significantly outperforms a single-agent baseline, boosting exact match scores from 4.5 to 8.6 across six model scales. Crucially, delegation scaling yields a 11-point EM gain versus only 2.6 points from execution scaling, indicating decomposition is the primary bottleneck. Furthermore, a 1.7B-parameter execution agent trained via trajectory distillation achieves frontier-level accuracy while using 37% fewer tokens, demonstrating a Pareto-efficient design.
为了解决当前多智能体搜索系统中统一使用同等规模模型的效率问题，本研究探讨如何在任务分解（委托角色）、检索与证据提取（执行角色）和答案生成（固定控制角色）三个角色间合理分配模型容量。通过在五个多跳问答基准上系统性调整委托与执行角色的容量，实验发现角色分解显著优于单智能体基线，使精确匹配得分从4.5提升至8.6（跨六种模型规模）。更重要的是，容量敏感性呈现不对称性：委托角色扩容带来约11点EM提升，而执行角色扩容仅贡献约2.6点，表明任务分解是主要瓶颈。此外，通过质量过滤轨迹蒸馏训练的1.7B参数执行器，实现与前沿执行器相当的准确率，同时节省37%的子智能体token，证明执行角色可被压缩而不牺牲性能。研究结果表明，应集中容量于委托角色并缩小执行角色规模，以构建高效分层搜索代理。
Face-trace: Open-Set Attribution and Progressive Discovery of Synthetic Face Generators
Authors: Alessia Infantino, Claudio Schiavella, Irene Amerini
First: 2026-07-08T15:42:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:42:58+00:00
Comments: Preprint. 17 pages, 16 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in generative Artificial Intelligence have made synthetic face images increasingly realistic, creating new challenges for multimedia forensics. Source attribution methods should not only identify the generator of an image when the source is known, but also handle samples produced by previously unseen models. However, most existing approaches address synthetic face attribution in a closed-set setting, where all possible generators are available during training. This assumption does not hold in real-world scenarios, where new generators continuously appear and rejected samples should be organized rather than simply discarded. In this work we propose a pipeline for open-set synthetic face source attribution that combines known generator classification, energy-based OOD rejection, and unknown generator discovery. A classifier is trained on known generators using frozen I-JEPA embeddings, while rejected samples are represented by combining projected I-JEPA features with Forensic Self-Descriptors and then clustered to discover groups of unknown generators. We also extend the discovery stage to an incremental scenario, where rejected samples arrive over time. Experiments on the WILD dataset show that the proposed method achieves 96.73% closed-set attribution accuracy. In the open-set setting, energy-based rejection reaches 71.25% balanced accuracy, while rejected samples are clustered into meaningful unknown-generator groups, obtaining an ARI of 0.81, an NMI of 0.90, and an overall clustering purity of 87.74%. In the incremental setting, the discovered generator space is progressively extended while maintaining a final purity of 99.23%. Cross-dataset experiments suggest that the pipeline can operate beyond the original dataset distribution, although post-processing remains challenging.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Face-trace：开放集归因与合成人脸生成器的渐进发现
生成式人工智能的最新进展使合成人脸图像日益逼真，为多媒体取证带来了新挑战。源归因方法不仅应在已知源时识别图像生成器，还应处理由先前未见过的模型生成的样本。然而，大多数现有方法在封闭集设定下处理合成人脸归因，即训练时所有可能的生成器均已知。这一假设在现实场景中不成立，因为新生成器持续出现，被拒样本应被组织而非简单丢弃。本文提出一种开放集合成人脸源归因的流程，结合已知生成器分类、基于能量的OOD拒识与未知生成器发现。使用冻结的I-JEPA嵌入在已知生成器上训练分类器，而被拒样本则通过结合投影I-JEPA特征与取证自描述符进行表示，并聚类以发现未知生成器组。我们还将发现阶段扩展至增量场景，即被拒样本随时间到达。在WILD数据集上的实验表明，所提方法在封闭集归因准确率达96.73%。在开放集设定下，基于能量的拒识达到71.25%的平衡准确率，被拒样本聚类为有意义的未知生成器组，获得ARI为0.81、NMI为0.90、整体聚类纯度为87.74%。在增量设定下，生成器空间逐步扩展，最终纯度保持在99.23%。跨数据集实验表明，该流程可超越原始数据分布，尽管后处理仍具挑战。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to attribute synthetic face images to their generators even when encountering previously unseen models, this work proposes an open-set attribution pipeline combining known-generator classification, energy-based out-of-distribution rejection, and incremental discovery of unknown generators. Using frozen I-JEPA embeddings for known generators and projecting rejected samples with forensic self-descriptors for clustering, the method identifies unknown generator groups. Experiments on WILD show 96.73% closed-set accuracy, 71.25% balanced accuracy in open-set rejection, and high clustering purity (87.74%) with ARI 0.81 and NMI 0.90. In incremental settings, purity remains high at 99.23%, and cross-dataset tests indicate generalization capability despite post-processing challenges.
为应对生成式AI合成人脸日益逼真带来的多媒体取证挑战，本文提出一种开放集人脸生成器归因管道，结合已知生成器分类、基于能量的异常检测与未知生成器发现。方法利用冻结的I-JEPA嵌入训练已知生成器分类器，对拒绝样本则融合I-JEPA特征与取证自描述特征进行聚类，以发现未知生成器群体。在WILD数据集上，闭集归因准确率达96.73%，开放集拒绝平衡准确率71.25%，未知生成器聚类纯度达87.74%，增量发现场景下最终纯度保持99.23%。跨数据集实验表明该管道具备泛化能力，但后处理仍具挑战。
Participatory provenance as representational auditing for AI-mediated public consultation
Authors: Sachit Mahajan
First: 2026-04-22T15:54:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:41:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artificial intelligence is increasingly deployed to synthesize large-scale public input in policy consultations and participatory processes. Yet no formal framework exists for auditing whether these summaries faithfully represent the source population, an accountability gap that existing approaches to AI explainability, grounding and hallucination detection do not address because they focus on output quality rather than input fidelity. Here, participatory provenance is introduced: a measurement framework grounded in optimal transport theory, causal inference and semantic analysis that tracks how individual public submissions are transformed, filtered or lost through AI-mediated summarization. Applied to Canada's 2025-2026 national AI Strategy consultation ($n = 5{,}253$ respondents across two independent policy topics), the framework reveals that both official government summaries underperform a random-participant baseline ($-9.1\%$ and $-8.0\%$ coverage degradation), with $16.9\%$ and $15.3\%$ of participants effectively excluded. Exclusion concentrates in clusters expressing dissent, scepticism and critique of AI ($33$-$88\%$ exclusion rates). Brevity, semantic isolation and rhetorical register independently predict representational outcome. An accompanying open-source interactive tool, the Co-creation Provenance Lab, enables policymakers to audit and iteratively improve summaries, establishing genuine human-in-the-loop oversight at scale.
中文标题/摘要
标题：作为AI介导公共咨询代表性审计的参与式溯源
人工智能正越来越多地用于综合大规模公众意见以支持政策咨询和参与式过程。然而，目前尚无正式框架用于审计这些摘要是否忠实代表了原始人群，而现有AI可解释性、锚定性和幻觉检测方法未能解决这一问责缺口，因为它们关注的是输出质量而非输入保真度。本文引入“参与式溯源”：一种基于最优传输理论、因果推断和语义分析的测量框架，用于追踪个体公众提交内容如何通过AI介导的摘要过程被转化、过滤或丢失。该框架应用于加拿大2025-2026年国家AI战略咨询（涵盖两个独立政策议题，共5253名受访者），结果显示，官方政府摘要均劣于随机参与者基线（覆盖率分别下降9.1%和8.0%），且分别有16.9%和15.3%的参与者被实质排除。排除现象集中于表达异议、怀疑和对AI批判的群体（排除率33%-88%）。简洁性、语义孤立性和修辞语体独立预测代表性结果。配套开源交互式工具“共创溯源实验室”使政策制定者能够审计并迭代改进摘要，实现大规模真正的人类参与式监督。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of formal methods to audit whether AI-generated summaries of public input accurately reflect the source population, the paper introduces 'participatory provenance'—a framework combining optimal transport theory, causal inference, and semantic analysis to trace how individual submissions are transformed or excluded during AI-mediated summarization.
