为解决AI生成的公共意见摘要是否真实反映原始群体的问题，本文提出“参与式溯源”框架，结合最优传输理论、因果推断和语义分析，追踪个体意见在AI摘要过程中的转化与流失。应用于加拿大2025-2026人工智能战略咨询（5253名受访者），结果显示官方摘要覆盖率较随机参与者基线分别下降9.1%和8.0%，实际排除16.9%和15.3%的参与者，持异议群体被排除比例高达33–88%。简短性、语义孤立性和修辞风格是影响代表性偏差的关键因素，同时开发了开源工具“共创溯源实验室”，支持政策制定者进行迭代审计与人类参与监督。
SonoRank: Towards Calibration-Free Real-Time Finger Flexion Detection from Forearm Ultrasound Sequences
Authors: Dean Zadok, Alon Wolf, Alex M. Bronstein, Oren Salzman
First: 2026-07-08T15:41:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:41:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Powered prosthetic hands are frequently abandoned, largely due to the limited functionality of current devices that rely on surface electromyography (sEMG). Sonomyography (ultrasound) has emerged as a promising alternative, owing to its ability to observe muscle activity in real time and control a greater number of degrees of freedom. Yet, existing ultrasound-based methods require per-user fine-tuning, limiting their commercialization. We propose SonoRank, an important step towards calibration-free finger flexion detection from forearm ultrasound video. SonoRank first learns to rank pairs of ultrasound sequences by their relative motion magnitude for each of the five fingers. The learned representations are then fine-tuned to classify whether each finger is actively flexing, using a rest reference that is captured at the beginning of the operation. Under 12-fold leave-one-subject-out cross-validation on a dataset of twelve subjects with synchronized kinematics, SonoRank achieves a 28% improvement in F1 score over direct classification baselines that skip the ranking stage. These results establish pairwise ranking as an effective pretraining signal for subject-independent control, bringing ultrasound-based prosthetics closer to practical, calibration-free deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SonoRank：面向免校准实时前臂超声序列手指屈曲检测
动力假肢手常被弃用，主要因当前依赖表面肌电图（sEMG）的设备功能有限。超声成像（sonomyography）因其能实时观察肌肉活动并控制更多自由度，成为有前景的替代方案。但现有超声方法需用户个性化调校，限制其商业化。我们提出SonoRank，是迈向免校准前臂超声视频手指屈曲检测的重要一步。SonoRank首先学习对五指的超声序列按相对运动幅度进行排序。随后，利用操作开始时采集的静息参考状态，对学习到的表示进行微调，以分类每指是否主动屈曲。在包含十二名受试者同步运动学数据的十二折留一受试者交叉验证中，SonoRank相较跳过排序阶段的直接分类基线方法，F1分数提升28%。该结果证实成对排序作为免用户校准控制的有效预训练信号，使超声假肢更接近实用化、免校准部署。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limited functionality of current sEMG-powered prosthetics and the potential of ultrasound for real-time muscle observation, the paper introduces SonoRank, a calibration-free method for finger flexion detection from forearm ultrasound sequences. SonoRank first learns to rank pairs of ultrasound frames by relative motion magnitude for each finger, then fine-tunes these representations to classify active flexion using a rest reference captured at session start. Evaluated on 12 subjects with synchronized kinematics under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation, SonoRank achieved a 28% higher F1 score than direct classification baselines, demonstrating that pairwise ranking serves as an effective pretraining signal for subject-independent control.
为了解决当前依赖表面肌电图的假肢功能有限、用户需频繁校准的问题，该研究提出SonoRank方法，通过前臂超声视频实现无需校准的指关节屈曲检测。该方法首先学习对每根手指的超声帧对按相对运动幅度进行排序，再利用初始静止状态作为参考，微调排序表示以分类当前是否屈曲。在12名受试者同步运动数据的12折交叉验证中，SonoRank相较跳过排序阶段的直接分类基线方法F1分数提升28%，验证了成对排序作为跨用户控制的有效预训练信号。
Avoiding unsafe sets when training with Langevin Dynamics
Authors: Adam M. Oberman
First: 2026-07-08T15:36:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:36:45+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training a model with noisy gradient descent can be idealized as overdamped Langevin dynamics on the loss landscape, and a natural safety question is to bound the probability $ν_t(\mathcal{A}_H) = \mathbb{P}(Q_t \in \mathcal{A}_H)$ that the trajectory lies in a designated failure region $\mathcal{A}_H$. We study this for a smooth, strongly convex loss in $d$ dimensions and a failure region separated from the minimizer by an energy gap. Three bounds emerge. At the end of training, the equilibrium mass $π(\mathcal{A}_H)$ is exponentially small in $d$, with a complementary energy-barrier rate when the noise is small. Along the trajectory, a shape-free bound $ν_t(\mathcal{A}_H) \le π(\mathcal{A}_H)(1 + \sqrt{χ_0^2/π(\mathcal{A}_H)}\,e^{-mt})$ shows that the in-set probability relaxes to (twice) the static value after a burn-in time of order $d$, using only the global spectral gap $m$ of the loss. A worked Ornstein-Uhlenbeck example shows this burn-in is necessary: an angular slice of the equilibrium shell can transiently swell by a factor exponential in $d$, even though its equilibrium mass is tiny. To rule such swelling out we introduce a local relaxation rate attached to the failure region, defined through the spectral measure of its centered indicator rather than a Dirichlet-form Rayleigh quotient. For geometrically isolated regions this rate exceeds the global one, shrinking the burn-in proportionally, and combined with a maximum-principle ceiling it caps the trajectory probability uniformly in time. The picture is that strong convexity sets how fast training relaxes, but the shape of the unsafe set decides whether the trajectory bulges through it on the way home.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用朗之万动力学训练时避免不安全集合
使用带噪声的梯度下降训练模型可理想化为损失景观上的过阻尼朗之万动力学，一个自然的安全问题是界定轨迹位于指定失效区域 $\mathcal{A}_H$ 的概率 $ν_t(\mathcal{A}_H) = \mathbb{P}(Q_t \in \mathcal{A}_H)$。我们研究了在 $d$ 维空间中光滑且强凸的损失函数，且失效区域与最小值点之间存在能量间隙的情形。得出三个界限：训练结束时，平衡质量 $π(\mathcal{A}_H)$ 随 $d$ 指数衰减，当噪声较小时，衰减速率与能量屏障互补；沿轨迹，一个无形状依赖的界限 $ν_t(\mathcal{A}_H) \le π(\mathcal{A}_H)(1 + \sqrt{χ_0^2/π(\mathcal{A}_H)}\,e^{-mt})$ 表明，在经过与 $d$ 同阶的烧入时间后，轨迹内概率收敛至静态值的两倍，仅需使用损失函数的全局谱间隙 $m$；一个工作示例（奥恩斯坦-乌伦贝克过程）表明该烧入时间是必要的：即使其平衡质量极小，其平衡壳层的角切片在瞬态中可膨胀至指数于 $d$ 的因子。为排除此类膨胀，我们引入与失效区域关联的局部松弛率，通过其居中指示函数的谱测度定义，而非狄利克雷形式的瑞利商。对于几何孤立区域，该局部率超过全局率，从而按比例缩短烧入时间，结合最大值原理上限，可对轨迹概率在时间上统一限制。整体图景是：强凸性决定训练松弛速度，但不安全集合的形状决定轨迹是否在返回途中膨胀穿过它。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to quantify safety during noisy gradient descent training, this work analyzes the probability that a Langevin dynamics trajectory enters a failure region in a strongly convex loss landscape. Using a spectral analysis of the loss function’s generator, it derives three bounds: an equilibrium bound showing exponential decay in dimension for the failure region’s mass, a trajectory bound showing relaxation to twice the equilibrium value after a burn-in time proportional to dimension, and a local relaxation rate that accounts for the geometry of the failure region. Experimental analysis via an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck example reveals that without local geometric consideration, failure region probabilities can transiently swell exponentially with dimension, even if equilibrium mass is negligible. The paper introduces a local spectral measure-based relaxation rate that outperforms the global spectral gap for geometrically isolated regions, reducing burn-in time and providing a uniform time-bound on trajectory probabilities.
为量化噪声梯度下降训练中的安全性，该研究分析强凸损失函数下朗之万动力学轨迹进入失效区域的概率。通过损失函数的谱性质，推导出三类界：平衡态界显示失效区域质量随维度呈指数衰减；轨迹界表明轨迹概率在与维度成比例的烧入时间内收敛至平衡值的两倍；局部松弛率结合几何形状可防止瞬时膨胀，并通过最大值原理确保轨迹概率随时间均匀有界。实验表明烧入时间必要，且局部谱测度可缩短烧入时间。
Infinite Worlds with Versatile Interactions
Authors: Zelin Gao, Qiuyu Wang, Jiapeng Zhu, Jingye Chen, Zichen Liu, Qingyan Bai, Jiahao Wang, Yufeng Yuan, Hanlin Wang, Yichong Lu, Ka Leong Cheng, Haojie Zhang, Jian Gao, Tianrui Feng, Yuzheng Liu, Yao Yao, Yinghao Xu, Xing Zhu, Yujun Shen, Hao Ouyang
First: 2026-07-08T15:33:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:33:25+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://technology.robbyant.com/lingbot-world-v2 Code: https://github.com/robbyant/lingbot-world-v2
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We present LingBot-World 2.0 (also known as LingBot-World-Infinity), an advanced iteration of LingBot-World featuring four distinct upgrades. (1) Our model achieves an unbounded interaction horizon while maintaining consistent output quality, benefiting from a carefully crafted causal pretraining paradigm. (2) Through distilling a real-time variant from the base model, our system guarantees rapid response time, sufficient to drive 720p video streams at 60 fps. (3) Compared to the previous version, this update introduces highly diverse interactive elements, comprising a broader spectrum of actions (e.g., attacking, archery, spell-casting, and shooting) alongside a richer variety of text-driven events. (4) We pioneer the integration of an agentic harness within the domain of world modeling, wherein a pilot agent is tasked with planning and executing character behaviors, while a director agent is responsible for synthesizing novel environmental elements as the scene progresses. Additionally, to facilitate a shared experience, we develop an interface that permits multiple players to simultaneously immerse themselves in this vivid world simulator. We pair our primary 14B model with a lightweight 1.3B counterpart, which supports effortless deployment on a single GPU.
中文标题/摘要
标题：具备多功能交互的无限世界
我们推出了LingBot-World 2.0（亦称LingBot-World-Infinity），这是LingBot-World的高级迭代版本，包含四项显著升级。（1）我们的模型在保持输出质量稳定的同时实现了无界交互时长，得益于精心设计的因果预训练范式。（2）通过从基础模型蒸馏出实时变体，系统确保快速响应，足以支持720p视频流以60fps播放。（3）相较于前一版本，本更新引入了高度多样化的交互元素，涵盖更广泛的行动类型（如攻击、射箭、施法、射击）及更丰富的文本驱动事件。（4）我们首次在世界建模领域集成代理框架：飞行员代理负责规划与执行角色行为，导演代理则负责在场景推进中合成新颖环境元素。此外，为促进多人共享体验，我们开发了支持多人同时沉浸于该生动世界模拟器的界面。我们同时提供主模型（14B）与轻量级模型（1.3B），后者可轻松部署于单张GPU。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, high-fidelity interactive world simulation, LingBot-World 2.0 introduces four key upgrades: an unbounded interaction horizon via causal pretraining, real-time responsiveness through distilled inference, expanded action and event diversity, and an agentic architecture with pilot and director agents managing character behavior and environmental synthesis. Experimental results demonstrate consistent output quality over extended interactions, real-time 720p video streaming at 60 fps, and richly varied gameplay experiences enabled by the new agent system, all while supporting multi-user immersion and deployable on a single GPU via a lightweight 1.3B companion model.
为实现可扩展、高保真且实时响应的交互世界，LingBot-World 2.0推出四项关键升级：通过因果预训练实现无界交互时长，通过蒸馏模型保障60帧720p视频流的实时响应，扩展了包括攻击、射箭、施法等多样动作与文本驱动事件，引入代理架构（飞行员与导演代理）分别管理角色行为与环境生成。实验表明，系统在长时交互中保持输出质量稳定，响应速度满足高分辨率视频需求，多玩家同步沉浸体验显著提升，且1.3B轻量模型支持单GPU高效部署。
Towards Understanding Steering Strength
Authors: Magamed Taimeskhanov, Samuel Vaiter, Damien Garreau
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-02T19:25:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:31:49+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication at ICML 2026 (50 pages)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A popular approach to post-training control of large language models (LLMs) is the steering of intermediate latent representations. Namely, identify a well-chosen direction depending on the task at hand and perturbs representations along this direction at inference time. While many propositions exist to pick this direction, considerably less is understood about how to choose the magnitude of the move, whereas its importance is clear: too little and the intended behavior does not emerge, too much and the model's performance degrades beyond repair. In this work, we propose the first theoretical analysis of steering strength. We characterize its effect on next token probability, presence of a concept, and cross-entropy, deriving precise qualitative laws governing these quantities. Our analysis reveals surprising behaviors, including non-monotonic effects of steering strength. We validate our theoretical predictions empirically on eleven language models, ranging from a small GPT architecture to modern models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：理解转向强度的研究
在大型语言模型（LLMs）的后训练控制中，一种流行的方法是转向中间潜在表示。即根据任务选择合适的方向，并在推理时沿该方向扰动表示。虽然已有多种方法用于选择该方向，但对如何选择移动幅度的理解却少得多，而其重要性显而易见：移动过小则无法实现预期行为，移动过大则模型性能会严重下降。本文首次对转向强度进行了理论分析，刻画了其对下一个词概率、概念存在性及交叉熵的影响，推导出精确的定性规律。我们的分析揭示了令人惊讶的行为，包括转向强度的非单调效应。我们在十一种语言模型上实证验证了理论预测，涵盖从小型GPT架构到现代模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better understand how much to perturb latent representations in post-training control of large language models, this work introduces the first theoretical analysis of steering strength. It derives precise qualitative laws governing how steering magnitude affects next-token probability, concept presence, and cross-entropy, revealing non-monotonic behaviors that contradict intuitive expectations. Empirical validation across eleven models—from small GPT architectures to modern LLMs—confirms these theoretical predictions, demonstrating that optimal steering strength is task-dependent and often counterintuitive.
为理解大语言模型后训练控制中扰动潜空间向量的幅度，本文首次从理论上分析了“引导强度”的影响，推导出其对下一词概率、概念存在性及交叉熵的精确定性规律，揭示了引导强度的非单调效应等反直觉现象。实验在十一类模型（从小型GPT架构到现代大模型）上验证了理论预测，表明最优引导强度因任务而异，且常与直觉不符。
Generating Personalized Lower-Limb Kinematics Across Walking Speeds Using Subject-Conditioned Diffusion
Authors: Diya Dinesh, Adrian Krieger, Changseob Song, Dongho Park, Aaron J. Young, Inseung Kang
First: 2026-07-08T15:29:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:29:18+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Personalizing exoskeleton assistance requires user-specific gait data across many locomotor tasks, yet collecting this data demands repeated motion capture sessions that are costly, time-intensive, and especially burdensome for clinical populations. This challenge is most acute across walking speeds, where gait changes substantially and deviates further in clinical gait. This work introduces a subject-conditioned residual diffusion framework that generates personalized lower-limb kinematics at unseen walking speeds from a subject's gait sequence at a single seen speed. Given sagittal-plane hip, knee, and ankle trajectories at a seen speed and a desired unseen speed, the model generates a residual that transforms the seen trajectory into the unseen one, using a transformer denoiser conditioned on the subject's gait and the two speeds through feature-wise linear modulation. Trained only on able-bodied data, the model achieved a mean absolute error (MAE) of 3.4° on held-out able-bodied subjects. Without any stroke-specific fine-tuning, it achieved a 6.0° MAE on out-of-training-distribution stroke subjects, retaining subject identity for clinical gait. The framework reduced the MAE by over 70% relative to supervised feed-forward baselines, and a single seen speed matched the accuracy of four speeds within 0.4°. These results demonstrate that subject-conditioned residual diffusion can synthesize personalized gait across speeds from minimal data, reducing the collection burden for downstream exoskeleton personalization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用受试者条件扩散生成跨步行速度的个性化下肢运动学
个性化外骨骼辅助需要用户在多种运动任务下的特定步态数据，但收集这些数据需多次运动捕捉，成本高、耗时长，尤其对临床人群负担沉重。这一挑战在步行速度变化时尤为突出，步态变化显著，临床步态偏离更大。本文提出一种受试者条件残差扩散框架，可从受试者在单一已知速度下的步态序列生成其在未知速度下的个性化下肢运动学。给定矢状面髋、膝、踝在已知速度下的轨迹及目标未知速度，模型通过基于受试者步态和两个速度的特征线性调制条件下的Transformer去噪器，生成残差以将已知轨迹转换为未知轨迹。该模型仅在健康人群数据上训练，在独立健康受试者上达到3.4°的平均绝对误差（MAE）。无需针对中风患者进行任何微调，其在训练分布外的中风患者上仍保持6.0° MAE，并保留临床步态的受试者身份。该框架相较监督前馈基线降低MAE超过70%，且单一已知速度的精度与四速度相当（误差仅0.4°）。结果表明，受试者条件残差扩散可从少量数据合成跨速度的个性化步态，显著降低下游外骨骼个性化所需的数据采集负担。
Summary / 总结
To address the high cost and burden of collecting personalized gait data across multiple walking speeds—especially for clinical populations—this work proposes a subject-conditioned residual diffusion model that generates personalized lower-limb kinematics at unseen speeds using only a single seen speed. The method employs a transformer-based denoiser conditioned on subject-specific gait and speed information via feature-wise linear modulation to compute a residual transformation between trajectories. Trained solely on able-bodied data, the model achieved 3.4° mean absolute error on unseen able-bodied subjects and 6.0° on stroke patients without fine-tuning, preserving subject identity. It outperformed supervised baselines by over 70% and matched multi-speed accuracy with a single speed, demonstrating its efficiency for exoskeleton personalization.
为降低临床人群收集多步速个性化步态数据的成本与负担，本文提出一种基于主体条件的残差扩散模型，仅用单一步速的运动捕捉数据即可生成未见过步速下的个性化下肢运动学数据。该模型采用基于Transformer的去噪器，通过特征级线性调制结合主体步态特征与步速信息，计算轨迹间的残差变换。仅在健康人群数据上训练，模型在未见健康受试者上达到3.4°平均绝对误差（MAE），在未见过分布的中风患者上无需微调即达6.0° MAE，仍保持主体身份。相比监督前馈基线，误差降低超70%，且单步速精度接近四步速的0.4°以内。
From system models to class models: An in-context learning paradigm
Authors: Marco Forgione, Filippo Pura, Dario Piga
First: 2023-08-25T13:50:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:28:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Is it possible to understand the intricacies of a dynamical system not solely from its input/output pattern, but also by observing the behavior of other systems within the same class? This central question drives the study presented in this paper. In response to this query, we introduce a novel paradigm for system identification, addressing two primary tasks: one-step-ahead prediction and multi-step simulation. Unlike conventional methods, we do not directly estimate a model for the specific system. Instead, we learn a meta model that represents a class of dynamical systems. This meta model is trained on a potentially infinite stream of synthetic data, generated by simulators whose settings are randomly extracted from a probability distribution. When provided with a context from a new system-specifically, an input/output sequence-the meta model implicitly discerns its dynamics, enabling predictions of its behavior. The proposed approach harnesses the power of Transformers, renowned for their \emph{in-context learning} capabilities. For one-step prediction, a GPT-like decoder-only architecture is utilized, whereas the simulation problem employs an encoder-decoder structure. Initial experimental results affirmatively answer our foundational question, opening doors to fresh research avenues in system identification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从系统模型到类模型：一种上下文学习范式
能否通过观察同一类中的其他系统行为，而不仅依赖输入/输出模式，来理解动态系统的复杂性？这一核心问题驱动了本文的研究。为回应此问题，我们提出了一种新的系统辨识范式，解决两个主要任务：一步预测与多步仿真。与传统方法不同，我们不直接为特定系统建模，而是学习一个代表动态系统类别的元模型。该元模型在由模拟器生成的合成数据上训练，模拟器的设置从概率分布中随机抽取，数据流可能无限。当提供新系统的上下文（即输入/输出序列）时，元模型隐式识别其动态特性，从而预测其行为。该方法利用了Transformer强大的“上下文学习”能力：一步预测采用类似GPT的解码器架构，仿真问题采用编码器-解码器结构。初步实验结果肯定了我们的基础性问题，为系统辨识开辟了新的研究方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the question of whether system dynamics can be inferred by observing similar systems rather than direct measurements, this paper proposes an in-context learning paradigm for system identification. Instead of modeling a specific system, it trains a meta-model representing a class of dynamical systems using synthetic data generated from simulators with randomly sampled parameters. The meta-model, built with Transformer architectures (decoder-only for one-step prediction, encoder-decoder for multi-step simulation), implicitly captures system behavior from input-output context. Experimental results confirm that this approach successfully predicts system responses without explicit modeling, validating the feasibility of class-based inference in dynamical systems.
该研究旨在探索是否可以通过观察同类系统的动态行为而非直接输入输出数据来理解特定系统的复杂性。为此，提出了一种基于上下文学习的范式，训练一个代表系统类别的元模型，该模型在由随机采样系统配置生成的合成数据上进行训练。预测任务采用解码器结构，模拟任务采用编码器-解码器结构，实验结果表明该元模型能从上下文输入输出序列中有效推断并预测未见过系统的动态行为，验证了基于类别的系统识别方法的可行性。
Zoom In Disparities in Healthcare LLM Q&A
Authors: Ipek Baris Schlicht, Burcu Sayin, Zhixue Zhao, Frederik M. Labonté, Cesare Barbera, Marco Viviani, Paolo Rosso, Lucie Flek
Venue: NLDB 2026
First: 2025-10-20T12:19:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:27:00+00:00
Comments: It is accepted to NLDB 2026: The paper can be accessed at https://link.springer.com/chapter/10.1007/978-3-032-29532-3_12
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Equitable access to reliable health information is vital when integrating AI into healthcare. Yet, information quality varies across languages, raising concerns about the reliability and consistency of multilingual Large Language Models (LLMs). We systematically examine cross-lingual disparities in pre-training source and factuality alignment in LLM answers for multilingual healthcare Q&A across English, German, Turkish, Chinese (Mandarin), and Italian. We (i) constructed Multilingual Wiki Health Care (MultiWikiHealthCare), a multilingual dataset from Wikipedia; (ii) analyzed cross-lingual healthcare coverage; (iii) assessed LLM response alignment with these references; and (iv) conducted a case study on factual alignment through the use of contextual information and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). Our findings reveal substantial cross-lingual disparities in both Wikipedia coverage and LLM factual alignment. Across LLMs, responses align more with English Wikipedia, even when the prompts are non-English. Providing contextual excerpts from non-English Wikipedia at inference time effectively shifts factual alignment toward culturally relevant knowledge. These results highlight practical pathways for building more equitable, multilingual AI systems for healthcare.
中文标题/摘要
标题：医疗领域大语言模型问答中的跨语言差异研究
在将人工智能整合到医疗保健中时，公平获取可靠健康信息至关重要。然而，不同语言的信息质量存在差异，这引发了对多语言大语言模型（LLMs）可靠性和一致性问题的担忧。我们系统性地研究了英语、德语、土耳其语、中文（普通话）和意大利语五种语言中，LLM在医疗问答任务中预训练数据源和事实对齐的跨语言差异。我们（i）构建了多语言维基健康护理（MultiWikiHealthCare）数据集，从维基百科中提取多语言内容；（ii）分析了跨语言医疗覆盖情况；（iii）评估了LLM响应与这些参考内容的对齐程度；（iv）通过上下文信息和检索增强生成（RAG）技术开展事实对齐的案例研究。研究发现，维基百科覆盖范围和LLM事实对齐在跨语言层面存在显著差异。无论提示语言为何，LLM响应更倾向于与英语维基百科对齐。在推理阶段提供非英语维基百科的上下文片段，能有效将事实对齐转向文化相关知识。这些结果为构建更公平、多语言的医疗AI系统提供了实用路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for equitable access to reliable health information via AI, this study investigates cross-lingual disparities in multilingual LLMs' performance on healthcare Q&A across English, German, Turkish, Chinese, and Italian. The researchers built a multilingual dataset, MultiWikiHealthCare, from Wikipedia, analyzed coverage gaps, and evaluated LLM alignment with factual references. Experimental results show significant disparities: LLMs consistently align more with English Wikipedia content even when prompted in other languages, but incorporating contextual excerpts from non-English Wikipedia at inference time improves factual alignment with culturally relevant knowledge, offering a practical path toward more equitable multilingual healthcare AI.

Context-Aware Slum Mapping in Sub-Saharan Africa Using Sentinel-1 Texture and Local Climate Zones
Authors: Peterson Chepkilot, Babak Memar, Paolo Gamba
First: 2026-07-08T15:26:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:26:38+00:00
Comments: Submitted to IEEE Journal of Selected Topics in Applied Earth Observations and Remote Sensing (JSTARS) for possible publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate mapping of informal settlements remains a major challenge in Sub-Saharan African (SSA) cities because optical imagery often fails to distinguish Informal Settlements (defined here as LCZ 7) from spectrally similar formal Compact Low-Rise areas (LCZ 3). This study presents a context-aware, reproducible Optical-SAR framework that improves informal settlement delineation using Sentinel-2 spectral features and Sentinel-1 structural information within an adapted Local Climate Zone (LCZ) taxonomy. We implement a three-tier SAR integration strategy: calibrated backscatter, GLCM textures, and a physics-guided feature engineered to capture the high structural disorder and weak radar return characteristic of SSA informal settlements. Using reference data across Nairobi and Eldoret (Kenya), we evaluate performance via a stratified hold-out protocol and a season-aware ablation study. Results show that SAR textures provide the dominant performance gain for LCZ 7 detection. The Optical-SAR model achieves overall accuracy of 0.816 (dry) and 0.807 (wet), significantly outperforming the WUDAPT baseline (OA 0.704) and reducing the critical LCZ 3 - LCZ 7 confusion to 7%. Seasonal analysis indicates that while optical-only separability varies with phenology, SAR-derived textures stabilize informal settlement mapping across seasons. These findings demonstrate that the incorporation of SAR-derived features yields consistent improvements for urban morphology mapping in data-scarce environments across seasons and across the evaluated source cities, while cross-city transfer remains limited without local adaptation strategies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用哨兵-1纹理与局部气候区在撒哈拉以南非洲进行情境感知贫民窟制图
在撒哈拉以南非洲（SSA）城市中，准确绘制非正规定居点仍是一项重大挑战，因为光学影像常难以区分非正规定居点（此处定义为LCZ 7）与光谱相似的正规紧凑低层区域（LCZ 3）。本研究提出一种情境感知、可重复的光学- SAR框架，通过结合哨兵-2光谱特征与哨兵-1结构信息，在改进的局部气候区（LCZ）分类体系内提升非正规定居点的界定能力。我们实施了三级SAR集成策略：校准后散射强度、GLCM纹理及物理引导的特征工程，以捕捉撒哈拉以南非洲非正规定居点特有的高结构紊乱性和弱雷达回波特性。利用内罗毕和埃尔多雷特（肯尼亚）的参考数据，我们通过分层保留验证协议和季节感知消融研究评估性能。结果表明，SAR纹理对LCZ 7检测提供了主要性能提升。光学-SAR模型在干燥季和湿润季的整体准确率分别为0.816和0.807，显著优于WUDAPT基线（OA 0.704），并将关键的LCZ 3 - LCZ 7混淆率降至7%。季节性分析显示，尽管光学仅能分离度随物候变化，但SAR导出的纹理可稳定实现非正规定居点的跨季节制图。这些发现表明，在数据匮乏环境中，引入SAR导出特征可实现跨季节及评估城市间一致的改进，但跨城市迁移仍需本地化适应策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of distinguishing informal settlements (LCZ 7) from spectrally similar formal areas (LCZ 3) in Sub-Saharan Africa using optical imagery alone, this study develops a context-aware Optical-SAR framework combining Sentinel-2 spectral data with Sentinel-1 radar textures and physics-guided features to capture structural disorder. The method integrates three tiers of SAR data: calibrated backscatter, GLCM textures, and engineered features, and is evaluated across Nairobi and Eldoret using stratified hold-out and seasonal ablation. Results show SAR textures drive the largest performance gain, achieving overall accuracy of 0.816 (dry) and 0.807 (wet), significantly surpassing the WUDAPT baseline (0.704) and reducing LCZ 3–LCZ 7 confusion to 7%. 该研究旨在解决撒哈拉以南非洲城市中利用光学影像准确识别非正规聚落（LCZ 7）的难题，因光学影像难以区分其与光谱相似的正规紧凑低层建筑区（LCZ 3）。作者提出一种结合Sentinel-2光学特征与Sentinel-1 SAR数据的上下文感知光学- SAR框架，专门设计用于捕捉非正规聚落特有的结构紊乱和弱雷达反射特性。方法包含三层SAR数据整合：校准后散射、GLCM纹理及物理引导特征。在内罗毕和埃尔多雷特两地采用分层保留和季节消融评估，模型在旱季和雨季的整体精度分别达0.816和0.807，显著优于WUDAPT基线（0.704），LCZ 3与LCZ 7混淆率降至7%。结果表明，SAR纹理是性能提升的主要因素，能在不同季节提供稳定识别，而跨城市迁移仍需本地化策略。
DICE: Entropy-Regularized Equilibrium Selection for Stable Multi-Agent LLM Coordination
Authors: Yi Xie, Zhanke Zhou, Chentao Cao, Bo Liu, Bo Han
First: 2026-06-06T09:33:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:21:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems often fail to reliably outperform a single strong model equipped with best-of-N sampling. We argue that a core source of this instability is ill-posed equilibrium selection: current systems specify what information agents share, but not which coordination convention should be selected. We formalize a broad class of such systems as discounted incomplete-information Markov games and show that two common pathologies, oscillation between competing conventions and drift across them, can both induce unstable learning and linear Bayesian regret. To obtain a well-posed target, we introduce the Heterogeneous Quantal Response Equilibrium (HQRE), an entropy-regularized equilibrium concept with agent- and state-dependent temperatures. Under a monotonicity condition, HQRE is unique, admits linearly convergent mirror updates, and yields bounded Bayesian regret; the same condition yields rollout-measurable stability diagnostics. We instantiate this objective in two algorithms: DICE-PC, which coordinates frozen models through prompt-control actions, and DICE-FT, which performs parameter-efficient mirror fine-tuning. Across eleven benchmarks in four domains, DICE improves accuracy-cost trade-offs over strong within-class baselines; on reasoning and planning tasks, DICE-PC improves by 4.3 percentage points on average and DICE-FT by 8.5 points.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DICE：用于稳定多智能体大语言模型协调的熵正则化均衡选择
多智能体大语言模型（LLM）系统往往无法可靠地超越配备最佳N采样的单个强模型。我们认为，这种不稳定性的核心来源是未明确定义的均衡选择：当前系统指定了智能体间共享的信息，但未指定应选择哪种协调惯例。我们将此类系统形式化为折扣不完全信息马尔可夫博弈，并证明两种常见病态——在竞争性惯例间振荡和跨其漂移——均可导致不稳定学习和线性贝叶斯遗憾。为获得良定义的目标，我们引入异质量反应均衡（HQRE），这是一种具有智能体和状态依赖温度的熵正则化均衡概念。在单调性条件下，HQRE是唯一的，支持线性收敛的镜像更新，并产生有界的贝叶斯遗憾；同一条件还可提供可滚动测量的稳定性诊断。我们在两种算法中实现该目标：DICE-PC通过提示控制动作协调冻结模型，DICE-FT执行参数高效镜像微调。在四个领域十一项基准测试中，DICE在准确率-成本权衡上优于强同类基线；在推理与规划任务中，DICE-PC平均提升4.3个百分点，DICE-FT提升8.5个百分点。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the instability in multi-agent LLM systems that often underperform single strong models due to poor equilibrium selection, where coordination conventions are not formally specified. It formalizes these systems as discounted incomplete-information Markov games and identifies oscillation and drift as sources of unstable learning and high Bayesian regret. To resolve this, it introduces Heterogeneous Quantal Response Equilibrium (HQRE), an entropy-regularized equilibrium with state- and agent-dependent temperatures, which ensures uniqueness under monotonicity, linear convergence via mirror updates, and bounded regret, along with measurable stability diagnostics. Two algorithms, DICE-PC and DICE-FT, implement HQRE using prompt control and parameter-efficient fine-tuning respectively, achieving improved accuracy-cost trade-offs across eleven benchmarks, with DICE-PC boosting performance by 4.3 percentage points and DICE-FT by 8.5 points on reasoning and planning tasks.
该论文指出多智能体LLM系统不稳定的核心原因是缺乏明确的协调惯例选择机制。通过将系统形式化为折扣不完备信息马尔可夫博弈，提出异质量化反应均衡（HQRE）——一种具有智能体与状态依赖温度的熵正则化均衡概念，在单调性条件下保证唯一性、线性收敛与有界后悔。两种算法DICE-PC和DICE-FT分别通过提示控制与参数高效微调实现该目标，在十一个基准测试中，DICE显著改善准确率-成本权衡，其中DICE-PC平均提升4.3个百分点，DICE-FT提升8.5个百分点。
A Unified Detection Framework for AI-Related Content and Artifacts
Authors: Xifeng Zhang, Tao Hu, Yijie Peng, Wan Tian
First: 2026-07-08T15:21:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:21:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artificial intelligence (AI) is a double-edged sword: while it has achieved remarkable success across a wide range of domains, its deployment also calls for effective oversight and regulation, for which the detection of AI-related content and artifacts is perhaps the most direct and cost-effective approach. To this end, we propose a unified detection framework based on Mahalanobis distance scores (MDS), applicable to several important settings, including the detection of large language model (LLM) generated text, hallucination, watermark, and adversarial examples. A key component of the proposed method is to accurately characterize the positive class--such as human-generated text, factual statements, unwatermarked text, or non-adversarial samples--which requires an efficient and robust estimator of the covariance matrix of deep representations of positive samples before computing the MDS. Since the positive samples typically consist of multiple classes, and these classes may exhibit both homogeneity and heterogeneity, we develop joint estimation methods for both the casewise and cellwise minimum covariance determinant (MCD) estimators. We provide efficient optimization algorithms for both estimators and prove their convergence. We provide a reasonable definition of the breakdown point for the joint estimators and prove their corresponding high breakdown point properties. Empirical evaluations confirm the effectiveness of the proposed detection framework.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种用于AI相关内容和产物的统一检测框架
人工智能（AI）是一把双刃剑：尽管它在众多领域取得了显著成功，但其部署也要求有效的监管，而检测AI相关内容和产物可能是最直接且成本效益最高的方法。为此，我们提出了一种基于马氏距离得分（MDS）的统一检测框架，适用于多种重要场景，包括大语言模型（LLM）生成文本、幻觉、水印和对抗样本的检测。该方法的关键组成部分是准确刻画正类样本——如人类生成文本、事实陈述、无水印文本或非对抗样本——这需要在计算MDS前，对正样本深度表征的协方差矩阵进行高效且鲁棒的估计。由于正样本通常包含多个类别，且这些类别可能表现出同质性和异质性，我们开发了联合估计方法，用于案例级和单元级最小协方差确定（MCD）估计器。我们为这两种估计器提供了高效的优化算法并证明其收敛性。我们为联合估计器提供了合理的降阶点定义，并证明其具有高降阶点性质。实证评估证实了所提检测框架的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable oversight of AI-generated content, this paper introduces a unified detection framework using Mahalanobis distance scores to identify AI-related artifacts such as LLM-generated text, hallucinations, watermarks, and adversarial examples. The method relies on accurately modeling the covariance structure of human-generated or clean samples to compute distances that distinguish AI artifacts from genuine content. To handle heterogeneous positive classes, it jointly estimates casewise and cellwise minimum covariance determinant (MCD) estimators, with efficient optimization algorithms and proven convergence. Empirical results demonstrate the framework’s effectiveness across multiple detection tasks, confirming its robustness and high breakdown point properties.
为应对AI生成内容监管需求，本文提出一种基于马氏距离得分的统一检测框架，用于识别大语言模型生成文本、幻觉、水印和对抗样本等AI相关产物。该方法依赖于对正样本（如人类写作或干净数据）的协方差结构进行精确建模，从而计算马氏距离得分，为此开发了兼顾样本与单元最小协方差确定估计器的联合估计方法，并设计高效优化算法，证明其收敛性与高抗异常能力。实验表明，该框架在多种场景下能有效检测AI产物。
Diffusion Models in Simulation-Based Inference: A Tutorial Review
Authors: Jonas Arruda, Niels Bracher, Ullrich Köthe, Jan Hasenauer, Stefan T. Radev
First: 2025-12-22T15:10:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:20:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion models have recently emerged as powerful learners for simulation-based inference (SBI), enabling fast and accurate estimation of latent parameters from simulated and real data. Their score-based formulation offers a flexible way to learn conditional or joint distributions over parameters and observations, thereby providing a versatile solution to various modeling problems. In this tutorial review, we synthesize recent developments on diffusion models for SBI, covering design choices for training, inference, and evaluation. We highlight opportunities created by various concepts such as guidance, score composition, flow matching, consistency models, and joint modeling. Furthermore, we discuss how efficiency and statistical accuracy are affected by noise schedules, parameterizations, and samplers. Finally, we illustrate these concepts with case studies across parameter dimensionalities, simulation budgets, and model types, and outline open questions for future research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于扩散模型的仿真推断：教程综述
扩散模型近期已成为仿真推断（SBI）的强大学习工具，能够快速准确地从模拟和真实数据中估计潜在参数。其基于梯度的建模方式提供了一种灵活的方法，用于学习参数与观测值之间的条件或联合分布，从而为各种建模问题提供通用解决方案。本教程综述整合了扩散模型在SBI领域的最新进展，涵盖训练、推断与评估的设计选择。我们强调了引导、梯度组合、流匹配、一致性模型和联合建模等概念所创造的机遇。此外，我们讨论了噪声调度、参数化和采样器如何影响效率与统计精度。最后，我们通过不同参数维度、仿真预算和模型类型的实际案例说明这些概念，并概述未来研究的开放问题。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and accurate parameter estimation from simulations, this tutorial review examines diffusion models as a flexible framework for simulation-based inference. It synthesizes recent advances in training, inference, and evaluation strategies, emphasizing design choices such as guidance mechanisms, score composition, flow matching, and consistency models. The review demonstrates how noise schedules, parameterizations, and samplers influence both statistical accuracy and computational efficiency, and illustrates these concepts through case studies varying in parameter dimensionality, simulation budget, and model type, while identifying key open research questions.
为解决从模拟数据中高效准确估计参数的需求，本教程综述探讨了扩散模型作为模拟推理的灵活框架。文章梳理了训练、推理与评估中的关键方法选择，强调引导、分数组合、流匹配与一致性模型等概念对性能的提升作用。跨不同参数维度、模拟预算与模型类型的实验案例表明，扩散模型在保持计算效率的同时可实现高统计精度，但其表现受噪声调度、参数化方式与采样器影响显著。
Two-player Alternate Uses Test: A Controlled Testbed for Interactive Human-AI and Human-Human Co-Creation
Authors: Babak Hemmatian, Anita Keshmirian, Yijun Lin, Shravan Ramamoorthy, Maryam Jahadakbar, Eli Khuri-Reid, Jingtong Wang, Sarah Hadjarab, Sindre Veum, Pranav Gupta, Deepak Somaya, Lav R. Varshney
First: 2026-07-08T15:17:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:17:26+00:00
Comments: To appear in ACM Creativity and Cognition 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Controlled research on AI ideation typically compares independent agents, while field studies of human-AI collaboration sacrifice experimental control. We introduce a controlled, two-player extension of the Alternate Uses Test (AUT) that enables comparison of human-human and human-AI co-creation under matched interactive conditions, alongside calibrated non-interactive baselines. The platform supports decomposition of performance into three typically confounded factors: participant traits, partner perceptions, and content dynamics. An in-person pilot (N = 62) demonstrates its utility. Under matched time limits, originality with a GPT-4 partner is statistically equivalent to that with a human partner. Approach motivation (BAS Drive) moderates whether interactive partnership benefits originality, and self-reported cognitive outsourcing predicts lower originality specifically in human-human dyads. Prior exposure to highly creative ideas improves later performance, suggesting a "seeding" intervention. We release the platform, code, and dataset as a shared testbed for controlled studies of human-AI co-creation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：双人交替用途测试：用于交互式人机与人机协同创作的受控实验平台
通常对AI创意的受控研究比较独立代理，而人机协作的实地研究则牺牲了实验控制。我们引入了交替用途测试（AUT）的双人受控扩展版本，使在匹配的交互条件下比较人机与人机协同创作成为可能，并辅以校准的非交互基线。该平台支持将表现分解为三个通常混杂的因素：参与者特质、伙伴感知和内容动态。62人的现场试点验证了其效用。在匹配的时间限制下，与GPT-4伙伴的原创性与与人类伙伴相当。趋近动机（BAS Drive）调节交互伙伴关系是否提升原创性，而自我报告的认知外包预测在人机配对中原创性更低。先前接触高创意想法可提升后续表现，表明存在“播种”干预效应。我们发布该平台、代码和数据集，作为受控研究人机协同创作的共享实验平台。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to study human-AI co-creation under controlled conditions while preserving interactive dynamics, the authors developed a two-player extension of the Alternate Uses Test that allows direct comparison between human-human and human-AI collaboration. The method involves matched time constraints and decomposes performance into participant traits, partner perceptions, and content dynamics, with calibrated non-interactive baselines for comparison. In a pilot study with 62 participants, originality scores were statistically equivalent between human and GPT-4 partners under time pressure, but approach motivation moderated the benefit of interactive collaboration, and self-reported cognitive outsourcing predicted lower originality in human-human pairs. Exposure to highly creative ideas prior to the task improved subsequent performance, indicating a potential "seeding" effect.
为在保持互动性的同时对人类-人工智能协作进行可控研究，本文提出一种双人版替代用途测试，可直接比较人类-人类与人类-人工智能协作的效果。该方法通过匹配时间限制和专门平台，将表现分解为三类因素：参与者特质、伙伴感知与内容动态。62名受试者的实地实验结果显示，与GPT-4合作的原创性与人类搭档相当。趋近动机（BAS Drive）调节互动对原创性的影响，自我报告的认知外包在人类-人类搭档中与较低原创性相关。此外，先前接触高创意内容可提升后续表现，表明存在“播种”效应。
Creativity from Friction: Human-AI Interaction for Exploratory Structural Design
Authors: Ricardo Maia Avelino, Rita Sevastjanova, Tom Van Mele, Philippe Block, Mennatallah El-Assady
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-07-08T15:17:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:17:23+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026, Workshop on Human-AI Co-Creativity
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI agents that generate final answers based on user input often do not meet the needs of creative fields. Fields such as structural design and architecture need interactive systems that help users externalise and develop ideas, explore alternatives, and refine partial solutions. The final product of such designs needs to comply with many constraints concerning, e.g., spatial configuration, mechanical behaviour, material quantities, and costs. These constraints create friction in the design process, which can stimulate novel and creative solutions. In this paper, we discuss the misalignment between current generative AI goals to remove friction and provide final solutions and the needs of creators, such as structural designers, who develop ideas through iterative work. We present the design dimensions of systems allowing for constrained human-AI co-creation that rely on vision-language models making structural exploration conversational, multimodal, and responsive to evolving human intent in ways that follow and augment the discipline's creative process. Through a pilot design interface based on these principles and a study with experts in the field, this paper shows how structural designers perceive interactive AI systems and how such systems can support design space exploration by reducing repetitive modelling friction while preserving reflective design friction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：摩擦中的创造力：人机交互在探索性结构设计中的应用
基于用户输入生成最终答案的AI代理往往难以满足创意领域的需求。结构设计与建筑等领域需要交互式系统，帮助用户外化并发展创意、探索替代方案、完善部分解决方案。此类设计的最终成果需满足诸多约束，如空间布局、力学行为、材料用量和成本等。这些约束在设计过程中形成“摩擦”，反而能激发新颖创意。本文探讨当前生成式AI旨在消除摩擦并提供最终解决方案的目标，与创作者（如结构设计师）通过迭代工作发展创意的需求之间的错位。我们提出支持受限人机共创的系统设计维度，利用视觉-语言模型使结构探索过程对话化、多模态化，并响应人类意图的动态演化，从而遵循并增强该领域的创意流程。通过基于这些原则的原型设计界面及对领域专家的研究，本文展示了结构设计师如何感知交互式AI系统，以及此类系统如何通过减少重复建模摩擦、保留反思性设计摩擦，支持设计空间探索。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the mismatch between generative AI’s goal to eliminate friction and the creative needs of structural designers who benefit from iterative, constraint-driven exploration, this paper proposes human-AI co-creation systems that embrace design friction as a catalyst for innovation. Using vision-language models, the system enables conversational, multimodal interaction that adapts to evolving user intent while respecting structural constraints like spatial layout, material use, and cost. Experimental results with structural design experts show that such interactive AI reduces repetitive modeling tasks while preserving the reflective, exploratory friction essential to creative design processes.
鉴于当前生成式AI倾向于消除摩擦而与结构设计师等创意工作者需要通过迭代、受约束的探索过程产生创意的需求存在错位，本文提出一种人机协同创作系统，将摩擦视为创新催化剂。系统基于视觉-语言模型，支持对话式、多模态交互，能动态响应用户意图并尊重空间布局、材料用量和成本等结构约束。实验表明，该交互式AI可减少重复建模工作，同时保留创造性设计所需的反思性摩擦，从而提升设计空间探索与创意生成效率。
Gradient-free Riemannian Langevin Sampler
Authors: Ricardo Baptista, Olivier Zahm
First: 2026-07-08T15:16:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:16:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We address the problem of efficiently sampling multimodal probability distributions, where standard Markov Chain Monte Carlo methods often suffer from poor mixing and mode trapping. To mitigate these issues, we propose Gradient-free Riemannian Langevin Sampler (GRiLS), a novel proposal that improves exploration without requiring gradient evaluations of the target density. Our approach introduces a Riemannian metric which reshapes the local geometry in order to facilitate transitions across modes. The resulting gradient-free MCMC algorithm is particularly suitable for complex, computationally expensive targets where derivatives are unavailable or impractical. The GRiLS proposal requires knowing the mean and covariance of the target density, which we estimate using an ensemble of interacting particles. Empirical results on multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that GRiLS achieves improved mixing compared to existing gradient-based and gradient-free MCMC approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无梯度黎曼朗之万采样器
为了解决在多模态概率分布中高效采样的问题——标准马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛方法常因混合效率低和模式陷阱而失效——我们提出了无梯度黎曼朗之万采样器（GRiLS），一种无需目标密度梯度评估即可提升探索能力的新方案。该方法引入黎曼度量，重塑局部几何结构以促进跨模式转移。所得到的无梯度MCMC算法特别适用于复杂且计算成本高的目标分布，其中导数不可用或难以计算。GRiLS采样器需要知道目标密度的均值和协方差，我们通过一组相互作用的粒子进行估计。在多模态基准测试中的实证结果表明，GRiLS相比现有基于梯度和无梯度的MCMC方法具有更优的混合性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of standard MCMC methods in sampling multimodal distributions due to poor mixing and mode trapping, this work introduces the Gradient-free Riemannian Langevin Sampler (GRiLS), a novel MCMC algorithm that enhances exploration without requiring gradient evaluations. GRiLS employs a Riemannian metric to locally reshape the geometry of the target space, enabling transitions between modes, and estimates the target’s mean and covariance via an ensemble of interacting particles. Experimental results on multimodal benchmarks show that GRiLS outperforms both gradient-based and gradient-free MCMC methods in terms of mixing efficiency.
为了解决标准MCMC方法在采样多模态分布时因混合效率低和陷入局部模式而导致的问题，本文提出了无需梯度计算的黎曼朗之万采样器（GRiLS）。该方法通过引入黎曼度量局部重塑目标空间几何结构，促进跨模态转移，并利用粒子群估计目标分布的均值与协方差。实验结果表明，GRiLS在多模态基准测试中比现有基于梯度和非梯度的MCMC方法具有更优的混合效率。
Learning to Unify Deformable Shape and Texture Representations for Cardiac Video Classification
Authors: Tonmoy Hossain, Miaomiao Zhang
First: 2026-07-08T15:15:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:15:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deformable shape representations have proven to be robust complements to texture features in cardiac image classification, offering geometric priors that are invariant to imaging artifacts and intensity variations. However, existing deep networks perform simple concatenation to combine these distinct feature representations, which neither fully exploits their complementary nature nor learns cross-modal feature dependencies. Furthermore, this results in uniform attention across all timepoints; hence ignoring the varying diagnostic importance across the cardiac phases. In this paper, we propose a novel cardiac video classification model that, for the first time, learns temporal features in an integrated space of deformable shape and image texture representations. In particular, we design a bi-directional cross-attention in the latent space to fuse latent deformable shape and image features, allowing each modality to adaptively weight the other based on spatio-temporal correspondence. In contrast to current methods that apply uniform weighting across all the cardiac phases, our approach learns to dynamically adjust the contributions of shape and texture representations, derived from images, over time. We demonstrate state-of-the-art classification performance on a cine cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) video dataset, achieving improved interpretability from attention mechanisms that identify diagnostically critical cardiac phases and modality contributions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习统一可变形形状与纹理表示以进行心脏视频分类
可变形形状表示已被证明是心脏图像分类中纹理特征的稳健补充，提供对成像伪影和强度变化不变的几何先验。然而，现有深度网络仅通过简单拼接结合这些不同的特征表示，既未能充分挖掘其互补性，也未学习跨模态特征依赖关系。此外，这导致在所有时间点上注意力分布均匀，忽略了心脏各阶段诊断重要性的差异。本文提出了一种新颖的心脏视频分类模型，首次在可变形形状与图像纹理表示的联合空间中学习时序特征。具体而言，我们在潜在空间中设计双向交叉注意力机制，融合潜在的可变形形状与图像特征，使各模态可根据时空对应关系自适应加权对方。与当前方法在所有心脏阶段采用统一加权不同，我们的方法学习随时间动态调整来自图像的形状与纹理表示的贡献。我们在心脏电影磁共振（CMR）视频数据集上实现了最先进的分类性能，并通过注意力机制提升了可解释性，能够识别诊断关键的心脏阶段和模态贡献。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better integrate deformable shape and texture features for cardiac video classification, this paper introduces a novel model that learns to fuse these modalities in a shared latent space using bi-directional cross-attention. Unlike existing methods that simply concatenate features or apply uniform attention across time, the proposed approach dynamically adjusts the weighting of shape and texture representations based on spatio-temporal correspondence across cardiac phases. Experimental results on a cine cardiac MRI dataset show state-of-the-art classification performance and enhanced interpretability, as attention mechanisms highlight diagnostically critical timepoints and modality contributions.
为提升心脏视频分类中形变形状与纹理特征的融合效果，本文提出一种新颖模型，通过双向跨模态注意力机制在共享潜在空间中动态融合两种特征，依据时空对应关系自适应调整权重。与现有方法简单拼接特征或对所有心动周期统一加权不同，该模型能随时间动态调节形状与纹理的贡献，捕捉不同心动周期的诊断关键性。在心脏磁共振视频数据集上的实验表明，该方法达到当前最优分类性能，并通过注意力机制提升可解释性，明确识别出关键心动周期及模态贡献。
Fast Rates for Semi-Supervised Learning via Data-Augmentation Graph Regularization
Authors: Adam M. Oberman
First: 2026-07-08T15:11:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:11:21+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Self-supervised learning matches supervised accuracy from a fraction of the labels, but the labeled-sample efficiency behind this has lacked a theoretical explanation. We provide one. Data augmentation induces a similarity graph on the unlabeled data, so downstream learning on that graph is graph-Laplacian-regularized learning. We prove a fast transductive rate, $O(1/n_L)$ in the number of labels, in place of the supervised $O(1/\sqrt{n_L})$, by carrying the leave-one-out stability apparatus of Johnson and Zhang (JMLR 2007) over to the augmentation graph, and without the unrealistic assumptions of limit-based analyses (exact kernel, generalizing features). The bound makes augmentation quality explicit: the expected error is at most $C/n_L + R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$, where the data-augmentation alignment error $R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$ is the graph-cut mass of augmentations that cross a label boundary, so good augmentations let few labels suffice. The analysis uses a streamlined loss that drops the projector, negative-sample, and orthogonality overhead of standard objectives yet still recovers the top-$K$ ideal features in the infinite-data limit, the augmentation-kernel eigenspace studied by Zhai et al. The result explains the observed accuracy-versus-label-count curve rather than only bounding a generalization gap.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过数据增强图正则化实现半监督学习的快速收敛率
自监督学习仅用少量标签即可达到监督学习的精度，但其背后标签样本效率的理论解释一直缺失。我们提供了这一解释。数据增强在未标记数据上诱导出相似性图，因此其下游学习等价于图拉普拉斯正则化学习。我们通过将Johnson和Zhang（JMLR 2007）的留一法稳定性框架推广至增强图，且无需极限分析中不现实的假设（精确核、泛化特征），证明了快速转导收敛率 $O(1/n_L)$（标签数量），优于监督学习的 $O(1/\sqrt{n_L})$。该界明确体现了增强质量：期望误差至多为 $C/n_L + R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$，其中数据增强对齐误差 $R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$ 是跨越标签边界的增强图割质量，因此高质量增强可使少量标签即足够。分析采用简化损失函数，消除了标准目标中的投影器、负样本和正交性开销，但仍能在无限数据极限下恢复Zhai等人研究的增强核特征空间中的前 $K$ 个理想特征。该结果解释了观察到的精度-标签数曲线，而不仅限于泛化差距的上界。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the empirical success of self-supervised learning with few labels but lacking theoretical justification, this paper analyzes how data augmentation induces a similarity graph on unlabeled data, enabling graph-Laplacian-regularized learning. It proves a fast transductive generalization rate of O(1/n_L) — faster than the supervised O(1/√n_L) — by adapting the leave-one-out stability framework to the augmentation graph, avoiding unrealistic assumptions like exact kernels or ideal features. The bound explicitly links error to augmentation quality via R_DA(y), the graph-cut mass across label boundaries, showing that high-quality augmentations allow accurate prediction with fewer labels. The analysis uses a streamlined loss function that eliminates projector and orthogonality constraints while still recovering ideal features in the infinite-data limit, matching the augmentation-kernel eigenspace.
为了解释自监督学习中数据增强为何能在极少标注样本下达到监督学习的精度，本文提出理论分析：数据增强在未标注数据上诱导出相似性图，从而支持图拉普拉斯正则化学习。作者通过将Johnson和Zhang（2007）的留一稳定性框架迁移到增强图上，推导出O(1/n_L)的快速转导泛化率，优于监督学习的O(1/√n_L)，且无需理想化假设（如精确核或泛化特征）。误差上界明确体现增强质量：期望误差不超过C/n_L + R_DA(y)，其中R_DA(y)衡量跨标签边界的增强图割质量；增强质量越高，所需标注样本越少。分析采用简化损失函数，省去投影器、负样本和正交性开销，但仍能在无限数据极限下恢复Zhai等人研究的增强核特征空间的前K个特征，从而解释观察到的准确率-标注样本数曲线，而非仅提供泛化差距上界。
Synesthesia via Direct Latent Augmentation:Bypassing the Decode-Encode Loop for Cross-Modal Distillation
Authors: Cristian Sbrolli, Nicolas Michel, Matteo Matteucci, Toshihiko Yamasaki
First: 2026-06-06T21:02:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:10:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While multimodal integration significantly improves computer vision models, deploying them incurs prohibitive inference costs and requires scarce, perfectly paired datasets. Recent methods address this data bottleneck by synthesizing missing modalities via generative AI, yet they introduce a severe inefficiency: the Decode-Encode Loop. Specifically, information-rich generative latents are decoded into noisy raw signals, forcing the downstream classifier to waste capacity re-encoding them. To bypass this bottleneck, we propose Direct Latent Augmentation (DLA), utilizing undecoded generative latents directly as privileged information. Furthermore, to transfer this dense knowledge to a purely visual student, we introduce Multilayer Explicit Simulated Synesthesia (MESSy). Instead of enforcing rigid representation matching, which forces the student to distort its native visual features to accommodate complex multimodal topologies, MESSy uses a predictive objective to safely internalize these physical priors. Empirical results demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms raw data augmentation and traditional distillation. Ultimately, our approach yields highly accurate unimodal students with "synesthetic" latent structures that are inherently aligned with modalities they have never directly observed.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过直接潜在增强实现通感：绕过解码-编码循环的跨模态蒸馏
尽管多模态集成显著提升了计算机视觉模型性能，但部署时面临高昂的推理成本和稀缺的完美配对数据集。近期方法通过生成式AI合成缺失模态以缓解数据瓶颈，但引入了严重效率问题：解码-编码循环。具体而言，信息丰富的生成潜变量被解码为噪声原始信号，迫使下游分类器浪费容量重新编码。为绕过此瓶颈，我们提出直接潜在增强（DLA），直接利用未解码的生成潜变量作为特权信息。此外，为将此密集知识传递给纯视觉学生模型，我们引入多层显式模拟通感（MESSy）。MESSy不强制学生模型进行刚性表征匹配（避免学生模型扭曲其原生视觉特征以适应复杂多模态拓扑），而是通过预测目标安全地内化这些物理先验。实验证明，我们的框架显著优于原始数据增强和传统蒸馏方法。最终，我们的方法生成高精度的单模态学生模型，其“通感”潜结构天然与学生从未直接观察的模态对齐。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high inference costs and data scarcity in multimodal vision systems, this work proposes Direct Latent Augmentation (DLA) to bypass the inefficient Decode-Encode Loop by using undecoded generative latents as privileged input. To transfer this cross-modal knowledge to a visual-only student, it introduces Multilayer Explicit Simulated Synesthesia (MESSy), which employs a predictive objective to align the student’s latent space with multimodal priors without forcing rigid feature matching. Experiments show that DLA+MESSy significantly outperforms raw data augmentation and traditional distillation, producing highly accurate unimodal models with latent structures that implicitly encode cross-modal knowledge without direct exposure to other modalities.
为解决多模态视觉系统部署成本高、数据稀缺的问题，本文提出直接潜空间增强（DLA）方法，绕过生成式AI合成模态时存在的低效解码-编码循环，直接利用未解码的生成潜变量作为特权信息。结合多层显式模拟通感（MESSy）机制，通过预测性目标安全传递跨模态先验，避免强制视觉特征适应复杂模态拓扑。实验表明，该方法显著优于原始数据增强与传统蒸馏，生成的单模态学生模型具备与未直接观测模态对齐的“通感”潜空间结构。
Stability of Flow Models for Graph Signals
Authors: Martin Schmidt, Gonzalo Mateos
First: 2026-07-08T15:04:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T15:04:39+00:00
Comments: Submitted to the IEEE Transactions on Signal Processing
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generating signals on graphs requires permutation-equivariant models that exhibit stability with respect to relative structural perturbations. While favorable stability properties of Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have been well documented, it is unclear how structural errors propagate through the dynamics of continuous generative flow models that are gaining traction for graph signal generation. In this paper, we analyze continuous normalized flow models parameterized by GNNs and show that permutation equivariance is preserved for both the resulting continuous-time ordinary differential equations and their discrete numerical approximations used as graph signal samplers. Our primary contribution is to derive explicit stability bounds on the generated probability distributions, which quantify how relative graph perturbations affect the final sampled signals. Motivated by these theoretical bounds, we introduce a stability-promoting regularized flow matching strategy that actively penalizes the spatial Lipschitz constant of the vector field during model training. Experiments using synthetic smooth signals on stochastic block model graphs and real-world fMRI signals on brain connectomes demonstrate that this bound-oriented approach yields generative models that are more robust to structural noise, without sacrificing output quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：图信号生成模型的稳定性
在图上生成信号需要具有相对结构扰动稳定性的置换等变模型。虽然图神经网络（GNNs）的有利稳定性特性已得到充分研究，但连续生成流模型（正日益用于图信号生成）的结构误差如何通过其动态传播仍不明确。本文分析了由GNN参数化的连续归一化流模型，证明其对应的连续时间常微分方程及其作为图信号采样器的离散数值近似均保持置换等变性。我们的主要贡献是推导出生成概率分布的显式稳定性界，量化相对图扰动对最终采样信号的影响。受这些理论界启发，我们提出了一种稳定性促进的正则化流匹配策略，在模型训练中主动惩罚向量场的空间Lipschitz常数。在随机块模型图上的合成平滑信号和真实脑连接组上的fMRI信号实验表明，该以界为导向的方法生成的模型对结构噪声更鲁棒，且不牺牲输出质量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for stable generative models for graph signals that remain robust to structural perturbations, this paper investigates continuous normalized flow models parameterized by Graph Neural Networks. It proves that permutation equivariance is preserved in both the continuous-time ODE formulation and its discrete numerical approximations used for sampling. The authors derive explicit stability bounds quantifying how graph structure changes affect generated signals, and propose a regularized flow matching strategy that penalizes the spatial Lipschitz constant of the vector field to enhance stability during training. Experiments on synthetic and real fMRI data show that this approach improves robustness to structural noise without compromising signal quality.
为提升图信号生成模型对结构扰动的稳定性，本文分析了由GNN参数化的连续归一化流模型，证明其在连续时间ODE及离散数值近似中均保持置换等变性，并推导出量化结构扰动对输出分布影响的显式稳定性界。基于此理论，提出一种通过惩罚向量场空间Lipschitz常数来增强稳定性的流匹配正则化策略。实验表明，该方法在合成与真实fMRI数据上显著提升模型对结构噪声的鲁棒性，且不牺牲生成信号质量。
